

# RAVE I CHING 

# Foreword

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Foreword page for “The Complete Rave I’Ching,” dated August 3, 2001 in Diessen am Ammersee, Germany, with a paragraph by Ra Uru Hu and a closing line “Love Yourself” above his name.

Long description:  
White page with the centered heading “Forward.” Below, an italic line gives the date and location. A single justified paragraph describes the purpose and development of this edition, acknowledges Erik Memmert’s programming, outlines the multilingual editions, and defines the book as a comprehensive reference for Human Design students and professionals. The paragraph mentions Human Design and the Global Incarnation Index, gate descriptions, Incarnation Crosses, and line commentaries. The page ends with the sign-off “Love Yourself” and the author’s name “Ra Uru Hu.” Two thin yellow rules appear near the bottom margin.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
Forward

August 3, 2001 Diessen am Ammersee, Germany

In 2000 after 13 years of introducing the Human Design Sys-  
tem, it was time to focus on international standards for edu-  
cation. The first step was to respect the needs of the student.  
When I returned to Europe in 2000, I began immediately to  
work on this edition of the Rave I’Ching with Erik Memmert.  
Erik’s programming skills have made the uniform lay-  
out/translation process translator friendly. This English edition  
will be followed by German, Italian and Spanish editions. The  
Complete Rave I’Ching is just that; a complete reference  
work for Human Design students and professionals alike. It  
contains all the data one would want, combining both Human  
Design and Global Incarnation Index information. Each Gate  
is thematically described and the Incarnation Crosses of  
each Gate are listed and graphically presented. Each line is  
provided in its original form along with additional commen-  
tary. This Complete Rave I’Ching is now the standard text of  
the IHDS, the International Human Design Schools. I want to  
acknowledge the contribution of many to the fruition of this  
project.

Love Yourself

Ra Uru Hu

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

None.

Notes:  
Title printed as “Forward” on the page. No printed page number visible.

# Gate 1 — The Gate of Self-Expression

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 1 page. Header block shows Channel 8-1 “Inspiration” with the note “The creative Role Model,” Harmonic Gate 8 “Holding Together,” Center G, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic position in Scorpio. A body paragraph describes the drive to express individuality. A purple Scorpio glyph appears at upper right. Three small Rave mandalas at the bottom are captioned with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses related to this gate.

Long description:  
White page with a title “The Gate of Self-Expression.” A compact spec table sits beneath the title giving channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an astrologic position range. A thin rule separates this header from a single explanatory paragraph. At the bottom are three labeled mandala diagrams. A decorative Scorpio sign is on the right side of the header area.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Self-Expression

Channel 8-1 Inspiration  
The creative Role Model  
Harmonic Gate 8 Holding Together  
Center G  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 13° 15′ 00″ ♏ – 18° 52′ 30″ ♏

This is the yangest (six yang lines) of the Hexagrams. It is a deep need to express oneself. This gate demands  
attention. This is potentially an extremely creative gate but this is not a creativity rooted in awareness but in its  
unique direction. Individual expression, like individual insight can only be fulfilled through interaction with others.  
The role of this creativity is, ‘doing your own thing’ and when and if the creativity attracts attention, it may become  
an example for a new direction for others. Regardless of this potential, the drive to express one's individuality to  
others isn't always appreciated. This gate and its relationship to its harmonic gate 8: Holding Together, can best  
be expressed through the relationship between the ‘Artist’ (1) and the ‘Agent’ (8). When in harmony, this channel  
contributes creative direction to the collective. Tension can arise in this relationship when the Artist wants to be  
their own agents or when the Agent knows better than the Artist.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE SPHINX (4)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF SELF-EXPRESSION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF DEFIANCE (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Scorpio sign ♏ U+264F

Notes:  
Three small mandalas align along the bottom. A yellow rule runs near the lower margin.

## Gate 1 — THE CREATIVE, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 1 “THE CREATIVE” page showing the six lines, their degree ranges in Scorpio, and paired notes marked by up/down triangles for exaltation and detriment. A blue subheading reads “The tension of inner light.”

Long description:  
The page heading reads “THE CREATIVE.” Beneath it, a gold subtitle line states: “CREATION AS A PRIMAL FORCE. THE ENERGY POTENTIAL TO MANIFEST INSPIRATION WITHOUT LIMITATION.” Along the left margin a numbered list 6→1 shows line names with degree ranges. The main column presents paired bullets for each line, each pair beginning with a glyph and a triangle: ▲ indicates exaltation, ▽ indicates detriment. A thin yellow bar sits near the bottom margin.

Transcribed text (exact, preserving line order and punctuation visible):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Scorpio):  
6 Objectivity — 17° 56′ 15″ to 18° 52′ 30″  
5 The energy to attract society — 17° 00′ 00″ to 17° 56′ 15″  
4 Aloneness as the medium of creativity — 16° 03′ 45″ to 17° 00′ 00″  
3 The energy to sustain creative work — 15° 07′ 30″ to 16° 03′ 45″  
2 Love is light — 14° 11′ 15″ to 15° 07′ 30″  
1 Creation is independent of will — 13° 15′ 00″ to 14° 11′ 15″

Main text under “THE CREATIVE”:  
CREATION AS A PRIMAL FORCE. THE ENERGY POTENTIAL TO MANIFEST INSPIRATION WITHOUT LIMITATION.

• ⊕ ▲ Clear assessment of creative value. Clarity in creative expression.  
• \[glyph unreadable\] ▽ The risk that subjective appraisal will result in disappointment and creative frustration. Subjectivity in self-expression that may lead to creative frustration.

• ♂ ▲ Mars exalted for its powerful ego endurance. The power and drive to stay with the creative process.  
• ♅ ▽ Uranus in detriment, where eccentricity can handicap endurance. Eccentricity that though attractive will limit the drive.

The tension of inner light.  
• ⊕ ▲ The Earth exalted as the symbol of personal perspective manifested outside of influence, where the potential magic of inspiration is diluted. Creativity that must develop outside of influence.  
• \[glyph unreadable\] ▽ Where the potential magic of inspiration is diluted. The need to influence that abandons aloneness and limits creativity.

• ♂ ▲ Mars exalted as the symbol of the profound need for self-expression. The deep need for self-expression.  
• ⊕ ▽ Material forces can disrupt creativity and lead to overambition. Materialism disrupts creativity.

• ♀ ▲ Venus exalted as a symbol of beauty. The required harmony between established values and ideals that enriches inspiration. Self-expression conditioned by ideals and values.  
• ♂ ▽ Desires and passions have their place but not at the expense of Creation. Self-expression limited by desires and passions.

• ☾ ▲ The Moon exalted as a symbol of adaption. Time is everything. Self-expression which has its special timing.  
• \[glyph unreadable\] ▽ Instability leads to distortion. Here, patience is a virtue and revolution a vice. Creative instability unless there is patience.

Symbols (Unicode seen or implied on the page):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ♅ U+2645 Uranus; ☾/☽ U+263E/U+263D Moon (variant unclear at this size); ♏ U+264F Scorpio (contextual to the degree ranges).  
Unknown/uncertain glyphs appear in three detriment bullets; mark as unreadable at this resolution.

Notes:  
Line names and ranges list Scorpio degrees from 13° 15′ 00″ to 18° 52′ 30″. Blue subheading “The tension of inner light.” appears mid-page.

# Gate 2 — The Gate of Higher Knowing

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 2 page. Header lists Channel 2-14 “The Beat,” Harmonic Gate 14 “Possession in Great Measure,” Center G, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic position span in Taurus. One paragraph explains this gate as the seat of the “Driver.” Three small mandalas at the bottom are labeled with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Higher Knowing.” A compact specification block at upper center details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an astrologic position range marked with Taurus glyphs. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Taurus symbol appears at upper right.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Higher Knowing

Channel 2-14 The Beat  
The design of being the keeper of keys  
Harmonic Gate 14 Possession in Great Measure  
Center G  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 13° 15′ 00″ ♉ – 18° 52′ 30″ ♉

This is the most receptive (six yin lines) of all the hexagrams. This gate is the seat of the ‘Driver’. The ancients  
would refer it as the ‘Higher Self’. It is important to remember and the Gods love a Joke, that the Self is unaware.  
It is a mechanical function of the vehicle, holding us together in the illusion of our separateness and giving us our  
direction. This is the potential to continue evolving which finds expression and manifestation through the three  
other Sphinx gates.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE SPHINX (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF THE DRIVER  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF DEFIANCE

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Taurus ♉ U+2649

Notes:  
Three small mandalas along the bottom; thin yellow rule near the lower margin.

## Gate 2 — THE RECEPTIVE, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 2 “THE RECEPTIVE” page with six line themes, degree ranges, and paired notes marked by ▲ for exaltation and ▽ for detriment.

Long description:  
Heading reads “THE RECEPTIVE.” A gold subtitle states: “RECEPTIVITY AS THE PRIMAL BASE THROUGH WHICH ANY RESPONSE IS DETERMINED. THE ROOT OF ACTION.” Left margin lists lines 6 to 1 with names and Taurus degree ranges. The main column presents blue subheadings for each line, followed by two bullets per line: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽) with planetary glyphs. A thin yellow rule sits near the bottom margin and a small blue “2” appears near the lower corners.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; line subheadings in blue, italics reproduced where visible):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Taurus):  
6 Fixation — 17° 56′ 15″ to 18° 52′ 30″  
5 Intelligent application — 17° 00′ 00″ to 17° 56′ 15″  
4 Secretiveness — 16° 03′ 45″ to 17° 00′ 00″  
3 Patience — 15° 07′ 30″ to 16° 03′ 45″  
2 Genius — 14° 11′ 15″ to 15° 07′ 30″  
1 Intuition — 13° 15′ 00″ to 14° 11′ 15″

Main text:  
THE RECEPTIVE

RECEPTIVITY AS THE PRIMAL BASE THROUGH WHICH ANY RESPONSE IS DETERMINED. THE ROOT OF ACTION.

Unable or unwilling to see the whole picture.  
• ▲ With Mercury exalted, less negative, though the intellect becomes absorbed in constant rationalization. Higher knowing that is extremely narrow in its receptivity.  
• ▽ The need for security may distort awareness to its ultimate perversion, destructiveness. The Higher Self absorbed with the mundane and the need for security.

The strategist with Mercury exalted.  
• ▲ The strategist with Mercury exalted. Reasoned management of resources. Higher knowing as a gift for strategy.  
• ▽ The inability to share responsibility or recognize the abilities of others. Higher knowing as an exclusively individual and selfish process.

More than modesty, the ability to preserve harmony through discretion.  
• ▲ The higher goal transcends personal acclaim. The team player, acknowledged as the leader but never the captain. Where higher knowing does not have to be expressed to be recognized.  
• ▽ Loose lips sink ships. The unquenchable fire of the ego engenders enmity. The inability to keep silent when the opportunity arises for expression.

The teacher that never stops being a student.  
• ▲ Dedication to a lifetime of receptivity with the maturity to accept that the process never ends. When connected permanently to Gate 14 through the Beat Channel, rewards for services rendered. The recognition that receptivity is a lifelong process.  
• ▽ For the revolutionary patience is a vice. Higher knowing that cannot wait and demands expression.

Unconscious and unlearnable alignment of stimulus and response. The natural.  
• ▲ The inner strength to focus and realize. A natural gift for unlearnable knowing.  
• ▽ Genius as madness. Knowledge exclusively as power for the enhancement of the ego. The delusion that knowledge is power.

Sensitivity to disharmony and atrophy.  
• ▲ The importance of aesthetics whether inborn or acquired. Higher knowing through aesthetics.  
• ▽ The assertion of ego in spite of wisdom. The urge for action that will ignore the wisdom of the Higher Self.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♀ U+2640 Venus.  
Note: additional planet glyphs are present but are not fully legible at this resolution.

Notes:  
Degree spans match Taurus 13° 15′ 00″ to 18° 52′ 30″ for Gate 2\. Blue page number “2” appears at lower corners.

# Gate 3 — The Gate of Ordering

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 3 page. Header lists Channel 3-60 “Mutation” with the note “Energy which fluctuates and initiates,” Harmonic Gate 60 “Limitation,” Center Sacral, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic span from late Aries into early Taurus. One paragraph explains mutation as generational and cultural. Three small mandalas at the bottom are labeled with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Ordering.” A compact specification block at upper center details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an astrologic position range marked with Aries then Taurus glyphs. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Aries symbol appears at upper right and a large gray “3” sits at the upper left.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Ordering

Channel 3-60 Mutation  
Energy which fluctuates and initiates  
Harmonic Gate 60 Limitation  
Center Sacral  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 26° 22′ 30″ ♈ – 02° 00′ 00″ ♉

It is in the Sacral that the potential for mutation is empowered. This is a gate of confused energy, confused in the  
sense that it is unsubstantiated, like the individual insight, it is unique and has to find its form. Mutation can oper-  
ate in two basic ways. The simplest is the direct mutation through reproduction. The Sacral is our center of fertility.  
It is associated in biology with the Ovaries in a woman and the Testes in a man. The potential of mutation is  
deeply connected to our reproductive process and is an essential ingredient for our continued survival. The 3rd  
gate not only generates potential mutation but it determines what genetic material we inherit from our parents'  
genetic pool. This is Generational mutation. The second form of mutation is through the fulfillment of the Knowing  
circuit. Individual expression that through example changes and mutates those who have contact with them, this  
is Cultural mutation.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF LAWS  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF MUTATION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF WISHES

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Aries ♈ U+2648

Taurus ♉ U+2649

Notes:  
Three small mandalas align along the bottom; thin yellow rule near the lower margin. Side indicator shows neighboring numbers around this gate on the rim.

## Gate 3 — DIFFICULTY AT THE BEGINNING, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 3 “DIFFICULTY AT THE BEGINNING” lines page, showing six line themes with degree ranges, and paired notes for each line marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) with planetary glyphs.

Long description:  
Page heading reads “DIFFICULTY AT THE BEGINNING.” A gold subtitle states: “THE FUNDAMENTAL CHALLENGE OF INITIATION IS TO TRANSCEND CONFUSION AND ESTABLISH ORDER.” Along the left margin the six lines are listed from 6 down to 1 with degree ranges. The main column presents a blue subheading for each line followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each preceded by a small glyph. A thin yellow rule sits near the bottom; a small blue page number “3” appears at the lower corners; a large gray “3” sits at the top right.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges):  
6 Surrender — 01° 03′ 45″ – 02° 00′ 00″  
5 Victimization — 00° 07′ 30″ – 01° 03′ 45″  
4 Charisma — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
3 Survival — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″  
2 Immaturity — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
1 Synthesis — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″

Main text:  
DIFFICULTY AT THE BEGINNING

THE FUNDAMENTAL CHALLENGE OF INITIATION IS TO TRANSCEND CON-  
FUSION AND ESTABLISH ORDER.

The ultimate maturity to recognize when struggle is futile.  
• \[sun-like glyph\] ▲ As its light sustains, so life goes on. The innate acceptance that ordering is a process, not a problem.  
• \[omega-like glyph\] ▽ As darkness overwhelms, life can seem worthless leading to depression and the sense of hopelessness. The overwhelming power of confused energy can lead to depression.

When actions designed to overcome confusion alienate others.  
• ♂ ▲ The courage to stand by one's convictions. The unique energy of individuality to withstand confusion.  
• ⊕ ▽ The Earth in detriment, where victimization leads to appeasement and suffering. Confused energy which is dominated by the ordering power of others.

Innate quality which attracts valued guidance.  
• Ψ ▲ Physic attunement that magnetizes nurturing. A psychic energy which attracts nourishment and ensures ordering.  
• ♂ ▽ Mars in detriment, where the demands of ego lead to rejection. Confused energy that needs nourishment but is generally rejected.

The ability to recognize and distinguish between fertile and sterile in their various manifestations.  
• ♀ ▲ In reproduction, the ability to choose the best mate. An innate knowing of what is sterile and what is fertile where the mutation is specifically biological and dependent on collaboration with others.  
• \[omega-like glyph\] ▽ The perverse denial of evolutionary standards. An innate contrariness which refuses to mutate.

The unrestrained acceptance of guidance.  
• ♂ ▲ The unrelenting energy for growth will eventually triumph. The energy and potential for individual mutation.  
• \[unknown glyph\] ▽ Internal instability which both accepts and rejects authority simultaneously. Energy and potential that is conditioned by others leading to instability.

Difficulties can be only overcome when all the pertinent factors have been analyzed.  
• ⊕ ▲ The understanding that confusion is natural and must always exist before clarity can be achieved. An innate knowing that order will emerge from confusion.  
• ☿ ▽ The reliance on intellect at the expense of intuition can lead to unnecessary frustration. The inability to know that order will emerge and the drive to find this knowing elsewhere.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 (exaltation), ▽ U+25BD (detriment), ♂ U+2642 (Mars), ♀ U+2640 (Venus), ☿ U+263F (Mercury), ⊕ U+2295 (Earth).  
Uncertain at this resolution: sun-like circle-with-dot (likely ☉ U+2609 or ⊙ U+2299), omega-like node (looks like ω U+03C9), psi-like (Ψ U+03A8), one additional unknown glyph.

Notes:  
Degree spans correspond to the Gate 3 band (Aries → Taurus per the header page). Exact signs are not printed beside the ranges here.

# Gate 4 — The Gate of Formulization

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 4 page. Header lists Channel 63-4 “Logic” with the note “A design of mental ease mixed with doubt,” Harmonic Gate 63 “After Completion,” Center Ajna, Circuit Understanding, and an astrologic position span in Leo. One paragraph explains logic, doubt, and the idea that a formula is only a potential. Three small mandalas at the bottom are labeled with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Formulization.” A compact specification block at upper center details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Leo-to-Leo degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Leo glyph is at the upper right; a large gray “4” appears at upper left; a blue page number “4” is printed near the lower corners.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Formulization

Channel 63-4 Logic  
A design of mental ease mixed with doubt  
Harmonic Gate 63 After Completion  
Center Ajna  
Circuit Understanding  
Astrologic Position 18° 52′ 30″ ♌ – 24° 30′ 00″ ♌

This is a gate of good fortune. It is clear that the most grounded path is based on logic. To use logic, that is to be  
able to substantiate things in fact, protects one from misjudgment. And this gate through the Understanding circuit is related to the Channel of Judgment. The real difference lies in the frequency. Splenic awareness operates  
in the now, but the mind's frequency is over all time. The pressure of a doubt or suspicion can last a lifetime. The  
I’Ching name for this gate is Youthful Folly. The potential is to have an answer to the doubt. A Stream of Aware-  
ness has four stages in its process. An answer, a formula is only a potential. Many answers are sheer foolishness.  
A formula is the beginning of a logical awareness. It is worthy of remembering that an answer is not necessarily a  
solution or a fact.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF EXPLANATION (3)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF FORMULIZATION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF REVOLUTION (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Leo ♌ U+264C

Notes:  
Side indicator shows neighboring numbers around the rim: “7 ◁ 4 ▷ 21.” Thin yellow rule near the lower margin.

## Gate 4 — YOUTHFUL FOLLY, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 4 “YOUTHFUL FOLLY” lines page with six line themes, Leo degree ranges, and paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) with planetary glyphs.

Long description:  
Heading reads “YOUTHFUL FOLLY.” A gold subtitle states the theme in two lines. The left margin lists the six lines (6→1) with their degree spans in Leo. The main column shows a blue subheading for each line followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “4” sits at top right; a blue “4” appears near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

YOUTHFUL FOLLY

THE ENERGY TO BEGUILE AND SUCCEED DESPITE IGNORANCE. FREE-  
DOM FROM RETRIBUTION.

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Leo):  
6 Excess — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
5 Seduction — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″  
4 The liar — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
3 Irresponsibility — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″  
2 Acceptance — 19° 48′ 45″ – 20° 45′ 00″  
1 Pleasure — 18° 52′ 30″ – 19° 48′ 45″

Repeated and conscious abuse of norms will not escape discipline.  
• ☿ ▲ The development through experience of techniques to apply self-restraint. The potential in a logical process to recognize when the understanding is not complete and have the patience to wait out the process.  
• ♂ ▽ The gall to accept punishment as the price of excess. Despite recognizing the incompleteness a lack of patience with the process.

Allowing others to assume responsibility as a shield against potential punishment.  
• ♃ ▲ Unearned reward and recognition. The potential to succeed through the understanding of others.  
• ω ▽ A life of lip service to antiquated and unsatisfying values. Cynicism. The potential for cynicism that comes with always having to acknowledge the understanding of others.

Role playing as an art form. The actor.  
• ☉ ▲ Fantasy protects and nurtures a sense of purpose and reason no matter how mis- guided. The potential to find or to illustrate the formulas through fantasy.  
• ▽ Time always brings humiliation. The potential danger to see the fantasy as fact.

The general refusal to apply oneself diligently when one can get by with much less effort.  
• ♀ ▲ Where art is more valued then the artist. The potential to enjoy the formulas with no regard to their practical application.  
• ω ▽ The rationalization of irresponsibility as an act of refocalization. The potential to justify such a process in order to maintain it.

The recognition of limitation in oneself and others leads to tolerance and the suspension of judgment.  
• C ▲ The glorification of feelings. The mother which always pardons the errant child. The potential to recognize that not everyone can understand,  
• ♂ ▽ The assertion of the ego at the expense of others' failures. The potential to take advantage of the lack of understanding in others.

Ultimate pleasure cannot be achieved without perfect timing.  
• C ▲ The instinct to know the right moment and circumstances where pleasure is re- warded and not punished. The potential to recognize that there is a natural timing to the understanding process.  
• ⊕ ▽ Timing is not a product of discipline. Exaggerated self-discipline leads to the abuse of pleasure. The potential to recognize but the urge to force the timing.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ♃ U+2643 Jupiter; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ω U+03C9 omega; C \= stylized Moon glyph used in this edition (cf. ☽ U+263D / ☾ U+263E).

Notes:  
Blue page number “4” printed at bottom corners; thin yellow baseline near footer; sign context is Leo.

# Gate 5 — The Gate of Fixed Rhythms

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 5 page. Header lists Channel 15-5 “Rhythm” with the note “The design of being in the flow,” Harmonic Gate 15 “Modesty,” Center Sacral, Circuit Understanding, and an astrologic position span in Sagittarius. A body paragraph explains the mechanical power to fix patterns and daily rhythm/tempo. Three small mandalas at the bottom are labeled with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Fixed Rhythms.” A compact specification block beneath the title details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Sagittarius-to-Sagittarius degree range. A thin rule separates the header from two explanatory paragraphs. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Sagittarius arrow glyph appears at upper right; a large gray “5” is at upper left; a blue page number “5” is printed near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the footer. Side indicator shows “9 ◁ 5 ▷ 26”.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Fixed Rhythms

Channel 15-5 Rhythm  
The design of being in the flow  
Harmonic Gate 15 Modesty  
Center Sacral  
Circuit Understanding  
Astrologic Position 11° 22′ 30″ ♐ – 17° 00′ 00″ ♐

Tantric channels are about the empowerment of the Self. No other channel in Design has such significance. Being  
in the Flow is a universal mechanism and binds all life forms together in the ‘rhythm of Life’.

The 5th gate is the power to fix the pattern. It is the gate of habits, of daily rythm and tempo. This is not about establishing the pattern through awareness. This is a mechanical gate. The role of awareness is to not identify with concepts that try to alter the natural pattern. If it is natural to eat at 7 o’clock in the morning, nothing should be allowed to interfere with that pattern.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF CONSCIOUSNESS (4)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF HABITS  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF SEPARATION (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Sagittarius ♐ U+2650

Notes:  
Yellow baseline rule near footer; blue page number “5”; side index “9 ◁ 5 ▷ 26”.

## Gate 5 — WAITING, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 5 “WAITING” lines page with six line themes, Sagittarius degree ranges, and paired notes per line marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) with planetary glyphs.

Long description:  
Page heading reads “WAITING.” A gold subtitle states: “THE FUNDAMENTAL ATTUNEMENT TO NATURAL RHYTHMS. WAITING AS AN ACTIVE STATE OF AWARENESS.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with their degree spans. The main column presents a blue subheading for each line, followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “5” sits at the top right; a blue “5” appears at the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Sagittarius):  
6 Yielding — 16° 03′ 45″ – 17° 00′ 00″  
5 Joy — 15° 07′ 30″ – 16° 03′ 45″  
4 The hunter — 14° 11′ 15″ – 15° 07′ 30″  
3 Compulsiveness — 13° 15′ 00″ – 14° 11′ 15″  
2 Inner peace — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
1 Perseverance — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″

Main text:  
WAITING

THE FUNDAMENTAL ATTUNEMENT TO NATURAL RHYTHMS. WAITING AS  
AN ACTIVE STATE OF AWARENESS.

Waiting is never free from pressure, physical or mental and is often punctu-  
ated by the unexpected.  
• ♆ ▲ The growth of awareness that comes with bending to the universal flow. Accepting that in one's fixed rhythm despite the pressures, growth will be empowered, and often through the unexpected. No polarity.  
• ▽ There is no planet in detriment; each in its way, given the power of this position, will yield to the inevitable. No planet in detriment.

Waiting as an aspect of enlightenment.  
• ♀ ▲ To remain calm as the ultimate aesthetic and thus recognize the inner meaning of being. The power to be calm and to find one's place in the flow.  
• ω ▽ Joy dismissed as an illusion, waiting as a failure. Disillusionment with recognizing one's place in the flow.

Waiting as a guarantee of survival.  
• ☉ ▲ The creative genius to transform the most passive experience into active achieve-  
ment. The power to make the best of one's fixed rhythm.  
• ☉ ▽ The vanity of a personality so strong that unwilling to hide behind a blind, threatens its very survival. The drive to deny one's own rhythms with predictable costs.

The fear engendered by the sense of helplessness resulting in unnecessary  
stress and activity.  
• ♆ ▲ Compulsiveness can be limited in its negative effect through flights of imagination. Though still stressful, they do not lead to action. A surrender to the limitations of a fixed rhythm through the empowerment of the imagination.  
• ☾ ▽ The Moon cannnot stand still. Unable to surrender and at odds with one's own rhythm.

The ability to ignore the temptation to take premature action.  
• ♀ ▲ The gift of maintaining composure through idealizing tranquility. The power to be comfortable with one's rhythm.  
• ω ▽ Inner peace experienced as stagnation. The drive for power that is constrained by the fixed rhythm.

If the captain must, he goes down with the ship.  
• ♂ ▲ Courage in the face of adversity. The power to maintain one's own rhythm.  
• ⊕ ▽ The premature and often disastrous urge to cut one's losses. Weakness in maintaining one's rhythm when challenged.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ω U+03C9 omega; ☾/☽ U+263E/U+263D Moon; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ⊕ U+2295 Earth.

Notes:  
Degree spans match Gate 5’s Sagittarius band from 11° 22′ 30″ to 17° 00′ 00″. Blue page number “5”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 6 — The Gate of Friction

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 6 page. Header lists Channel 59-6 “Mating,” Harmonic Gate 59 “Dispersion,” Center Solar Plexus, Circuit Defence, and an astrologic span in Virgo. One long paragraph explains conflict, intimacy boundaries, and the hope/pain cycle. Three mandalas at the bottom are labeled with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Friction.” A compact specification block at upper center details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Virgo-to-Virgo degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Virgo glyph is at the upper right; a large gray “6” appears at upper left; a small blue “6” is printed near the lower corners. Side indicator at left shows “47 ◁ 6 ▷ 46”.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Friction

Channel 59-6 Mating  
A design focused on reproduction  
Harmonic Gate 59 Dispersion  
Center Solar Plexus  
Circuit Defence  
Astrologic Position 22° 37′ 30″ ♍ – 28° 15′ 00″ ♍

This is the most complex gate of the Solar Plexus and the most difficult in terms of finding the awareness within  
the wave. Not only is it a powerful motor with the energy for producing life itself but it also contains the awareness  
possibilities of all three Streams of Emotional awareness; Feeling, Emoting, and Sensitivity. Where the 50th gate is  
the awareness of our Personal Defense Reflex, Conflict is our evolved Genetic Defense Reflex. There is no survival  
for Humanity unless it continues to reproduce. From the perspective of our genes, this is our only purpose, to  
maintain and continue the genetic pool. In our biology, the 6th gate is associated with our PH balance. It is PH that  
keeps our outside/outside and our inside/inside. It establishes the boundary of the body. Throughout most of our  
history, this has been a mechanical process. The possibility of awareness in this gate could transform the nature of  
all of Humanities intimacy. Who is in and who is out, who can penetrate or be penetrated is the work of this gate.  
However, as an energy, and this is its general state, it always confines intimacy to a hope and pain cycle.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF EDEN (3)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF CONFLICT  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF THE PLANE (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Virgo ♍ U+264D

Notes:  
Yellow baseline rule near footer; side index “47 ◁ 6 ▷ 46”; page number “6” in blue at lower corners.

## Gate 6 — CONFLICT, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 6 “CONFLICT” lines page with six line themes in Virgo degree ranges and paired notes per line marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) with planetary glyphs.

Long description:  
The heading reads “CONFLICT.” A gold subtitle states: “THE FUNDAMENTAL DESIGN COMPONENT OF PROGRESS. THE LAW THAT GROWTH CANNOT EXIST WITHOUT FRICTION.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with degree spans. The main column presents a blue subheading for each line, followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “6” appears at top right; a small blue “6” near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Virgo):  
6 The peacemaker — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
5 Arbitration — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″  
4 Triumph — 25° 26′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
3 Allegiance — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 26′ 15″  
2 The guerilla — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
1 Retreat — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″

Main text:  
CONFLICT

THE FUNDAMENTAL DESIGN COMPONENT OF PROGRESS. THE LAW THAT  
GROWTH CANNOT EXIST WITHOUT FRICTION.

The discipline and integrity of a superior force to unilaterally cease conflict to  
permit surrender and survival of its foe.  
• ☿ ▲ The highest form of reason is that life is sacred. The emotional power to end conflict tempered by feelings and sensitivity to others.  
• ♀ ▽ The peacemaker whose actions are just but whose terms are unacceptable. The emotional power to end conflict but only after one's conditions have been satisfied.

The faith derived from analytical diligence and emotional control that permits  
a higher authority to judge a conflict.  
• ♀ ▲ Harmony furthered through the avoidance of direct conflict. Sensitivity to conflict can lead to the avoidance of intimacy.  
• C ▽ Where a party to arbitration sees itself as the best possible judge and will only ac-  
cept judgement if it is the victor. Insensitivity to the concerns of others in a conflict.

A position on natural and unchallengeable power.  
• ☉ ▲ The charity and wisdom that must come with victory and the movement towards  
new horizons. The power of emotions to dominate a relationship.  
• ω ▽ The conqueror and purger. The lack of emotional control that is destructive in relationships.

The ability to secure support and create strength out of a weak position.  
When connected to the harmonic Gate 59, mating that results in conception.  
• ♆ ▲ The destruction of old forms through union; either mundane, as above, sexual un-  
ion, or exalted as universalization. The depth of feelings that can enrich union and intimacy.  
• ω ▽ The rejection of allegiance as submission to established order. Sensitivity to con-  
trols which may eventually reject intimacy.

The ability to maximize an inferior position through timely contact and with-  
drawal.  
• ♀ ▲ Aesthetic sensitivity and mental detail can find the weakest point. The sensitivity to find the weakest point in a conflict and to exploit it emotionally.  
• ♂ ▽ The kamikaze, striking but moot. A lack of sensibility that blunders into conflicts.

The realization that wasting one's resources against overwhelming odds is  
not courage but folly.  
• ω ▲ The power of regeneration that can embrace retreat as a phase and not a failure. The emotional stability to accept conflict.  
• ☿ ▽ The inferiority complex, where retreat is experienced as personal weakness. Emo-  
tional instability in times of conflict.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; ω U+03C9 omega; C \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).

Notes:  
Degree spans match Gate 6’s Virgo band. Yellow baseline near footer; blue page number “6”.

# Gate 7 — The Gate of the Role of the Self

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 7 page. Header lists Channel 31-7 “The Alpha” with the note “For ‘good’ or ‘bad’ the design of leadership,” Harmonic Gate 31 “Influence,” Center G, Circuit Understanding, and an astrologic position span in Leo. One paragraph explains leadership roles and conditioning. Three mandalas at the bottom are labeled with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of the Role of the Self.” A compact specification block beneath the title details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Leo-to-Leo degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Leo glyph appears at upper right, a large gray “7” at upper left, and a blue page number “7” near the lower corners. Side index at left shows “33 ◁ 7 ▷ 4”.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Role of the Self

Channel 31-7 The Alpha  
For ‘good’ or ‘bad’ the design of leadership  
Harmonic Gate 31 Influence  
Center G  
Circuit Understanding  
Astrologic Position 13° 15′ 00″ ♌ – 18° 52′ 30″ ♌

The 7th gate is part of the Sphinx Cross. It is a gate of logical direction. This is the gate of the role of the Self in  
interaction. The Channel of the ‘Alpha’ is a design of Leadership. It is the Understanding Circuit which leads us  
into the future. It is a rule of all forms, that only logic, only the tested and established patterns, can be followed  
with any certainty. Below in the line keynotes, it is clear how the nature of these leadership roles are determined.  
What is significant, is that the Self is unaware. The role is genetic and mechanical and it does not respond to a  
concept of role. An Authoritarian will always be one, regardless. However, the conditioning power of this gate is  
enormous and has had profound effects on our collective history. The 31 may have the Influence but the direction  
and role of the 7, is all too often the power behind the throne. Bill Clinton with his 31 and Hillary Clinton with 7\. 2 or  
Nehru’s 31 and Indira Ghandis’ 7\. 2 are classic examples of democratic role conditioning.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE SPHINX (3)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF INTERACTION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF MASKS (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Leo ♌ U+264C

Notes:  
Yellow baseline rule near footer; side index “33 ◁ 7 ▷ 4”; blue page number “7”.

## Gate 7 — THE ARMY, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 7 “THE ARMY” lines page with six line themes, Leo degree ranges, and paired notes per line marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) with planetary glyphs.

Long description:  
Heading reads “THE ARMY.” A gold subtitle states: “THE POINT OF CONVERGENCE. BY DESIGN, THE NEED FOR LEADERSHIP TO GUIDE AND ORDER SOCIETY.” The left margin lists the six lines (6→1) with their degree spans in Leo. The main column shows a blue subheading for each line followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “7” sits at top right; a blue “7” appears near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Leo):  
6 The administrator — 17° 56′ 15″ – 18° 52′ 30″  
5 The general — 17° 00′ 00″ – 17° 56′ 15″  
4 The abdicator — 16° 03′ 45″ – 17° 00′ 00″  
3 The anarchist — 15° 07′ 30″ – 16° 03′ 45″  
2 The democrat — 14° 11′ 15″ – 15° 07′ 30″  
1 Authoritarian — 13° 15′ 00″ – 14° 11′ 15″

Main text:  
THE ARMY

THE POINT OF CONVERGENCE. BY DESIGN, THE NEED FOR LEADERSHIP  
TO GUIDE AND ORDER SOCIETY.

The ability to share and justly apportion power.  
• ☿ ▲ The power to communicate the frameworks of responsibility. The capacity of the Self through its role to communicate responsibility.  
• \[circle-with-dot, upward arrow\] ▽ The bureaucrat whose lust for power eventually destabilizes the organisation. The role of the Self to seek power through the communication of responsibility.

Leadership whose authority must be absolute and is sanctioned by society in times of crisis.  
• ♀ ▲ The gift of attracting loyalty necessary in harmonizing the potential of society. The capacity of the Self through its role to attract loyalty.  
• ♆ ▽ The commander isolated from his troops and obsessed with victory at any price. The lack of loyalty when the Self insists on isolation.

The willingness to accept the judgment of the people and/or the rule of law.  
• ☉ ▲ The grace and wisdom to step down for the benefit of the whole. The capacity of the Self to accept the judgment of others.  
• \[circle-with-dot, upward arrow\] ▽ One who must be forced from power by overwhelming opposition. The refusal of the Self to accept the judgement of others.

The rejection of any institutionalized order.  
• C ▲ The constant need for change no matter what the prevailing conditions. The drive of the Self to express many roles.  
• ☿ ▽ The nihilist. The capacity of the Self to deny value in any role.

The ability to lead by serving the will of the majority.  
• ♆ ▲ The application of universally accepted systems. When connected through the Alpha Channel to Gate 31 Influence, the potential for widespread and revolutionary effect on society. The capacity of the Self to lead when chosen.  
• ☿ ▽ Elitism and denegation by democrats of democracy. The capacity of the Self once chosen to feel superior to those who chose them.

The iron hand both enlightened and despotic.  
• ♀ ▲ Venus exalted, as in the basic values and rules imposed on a child. The capacity of the Self to guide with authority.  
• ☿ ▽ The distorted intellect that believes only it knows best. The capacity of the Self to insist that its authority is best.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; ☉ U+2609 Sun; C \= stylized Moon glyph used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Unknown glyph: small circle with central dot and an upward arrow (appears in two detriment bullets).

Notes:  
Degree spans match Gate 7’s Leo band from 13° 15′ 00″ to 18° 52′ 30″. Blue page number “7”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 8 — The Gate of Contribution

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 8 page. Header lists Channel 8-1 “Inspiration,” Harmonic Gate 1 “The Creative,” Center Throat, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic span crossing late Capricorn into early Aquarius. One paragraph explains individual contribution and leadership by example. Three small mandalas at the bottom are labeled with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Contribution.” A compact specification block beneath the title gives channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an astrologic position range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about individuality and contribution. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A large gray “8” appears at upper left; a small blue “8” is printed near the lower corners. Side index at left shows “23 ◁ 8 ▷ 20.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Contribution

Channel 8-1 Inspiration  
The creative Role Model  
Harmonic Gate 1 The Creative  
Center Throat  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 24° 30′ 00″ ♑ – 00° 07′ 30″ ♒ \[I\]

The 8th gate is a mirror to the function of the 23rd gate of Assimilation. Both are part of the Knowing circuit, both  
have the ability to express the possibilities of individuality. The 23rd gate says, ‘I know’ and the 8th gate says, ‘I  
know I can’. This is the gate of the individual contribution to the whole. Imagine that a group of tourists are lost on  
an excursion. The 8th gate does not lead either logically (‘I think, it’s that way’ 31: Influence) nor Abstractly (‘I re-  
member, it was this way’ 33: Retreat), it can only act as an example. The 8th gate stands up, gets everybody’s  
attention and says, ‘I know, I can find the way’ and leaves. Those who wish to take the chance and follow are wel-  
comed and if no one takes heed, the knower will go on alone. The contribution of the Individual can never be  
pressed onto the collective. It would only lead to resistance.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF CONTAGEN (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF CONTRIBUTION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF UNCERTAINTY

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
♑ U+2651 Capricorn, ♒ U+2652 Aquarius

Notes:  
Yellow baseline rule near footer; side index “23 ◁ 8 ▷ 20”; blue page number “8”. The small “\[I\]” mark appears at the end of the astrologic line.

## Gate 8 — HOLDING TOGETHER, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 8 “HOLDING TOGETHER” lines page with six line themes, degree ranges spanning late Capricorn into early Aquarius, and paired notes per line marked by ▲ for exaltation and ▽ for detriment with planetary or related glyphs.

Long description:  
Heading reads “HOLDING TOGETHER.” A gold subtitle states: “THE BASIC WORTH REALIZED IN CONTRIBUTING INDIVIDUAL EFFORTS TO GROUP GOALS.” The left margin lists the six lines (6→1) with degree spans. The main column presents a blue subheading for each line followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “8” appears at the top right; a small blue “8” is printed near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Capricorn→Aquarius for line 6; Capricorn for 5→1):  
6 Communion — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
5 Dharma — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″  
4 Respect — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
3 The phoney — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″  
2 Service — 25° 26′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
1 Honesty — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 26′ 15″

Main text:  
HOLDING TOGETHER

THE BASIC WORTH REALIZED IN CONTRIBUTING INDIVIDUAL EFFORTS TO  
GROUP GOALS.

The certainty which grows out of harmony.  
• ♀ ▲ The awareness of patterns that ensures correct timing. The gift of knowing when to contribute creatively.  
• ω ▽ Doubt, which may engender regret even in the most ideal circumstances. Uncer-  
tainty in timing and regret despite circumstances.

Holding together does not exclude eventual separation. Successful union, after a  
proper term will encourage separation. The fledgling once mature is expected to  
leave the nest. This is right action which does not harm the integrity of the union.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ The teacher. Contribution as part of a process of sharing that accepts and expects limitation, exemplified in teaching.  
• ☉ ▽ The parent that cannot let go of the child, understanding only, that its authority is being challenged. Contribution as an end in itself that neither accepts nor expects limitation, exemplified by the parent that cannot let go of the child.

The gift of naturally recognizing the contributions of others and particularly,  
the acknowledgement of those who lead by example.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ The uncompromising drive to assimilate. The drive to contribute and be an example to and for others.  
• ☿ ▽ In a group where limitation is transcended, reason alone cannot predict individual worth. As an example, the acknowledged leader of an athletic team is not necessarily the most talented. A gift for contribution that is not conditioned by limitations.

The acceptance of the style and not the substance of communal actions.  
• C ▲ The perfected and rarely detected superficial intimacy. The example as an expres-  
sion through style, not substance.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ A shallowness that underestimates others and overestimates its own ability to con-  
tinue its deception without detection. An overreliance and unfounded confidence in style.

The highest good, to serve selflessly.  
• ☉ ▲ The potential to be an example through unselfish expression.  
• ⊕ ▽ The earth in detriment, where reward is a prerequisite of service. The willingness to be an example for a price.

The truthful acceptance of limitation and re-cognition that it can only be tran-  
scended through sharing.  
• ♆ ▲ The awareness that the whole is always greater than the sum of its parts. Know-  
ing that creative expression must be honestly communicated and shared.  
• ☿ ▽ Withdrawal. The fear of losing individuality in a group environment. The design to share creativity at the expense of individuality.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; ω U+03C9 omega; C \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Unclear at this resolution: two exaltation glyphs (lines 5 and 4\) and one detriment glyph (line 3).

Notes:  
Degree spans match Gate 8’s header band (Capricorn 24° 30′ 00″ → Aquarius 00° 07′ 30″). Blue page number “8”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 9 — The Gate of Focus

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
 Alt text:  
 Gate 9 header page. Channel 9–52 “Concentration” (a design of determination), Harmonic Gate 52 “Keeping Still (Mountain),” Center Sacral, Circuit Understanding (Logic). Astrologic span **05° 45′ 00″ – 11° 22′ 30″ ♐**. This gate provides the mechanical **power to focus**, the determinant that turns restless Root pressure into sustained, useful attention when coupled with Gate 52’s stillness.

Long description:  
 Gate 9 has a direct relationship to Gate 62 in the Throat: **62 \= Preponderance of the Small**, **9 \= Taming Power of the Small** — the two “detail” gates of the Logic circuit, both mechanical in nature. Gate 9’s power is **determination**: the capacity to **maintain focus**. When 52 is defined to this power, **restlessness** is channeled and the depressive tone dissolves. This focused determination underpins the whole Logic process, which demands **continued experimentation, testing, repetition, and practice**. Put simply: Gate 9 is the **power to focus** that empowers the entire Understanding stream.

Figures (bottom captions):

* THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF PLANNING (4)

* THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF FOCUS

* THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF IDENTIFICATION (2)

## Gate 9 — THE TAMING POWER OF THE SMALL, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 9 “THE TAMING POWER OF THE SMALL” lines page with six line themes, degree ranges, and paired notes per line marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) with glyphs.

Long description:  
Heading reads “THE TAMING POWER OF THE SMALL.” A gold subtitle states: “POTENTIAL CAN BE FULFILLED THROUGH DETAILED ATTENTION TO ALL PERTINENT ASPECTS.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with their degree spans. The main column presents a blue subheading for each line followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “9” sits at top right; a blue page number “9” appears at the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges):  
6 Gratitude — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
5 Faith — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″  
4 Dedication — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
3 The straw that breaks the camel’s back — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″  
2 Misery loves company — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
1 Sensibility — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″

Main text:  
THE TAMING POWER OF THE SMALL

POTENTIAL CAN BE FULFILLED THROUGH DETAILED ATTENTION TO ALL  
PERTINENT ASPECTS.

The joy which comes with accepting small rewards for small victories.  
• C ▲ The Moon exalted, where the power of the small nourishes right perspective. The power to enjoy the concentrating process.  
• ω ▽ No single step is of value until the journey is over. The energy for expression that cannot find joy in the process until it is complete.

The trust that detailed adherence will lead to fulfillment.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ Loyalty to the letter of the law. The power to focus and bring value to concentration.  
• ⊕ ▽ Like the mystery of God, doubt engendered through the perceived illogic of a process. Where the lack of power to concentrate leads to doubt.

Regardless of the pressures or stress the disciplined attention to detail.  
• C ▲ Right action that leads inevitably to actualization. The power to act on the potential of the focus.  
• ♂ ▽ The persistent urge to want to skip essential steps. The drive for action that ignores the details.

The overlooked minor element that always predetermines failure.  
• ⊕ ▲ The use of force to temporarily overcome impediments. The loss of power through the failure to focus.  
• ☉ ▽ Persistent force that saps vitality and turns molehills into mountains. The power to turn a focus into an obsession.

A balanced and responsible approach to problem solving.  
• ω ▲ The ability to avoid frustration through the creation of new forms. The power in focusing to create new forms.  
• ♂ ▽ After a hasty and frustrating search the urge to kick in the door when the key is in your pocket. The power to generate that will lose its focus.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; C \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); ω U+03C9 omega; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ♂ U+2642 Mars.  
Unclear at this resolution: the exaltation glyph on line 5\.

Notes:  
Blue page number “9”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 10 — The Gate of the Behaviour of the Self

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 10 page. Header lists three channels (10-34 Exploration, 10-57 Perfected Form, 20-10 Awakening), harmonic gates 34/57/20, Center G, Circuit Centering, and an astrologic span from late Sagittarius into early Capricorn. Two paragraphs explain behaviour of the Self and the Channel of Awakening. Three mandalas at the bottom are labeled with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of the Behaviour of the Self.” A compact specification block at upper center details the three related channels, their subtitles, the three harmonic gates with names, center G, circuit Centering, and an astrologic position range marked with Sagittarius then Capricorn symbols. A thin rule separates the header from two explanatory paragraphs about behaviour, conditioning, and awakening. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A large gray “10” appears at upper left; a small blue “10” is printed near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Behaviour of the Self

Channel 10-34 Exploration  
A design of following one’s convictions  
10-57 Perfected Form  
A design of survival  
20-10 Awakening  
A design of commitment to higher principles

Harmonic Gates 34 The Power of the Great  
57 The Gentle  
20 Contemplation  
Center G  
Circuit Centering  
Astrologic Position 28° 15′ 00″ ♐ – 03° 52′ 30″ ♑

This is the most complex gate that opens up out of the G center. Out of the 10th gate, three channels can be  
formed. The 10th gate is a behavioural bridge which links the Centering circuit to the Knowing circuit. It is the po-  
tential behaviour of the Self. Before this behaviour can manifest, it must be guided spontaneously by the Intuition  
(57) from the Splenic Awareness, and empowered by the Sacral (34) and then it can manifest through the Throat  
in the now (20). Treading is a gate of the Vessel, a gate of Love. It is the 10th gate which ensures the perfection of  
our form and its survival and the conviction which guides it, is the Love of Living, of being alive. The complexity of  
this gate can also be seen on its effects on others. This is a gate of powerful conditioning influence. How the  
planetary collective behaves is determined by this gate. Awakening is not possible without a fixed behaviour. The  
Li. The only way is surrender to the privilege of exploring life in a self-conscious form.

The Channel of Awakening (20-10) is the oldest of our three mystical possibilities. The first and foremost initiation  
is to recognize who we are. This channel is perfectly illustrated by the inscription over the Oracle of Delphi, ‘Know  
thyself’. Awakening is not a commitment to becoming something, it is a commitment to being oneself. One cannot  
know what is incomplete. The mystical love of this Vessel gate, is the love of Self as it is, in the now. This is  
Awakeness.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE VESSEL OF LOVE (4)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF BEHAVIOUR  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF PREVENTION (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
♐ U+2650 Sagittarius, ♑ U+2651 Capricorn

Notes:  
Yellow baseline rule near footer; blue page number “10”. Side gutter marks present; not legible at this resolution.

## Gate 10 — TREADING, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 10 “TREADING” lines page with six line themes and paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment). Degrees span late Sagittarius to early Capricorn.

Long description:  
Heading reads “TREADING.” A gold subtitle states: “THE UNDERLYING CODE OF BEHAVIOUR WHICH ENSURES SUCCESSFUL INTERACTION DESPITE CIRCUMSTANCES.” The left margin lists lines 6 to 1 with degree spans. The main column presents a blue subheading for each line, followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “10” sits at the top right; a blue page number “10” appears near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges):  
6 The role model — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
5 The heretic — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″  
4 The opportunist — 01° 03′ 45″ – 02° 00′ 00″  
3 The martyr — 00° 07′ 30″ – 01° 03′ 45″  
2 The hermit — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
1 Modesty — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″

Main text:  
TREADING

THE UNDERLYING CODE OF BEHAVIOUR WHICH ENSURES SUCCESSFUL  
INTERACTION DESPITE CIRCUMSTANCES.

The perfect expression of the norms through action rather than words.  
• ω ▲ The constant example that refocuses the complacent on the basic integrity of set behaviour. The enduring value of the expression of the Self through action rather than words.  
• \[unknown glyph, “2-like”\] ▽ The hypocrite. Do as I say, not as I do. Behaviour restricted to words rather than action.

Direct and overt challenge to norms.  
• \[unclear exaltation glyph\] ▲ The ability to succeed through the understanding and expression of higher principles. Principled behaviour which directly challenges tradition.  
• ♂ ▽ The burning at the stake. Behaviour which directly challenges behaviour and is eventually punished.

The acceptance of norms until a successful transformation can be engendered.  
• \[unclear exaltation glyph\] ▲ Transformation that is transcendence to a higher code. The maintaining of behavioural patterns until the right moment and opportunity for transformation.  
• ☿ ▽ Opportunism as a game and/or mental exercise. Altering one’s behavioural patterns in order to take advantage of opportunities.

The futile rejection of standards based on a just awareness.  
• ⊕ ▲ The martyr as an enduring example whose behaviour is ultimately enshrined. Behaviour that is ultimately challenged by others.  
• C ▽ The martyr complex. The active pursuit of martyrdom for personal aggrandizement. Behaviour as a way to attract attention.

The successful sidestepping of behavioral requirements through isolation.  
• ☿ ▲ Mercury exalted, where mental functions enrich aloneness. Independent behaviour through isolation.  
• ♂ ▽ The angry exile. Isolation to preserve independent behaviour in the face of conditioning.

An innate sense to know and accept one’s place.  
• ☉ ▲ A valued sense of purpose no matter what the position. The ability to know one’s place and how to act despite circumstances.  
• C ▽ The affliction of oversensitivity and hurt feeling. Oversensitivity to external conditioning of behaviour.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ω U+03C9 omega; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ☉ U+2609 Sun; “C” \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Unclear at this resolution: line-6 detriment glyph (“2-like”); line-5 exaltation glyph; line-4 exaltation glyph.

Notes:  
Degree spans match Gate 10’s header band (Sagittarius 28° 15′ 00″ → Capricorn 03° 52′ 30″). Blue page number “10”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 11 — The Gate of Ideas

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 11 page. Header lists Channel 11-56 “Curiosity” with the note “The design of the searcher,” Harmonic Gate 56 “The Wanderer,” Center Ajna, Circuit Sensing, and an astrologic span in Sagittarius. One long paragraph explains ideas as conceptual possibilities and links this gate to the left eye’s visual memory. Three mandalas at the bottom are captioned with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Ideas.” A compact spec block beneath the title gives the channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Sagittarius-to-Sagittarius degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about the nature of ideas, expression, stimulation, and the Sensing circuit. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Sagittarius arrow glyph appears at the upper right, a large gray “11” at the upper left, and a small blue “11” sits near the lower corners. Side index at left shows “24 ◁ 11 ▷ 10.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Ideas

Channel 11-56 Curiosity  
The design of the searcher  
Harmonic Gate 56 The Wanderer  
Center Ajna  
Circuit Sensing  
Astrologic Position 22° 37′ 30″ ♐ – 28° 15′ 00″ ♐

The gate of Ideas is the conceptual possibility which emerges from the difficult task of realizing. This gate pre-  
pares a concept for articulation through the capacity of its visual memory. Like its mirror, the 17th gate of Opinion,  
the 11th gate is associated in our biology with the eyes, here, the left eye. The left eye operates in a wave. In the  
now, it sees only an aspect of the whole, yet over time, given its memory, it can capture the entire picture. Ideas  
are not facts, they are possibilities. The process of making sense out of something cannot stop at realization. The  
goal of mental awareness is expression. The idea is a tool geared to express what has been sensed. This is a  
gate of those who seek stimulation, who have a need to stimulate, to share their ideas. So many people are frus-  
trated when their wonderful ideas do not turn into realities, but look at the design. The fulfillment of an idea is its  
verbal expression, not action; and with good reason, this is the circuit of cycles, transitoriness and the wave. Ideas  
are not a prescription for action, they are stimulation for reflection.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF EDEN (4)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF IDEAS  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF EDUCATION (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Sagittarius ♐ U+2650

Notes:  
Yellow baseline rule near footer; side index “24 ◁ 11 ▷ 10”; blue page number “11”.

## Gate 11 — PEACE, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 11 “PEACE” lines page with six line themes in Sagittarius degree ranges, each with paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) plus planetary glyphs.

Long description:  
Heading reads “PEACE.” A gold subtitle states: “A HARMONIC CONDITION IN THE INDIVIDUAL OR SOCIETY THAT PERMITS ASSESSMENT BEFORE RENEWED ACTION.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with their degree spans. The main column presents a blue subheading for each line followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “11” sits at the top right; a blue page number “11” appears near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Sagittarius):  
6 Adaptability — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
5 The philanthropist — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″  
4 The teacher — 25° 26′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
3 The realist — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 26′ 15″  
2 Rigour — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
1 Attunement — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″

Main text:  
PEACE

A HARMONIC CONDITION IN THE INDIVIDUAL OR SOCIETY THAT PERMITS  
ASSESSMENT BEFORE RENEWED ACTION.

The inner balance to accept transition.  
• ♆ ▲ The innate awareness that all forms are transitory. The realization that ideas lead to change and are changeable.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ Adaptability in its most negative manifestation. The speculator who profits at the expense of others in times of peace or war. The realization of what idea is of value in any situation.

The gift of naturally recognizing the contributions of others and particularly,  
the acknowledgement of those who lead by example.  
• C ▲ The motiveless nurturing of the disenfranchised to ensure harmony. Philosophic and humanitarian ideas.  
• ☿ ▽ The withdrawing from direct contact, where giving is a form of defence. Giving away ideas out of a sense of insecurity.

The ability to express the essential nature of peace.  
• C ▲ The sage, that in the extreme, can teach harmony to the tone deaf. Venus is also exalted. The ability to reach out and attract the alienated. Concepts which are clear and transferable. Ideas which can attract and inform the uneducated.  
• ☉ ▽ The Guru, whose most valued wisdom is intentionally limited to a few. Ideas which can only be grasped by the few.

The acknowledgment that peace is transitory.  
• ω ▲ The internal renewal to maintain strength and alertness. The realization that ideas come and go.  
• ♀ ▽ The tendency to appreciate harmony to the point of delusion. The belief that beauty is eternal. Fiddling while Rome burns. A pleasure in ideas that have no real application.

The recognition that without vigilance and risk, peace can lead to stagnation  
and collapse.  
• ♆ ▲ The imagination applied to ensuring the understanding of achieved values. A sense of boredom overcome through the imagination.  
• ♂ ▽ The resorting to factionalism to satisfy the ego’s need for action. Provoking with ideas to escape boredom.

The serendipity of being in the right place at the right time.  
• C ▲ The nourishment derived from being with those who share the same goals and aspirations. The gift of finding those who will value your ideas.  
• ♂ ▽ The fear of anonymity. The sense that no one will value your ideas.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ω U+03C9 omega; ♂ U+2642 Mars; “C” \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Unclear at this resolution: detriment glyph for line 6\.

Notes:  
Degree spans match Gate 11’s Sagittarius band from 22° 37′ 30″ to 28° 15′ 00″. Blue page number “11”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 12 — The Gate of Caution

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 12 page. Header lists Channel 12-22 “Openness,” Harmonic Gate 22 “Grace,” Center Throat, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic span in Gemini. One paragraph describes social caution, mood-based expression, and the role of this Throat gate. Three mandalas at the bottom are captioned with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Caution.” A compact specification block beneath the title shows channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Gemini-to-Gemini degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about social caution and mood-dependent articulation. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A large gray “12” sits at the upper left; a green Gemini glyph appears at the upper right; a small blue “12” is near the lower corners. Side index at left reads “45 ◁ 12 ▷ 15.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Caution

Channel 12-22 Openness  
The design of the social being  
Harmonic Gate 22 Grace  
Center Throat  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 22° 37′ 30″ ♊ – 28° 15′ 00″ ♊ \[I\]

Powerful emotions and unique insights aren’t for everyone. Standstill is the only gate in the Throat that is specifi-  
cally about not doing. It is a gate of social caution, of wariness about the collective. This is one of the three gates  
(40,33) of Aloneness. Since this is the gate of the mechanical expression of the Emoting Stream, its caution is  
naturally rooted in its mood. ‘I try’ is its voice but it is always a maybe, depending on the mood, the spirit. The indi-  
vidual always faces resistance and it is natural to be hesitant about social interaction. This gate is a destiny of  
‘familiarity bringing contempt’. The individual has its greatest impact as a stranger of consequence; releasing its  
awareness in the proper spirit and then withdrawing. As a Throat mechanic, this gate has the capacity to be ex-  
tremely articulate (when it’s in the mood). The power of Cultural Mutation is the mystery of Standstill.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF EDEN (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF ARTICULATION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF EDUCATION

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Gemini ♊ U+264A

Notes:  
Yellow baseline rule near footer; side index “45 ◁ 12 ▷ 15”; page number “12” in blue.

## Gate 12 — STANDSTILL, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 12 “STANDSTILL” lines page with six line themes in Gemini degree ranges, each showing paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) with small planetary glyphs.

Long description:  
Heading reads “STANDSTILL.” A gold subtitle explains restraint, meditation, and inaction in the face of temptation. The left margin lists lines 6 to 1 with degree spans. The main column presents a blue subheading for each line followed by two items: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “12” appears at top right; a blue page number “12” is near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Gemini):  
6 Metamorphosis — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
5 The pragmatist — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″  
4 The prophet — 25° 26′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
3 Confession — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 26′ 15″  
2 Purification — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
1 The monk — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″

Main text:  
STANDSTILL

THE QUALITY OF RESTRAINT AND THE IMPORTANCE OF MEDITATION AND  
INACTION IN CONFRONTING TEMPTATION.

Faith in, and energy applied towards change and the emergence from stand-  
still.  
• ☉ ▲ A creative transcendence that when connected to the Harmonic Gate 22, through the channel of Openness, leads to successful mutation and the emergence of a new social form. The capacity to mutate and express new social forms.  
• ⊕ ▽ A retrograde metamorphosis that has evolved a perfected adaption to standstill. The perfected adaption to caution that can accept social limitations.

The success of restraint lies in not abandoning the lessons learnt when the  
phase ends.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ Light is always conscious of darkness. Caution as an expression of social experience.  
• ♂ ▽ The tendency to remember only the most painful lessons learnt. Caution which is conditioned by the most painful social experiences.

The ability to foresee and plan for the end of standstill.  
• ⊕ ▲ The rousing of the stagnant for communal preparation. The ability to foresee and express the need for social interaction and an end to caution.  
• ☿ ▽ The voice in the wilderness. The expressed need for social interaction that falls on deaf ears.

The process of self-analysis.  
• ♆ ▲ The recognition of inadequacies and the purging of unjustified vanities. The expression of inadequacies in social interaction that leads to self-analysis and caution.  
• ♂ ▽ A perverse and often exaggerated self-hatred. Inadequacies in social interaction that lead to the expression of self-hatred.

Rigorous withdrawal from negative influences.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ The discipline to maintain a pure state. The expression of disciplined social caution.  
• ☿ ▽ The boredom that arises out of lack of stimulation. Caution that manifests boredom and the expressed desire for stimulus.

Withdrawal that can only be maintained with communal support.  
• ♀ ▲ The beauty and harmony possible beyond the reach of temptation. The expression of social withdrawal and its value when supported by others.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ Simon the Stylite, total and often absurd withdrawal. The absurd expression of social caution and extreme withdrawal from emotional contact.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; ♀ U+2640 Venus.  
Unclear at this resolution: exaltation glyph for line 5; exaltation glyph for line 2; detriment glyph for line 1\.

Notes:  
Degree spans match Gate 12’s Gemini band (22° 37′ 30″ → 28° 15′ 00″). Blue page number “12”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 13 — The Gate of the Listener

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 13 page. Header lists Channel 33-13 “The Prodigal” with the note “The design of the witness,” Harmonic Gate 33 “Retreat,” Center G, Circuit Sensing, and an astrologic span in Aquarius. One paragraph explains openness and listening at the end of the Abstract cycle. Three mandalas at the bottom are captioned with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of the Listener.” A compact specification block beneath the title gives the channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an Aquarius-to-Aquarius degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph describing the listener’s role in completing the Abstract process and accumulating memory at cycle’s end. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative green Aquarius glyph appears at upper right; a large gray “13” stands at upper left; a small blue “13” sits near the lower corners. Side index at left shows neighboring gates around the rim.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Listener

Channel 33-13 The Prodigal  
The design of the witness  
Harmonic Gate 33 Retreat  
Center G  
Circuit Sensing  
Astrologic Position 13° 15′ 00″ ♒ – 18° 52′ 30″ ♒

Where the logic process demands a Role for the Self, the abstract demands that the Self listens. This gate is the  
openness of the Self in interaction. It is a gate of the Cross of the Sphinx and manifests direction. In the logic  
process, direction is a projection through which the collective is pointed towards the possibilities of the future. The  
abstract process is direction through reflection and points out the experience of the past. This is a gate of the  
listener. This gate will always attract others who will come and share their experiences. This is the channel of the  
witness and the completion of the Abstract process. It is the point at the end of a cycle. Here, the knowledge of  
the cycle is accumulated as memory. There is also magic in this gate for it is the gate of the Hearer of Secrets.  
This is the Role of openness.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE SPHINX  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF LISTENING  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF MASKS

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Aquarius ♒ U+2652

Notes:  
Yellow baseline rule near footer; side index shows neighboring gates (e.g., “19 ◁ 13 ▷ 49”).
