## Gate 13 — THE FELLOWSHIP OF MAN

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 13 “THE FELLOWSHIP OF MAN” lines page with six line themes in Aquarius, each with paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) and small glyphs.

Long description:  
The heading reads “THE FELLOWSHIP OF MAN.” A gold subtitle states: “UNIVERSAL IDEAS AND VALUES IN AN ORDERED FRAMEWORK WHICH INSPIRES HUMANISTIC COOPERATION.” The left margin lists the six lines from 6 to 1 with their degree spans. For each line, a blue subheading is followed by two bullets: exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “13” appears at the top right; a blue page number “13” is near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aquarius):  
6 The optimist — 17° 56′ 15″ – 18° 52′ 30″  
5 The saviour — 17° 00′ 00″ – 17° 56′ 15″  
4 Fatigue — 16° 03′ 45″ – 17° 00′ 00″  
3 Pessimism — 15° 07′ 30″ – 16° 03′ 45″  
2 Bigotry — 14° 11′ 15″ – 15° 07′ 30″  
1 Empathy — 13° 15′ 00″ – 14° 11′ 15″

Main text:  
THE FELLOWSHIP OF MAN

UNIVERSAL IDEAS AND VALUES IN AN ORDERED FRAMEWORK WHICH IN-  
SPIRES HUMANISTIC COOPERATION.

The ability to accept any limited interaction as a necessary step towards greater union.  
• ♂ ▲ The energy to persevere. Unlimited hope. The hope that openness will lead to better relationships.  
• ☿ ▽ Naivete. The translation of mutual interest into universality. The belief that mutual interests can be projected on others.

The ability to overcome all obstacles for the betterment of humanity.  
• Ψ ▲ The charismatic genius who can find a role for everyone. The listener that has a gift for finding a role for others.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ The able administrator. Given the extreme positive nature of this position, mundane application is rare. The listener whose gift for finding a role for others is practical and suited to administration.

The point of exhaustion eventually reached when one is too tired to fight.  
• ω ▲ \[text partly illegible at this resolution\] Openness … and the need for silence.  
• ♀ ▽ Emotional exhaustion. Appeasement and withdrawal. A role where openness is a vulnerability.

The belief that what is best can never be achieved.  
• ⊕ ▲ A lack of trust that can only be transformed through concrete evidence. Openness that is conditioned by suspicion and seeks evidence.  
• ♀ ▽ Pessimism exalted to an art form, where as art, it may have the opposite effect. Satire. Where the rightness of suspicion can inspire satire.

The risk, always present, that fellowship can only exist for a particular type, whether racial, religious, national or intellectual.  
• C ▲ Tolerance as the least offensive manifestation of bigotry. A role of openness through tolerance.  
• ☉ ▽ The obsessive belief that the highest ideals cannot be embraced by the lowest forms. An extremely difficult position, where even the highest ideals provide rationalization for hatred. A role of openness so narrow that there is practically no one worth listening to.

The ability to relate and commune with everyone with equanimity.  
• ♀ ▲ Harmony through affection. A role of openness in listening to others with affection.  
• C ▽ The politician kissing babies. An openness that is never free of motives.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ☿ U+263F Mercury; Ψ U+03A8 psi; ω U+03C9 omega; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ☉ U+2609 Sun; C \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Unclear glyphs: line-5 detriment symbol not legible at this resolution.

Notes:  
Degree spans match Gate 13’s Aquarius band (13° 15′ 00″ → 18° 52′ 30″). Blue page number “13”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 14 — The Gate of Power Skills

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 14 page. Header lists Channel 2-14 “The Beat,” Harmonic Gate 2 “The Receptive,” Center Sacral, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic span from late Scorpio into early Sagittarius. One paragraph explains Gate 14 as the power that fuels direction, emphasizing that fuel determines direction. Three small mandalas at the bottom are captioned with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Power Skills.” A compact spec block beneath the title details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Scorpio→Sagittarius degree range. A thin rule separates the header from one explanatory paragraph about sacral fuel empowering the Self and driving the Sphinx through the Throat. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Scorpio glyph sits in the header area; a large gray “14” appears at upper left; a blue page number “14” is near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the footer. Left gutter shows “43 ◁ 14 ▷ 34.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Power Skills

Channel 2-14 The Beat  
The design of being the keeper of keys  
Harmonic Gate 2 The Receptive  
Center Sacral  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 24° 30′ 00″ ♏ – 00° 07′ 30″ ♐

This is the fertile power of the Sacral center at its most exalted. The channels between the G center and the Sacral  
are Tantric channels, where the sacral energy is available as fuel to empower the Self. The ultimate expression,  
however, always lies through the Throat. The 14th gate, is the power that drives the Sphinx. The ‘Driver’ the Prime  
Magnetic Monopole, may know the direction but if there is no key to turn on the motor, there is nowhere for the  
vehicle to go. Changes in Human Direction are determined by mutation in the Format energy and the consequent  
mutation of the ‘Drivers’ fuel. Fuel determines direction\! A vegetarian and a carnivore have different directions in  
life.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF CONTAGEN (4)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF EMPOWERING  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF UNCERTAINTY (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
♏ U+264F Scorpio, ♐ U+2650 Sagittarius

Notes:  
Yellow baseline near footer; side index “43 ◁ 14 ▷ 34”; page number “14” in blue; three mandalas along the bottom row.

## Gate 14 — POSSESSION IN GREAT MEASURE, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 14 “POSSESSION IN GREAT MEASURE” lines page with six line themes and paired notes (▲ exaltation, ▽ detriment), covering late Scorpio to early Sagittarius.

Long description:  
Title “POSSESSION IN GREAT MEASURE.” Gold subtitle: “THE ACCUMULATION AND RETENTION OF POWER THROUGH SKILLED INTERACTION, COUPLING GRACE WITH CONTROL.” Left margin lists lines 6→1 with degree ranges. The main column gives a blue subheading for each line followed by two bullets (▲ / ▽) prefixed by a small glyph (Sun, Earth, Venus, Mercury, Mars, etc). A large gray “14” sits top-right; blue page number “14” at the lower corners; a thin yellow rule near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; italics preserve in-page emphasis; bracketed notes mark unreadable spots)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges):  
6 Humility — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
5 Arrogance — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″  
4 Security — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
3 Service — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″  
2 Management — 25° 26′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
1 Money isn’t everything — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 26′ 15″

Main text:  
POSSESSION IN GREAT MEASURE

THE ACCUMULATION AND RETENTION OF POWER THROUGH SKILLED IN-  
TERACTION, COUPLING GRACE WITH CONTROL.

Wealth and power at its most exalted.  
• ☉ ▲ The enlightened recognition that material success is God’s will. Spirituality as the key to acceptance and the source of power.  
• ⊕ ▽ \[Text partly illegible\] All manifestations of this position are essentially positive… Existentialism as the key to acceptance and the source of power. \[last clause partly illegible\]

The ever present risk inherent in positions of power.  
• ☉ ▲ Innate dignity. Innate dignity that is a key to power.  
• ♀ ▽ A dissatisfaction with the gifts of others that creates feelings of superiority. \[second sentence partly illegible\]

The concentration on establishing a strong foundation.  
• C ▲ Protection from assault. The key to power lies in developing skills to ensure a strong foundation.  
• ♂ ▽ Overconfidence in meeting the challenge of competition that may threaten the very basis of security. Without the proper skills the inability to guarantee security.

The utilization of talent and wealth for the highest good.  
• ⊕ ▲ Selfless contribution to society. The key to power lies in selfless contribution to others.  
• Ψ ▽ Greed and the self-destruction of moral fibre. The power of selfishness to fuel greed.

The wisdom that investing in expertise brings rewards.  
• 2 ▲ Expansion. The ability to delegate responsibility. The key to power lies in not trying to be and to do everything alone.  
• ♂ ▽ The vanity to be one’s own best expert. The key to power is doing everything individually.

The recognition that wealth has its own problems.  
• 2 ▲ The lust for lucre tempered by higher principles. The key to manifesting power is higher principles.  
• ☿ ▽ The delusion that you can throw money at problems. Energy alone can never be the key.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ☉ Sun U+2609; ⊕ Earth U+2295; ♀ Venus U+2640; ☿ Mercury U+263F; ♂ Mars U+2642; Ψ psi U+03A8; “C” \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Unclear at this resolution: line-6 detriment body text (part), line-5 detriment second sentence (part).

Notes:  
Gate 14 lines span Scorpio → Sagittarius per the header page. Blue page number “14”; thin yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 15 — The Gate of Extremes

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 15 page. Header lists Channel 15-5 “Rhythm,” Harmonic Gate 5 “Waiting,” Center G, Circuit Understanding, and an astrologic span from late Gemini into early Cancer. One paragraph explains living out extremes in rhythm and why the 5/15 electro-magnetic connection can be difficult. Three mandalas at the bottom are captioned with the Vessel of Love, Extremes, and Prevention crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Extremes.” A compact specification block beneath the title details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and the degree range (Gemini to Cancer). A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A green Gemini glyph appears at upper right; a large gray “15” at upper left; a small blue “15” near the lower corners. Side index at left reads “12 ◁ 15 ▷ 52.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Extremes

Channel 15-5 Rhythm  
The design of being in the flow  
Harmonic Gate 5 Waiting  
Center G  
Circuit Understanding  
Astrologic Position 28° 15′ 00″ ♊ \[I\] – 03° 52′ 30″ ♋

The 15th gate is part of the Cross of the Vessel. It is the gate of the ‘Aura’ and is the Self that is magnetic. The  
Love of this gate is for Humanity. This channel defined always determines the rhythm of the environment. Modesty  
in the traditional sense is always a matter of balancing extremes. Through this gate, the possible extremes are  
lived out. People who are busy and not, sleep long and short, eat early, eat late; in other words, people who will  
live out extremes in their rhythm. In Design Analysis, in working with composites, there is a type of connection  
called Electro-Magnetic. In this connection each partner activates the opposite gate of a channel forming a defini-  
tion. This is attraction / repulsion, love / hate, a basic relationship dynamic. However, in three cases (45/21, 35/36  
and 5/15) this type of connection is very difficult. The 15th gate is uncomfortable with a fixed pattern and the 5th  
gate destabilized by extreme rhythms.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE VESSEL OF LOVE (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF EXTREMES  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF PREVENTION

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
♊ U+264A Gemini, ♋ U+264B Cancer

Notes:  
Yellow baseline rule near footer; side index “12 ◁ 15 ▷ 52”; page number “15” in blue.

## Gate 15 — MODESTY

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 15 lines page titled “MODESTY,” showing six themes with degree ranges and paired notes for exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽).

Long description:  
The page heading is “MODESTY.” A gold subtitle reads: “THE QUALITY OF BEHAVIOUR WHICH EXPRESSES THE PROPER BALANCE BETWEEN EXTREMES.” Along the left margin the six lines (6→1) list names and degree spans. For each line the main column gives a blue subheading followed by two bullets, an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each preceded by a small glyph. A large gray “15” sits at the top right; a blue “15” appears at the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads preserved; italics mark in-page emphasis):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges):  
6 Self-defense — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
5 Sensitivity — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″  
4 The wallflower — 01° 03′ 45″ – 02° 00′ 00″  
3 Ego inflation — 00° 07′ 30″ – 01° 03′ 45″  
2 Influence — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
1 Duty — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″

Main text:

MODESTY

THE QUALITY OF BEHAVIOUR WHICH EXPRESSES THE PROPER BALANCE  
BETWEEN EXTREMES.

Modesty that is never confused with weakness.  
• ω ▲ Constant reexamination to weed out the weakest aspect. The power of the Self in exploring the extremes to find the weakest point.  
• ♀ ▽ A tendency to use harmony as a weapon in problem situations rather than focusing on the root causes. The power of the Self to ignore the weakest point in favour of harmony.

The ability to sense when otherwise balanced behaviour must be adjusted to  
meet the requirements of changing environment.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ The power to grow. The capacity of the Self to grow through experiencing the extremes.  
• ω ▽ The tendency to overcompensate. The drive of the Self to overcompensate and disturb the flow.

Modesty as a shield against exposure of inadequacy.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ A genuine form that may or may not mask inadequacies. The uncomfortableness of the Self when it is out of the flow.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ An ultimately weak defense leading to exposure and humiliation. Extremism that keeps the Self out of the flow.

The risk that modesty once recognized will self-destruct.  
• ⊕ ▲ Where the otherwise negative contrived modesty is here reinforced by recognition and maintained as an effective strategy. The extremism of the Self as strategy to control the flow.  
• ☿ ▽ The “I told you so” mentality. The capacity of the Self to point out the extremes of others.

Modesty and right action result in enduring standards.  
• ☉ ▲ Modesty and right action result in enduring standards. The capacity of the Self to accept its extreme nature as correct.  
• ☉ ▽ Where the Sun’s actions are natural, the Earth’s are contrived, though given the power of this position, the same effect can be expected. The capacity to use the extreme nature of the Self to influence others.

The ability to confront any challenge without expectations.  
• ♀ ▲ Harmonic relationships which give support for the fulfillment of any task. The capacity of the Self to confront any challenge through extreme and harmonic relationships.  
• ♂ ▽ Alienation engendered through exaggerated claims. The capacity of the Self to alienate others through extremes.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ω U+03C9; ♀ U+2640; ⊕ U+2295; ☿ U+263F; ☉ U+2609; ♂ U+2642.  
Unclear at this resolution: the “2-like” symbol used on some bullets (lines 5 and 4).

Notes:  
Degree spans align with Gate 15’s header (Gemini 28° 15′ 00″ → Cancer 03° 52′ 30″). Yellow baseline near footer; large gray “15” at top right.

# Gate 16 — The Gate of Skills

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 16 page. Header lists Channel 16-48 “The Wave Length,” Harmonic Gate 48 “The Well,” Center Throat, Circuit Understanding, and a Gemini degree span. One paragraph explains talent as repetition and experimentation; 16 projects “I experiment.” Three mandalas at the bottom are captioned Planning (2), Experimentation, and Identification.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Skills.” A compact specification block details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an astrologic position range within Gemini (quarter mark “I”). A thin rule separates the header from a single paragraph about skills, repetition, and the 16/48 dynamic. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. Large gray “16” at upper left; green Gemini glyph at upper right; blue page number “16” near the lower margin; side index shows “20 ◁ 16 ▷ 35.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Skills

Channel 16-48 The Wave Length  
A design of talent  
Harmonic Gate 48 The Well  
Center Throat  
Circuit Understanding  
Astrologic Position 05° 45′ 00″ ♊ – 11° 22′ 30″ ♊ \[I\]

Talent by its very design is so often dependent on others for manifestation. The defining of the Spleen to the  
Throat, allows only for verbal manifestation. Every gate of the Throat center has a voice. The 16th gate says, ‘I  
experiment’. Since the Understanding Circuit is about patterns and focus, it makes a clear statement about the  
quality of talent being dependent on repetition. Constant experimentation, over and over, goaded by the critical  
capacity, to find the ‘perfect’ expression. The 16th gate is the gate of Skills, the ancient Chinese Hexagram of Mu-  
sic, Dance and the Arts. However, this gate is not specifically about the fine Arts, inherent in its capacity is skills  
for living; a talent for life. One of the keys to Design Analysis is to recognize that harmonic gates are always pro-  
jecting each other’s attributes on the other. The 48 is always self-critical about not having enough skills, and the  
16 not enough depth and more complex, the 48 will confuse skills with depth and the 16, vice versa. This was the  
relationship of John Lennon (48) and Paul McCartney (16).

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF PLANNING (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF EXPERIMENTATION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF IDENTIFICATION

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Gemini ♊ U+264A

Notes:  
Side index “20 ◁ 16 ▷ 35.” Blue page number “16.” Yellow baseline near footer.

## Gate 16 — ENTHUSIASM

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 16 “ENTHUSIASM” lines page with six line themes in Gemini and paired notes per line marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment), each preceded by a small glyph.

Long description:  
Heading reads “ENTHUSIASM.” A gold subtitle states: “THE GREAT ART OF ENRICHING LIFE BY THE HARMONIC CHANNELLING OF ENERGY.” The left margin lists lines 6 to 1 with degree spans. For each line, a blue subheading is followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽). A large gray “16” appears at the top right; a blue “16” near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs along the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve in-page emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Gemini):  
6 Gullibility — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
5 The grinch — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″  
4 The leader — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
3 Independence — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″  
2 The cynic — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
1 Delusion — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″

Main text:  
ENTHUSIASM

THE GREAT ART OF ENRICHING LIFE BY THE HARMONIC CHANNELLING OF  
ENERGY.

The susceptibility to propaganda.  
• Ψ ▲ The ability to experience, examine and then reject misleading enthusiasm. The talent to assess the expression of others.  
• \[unclear glyph\] ▽ The same principle but where Neptune will destroy and then seek new forms, Jupiter will painfully withdraw. Its enthusiasm for social structures permanently prejudiced. The failure to assess the expression of others.

The refusal to share in enthusiasm.  
• ω ▲ The power to avoid enthusiasm for the sole purpose of being converted. As with Dickens’ Scrooge, eventual conversion leads to greater and more enduring enthusiasm. A lack of confidence in the expression of skills that needs the encouragement of others.  
• C ▽ The perverse feeling that sharing in enthusiasm hampers individual development. Why should I be happy when … etc. A lack of confidence in the value of encouraging others.

Genuine and sincere support and recognition of others.  
• \[unclear glyph\] ▲ Enthusiasm for/and service to higher goals. The skill to recognize and support the talents of others.  
• ♂ ▽ The demagogue. The refusal to support or recognize the talents of others.

Self-generating and sustaining enthusiasm.  
• C ▲ The proper timing to maintain rhythm and avoid deflation. The independent skill and possible talent to express proper timing and rhythm.  
• ♂ ▽ The child whose overconfidence may lead to frustration and the ensuing dependence on others to regenerate enthusiasm; thus creating an unnecessary reliance. The need to have others confirm one’s skills or talent.

The sharpness to burst bubbles.  
• ☉ ▲ Self-reliance and the skill to judge objectively any claim regardless of rhetoric. The expression of the skill to judge objectively.  
• ♀ ▽ The compulsive cynic, whose very cynicism is a source of enthusiasm. Objectivity expressed through cynicism.

False enthusiasm.  
• ⊕ ▲ The day dreamer. The expression of talent through daydreaming.  
• ☿ ▽ The public communication of inevitably unrealized claims. The tendency to express fantasy as fact.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; Ψ U+03A8; ω U+03C9; C \= stylized Moon (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); ♂ U+2642; ☉ U+2609; ♀ U+2640; ☿ U+263F; ⊕ U+2295.  
Unclear: a “2-like” symbol appears for line 6 detriment and line 4 exaltation.

Notes:  
Degree spans match Gate 16’s Gemini band (05° 45′ 00″ → 11° 22′ 30″). Blue page number “16”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 17 — The Gate of Opinions

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 17 page. Header lists Channel 17-62 “Acceptance” with the note “A design of an organizational being,” Harmonic Gate 62 “Preponderance of the Small,” Center Ajna, Circuit Understanding, and an astrologic span in Aries. One paragraph explains Opinion as the form of the concept and links the 17th gate with the right eye. Three mandalas at the bottom are captioned with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Opinions.” A compact specification block beneath the title gives channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an Aries-to-Aries degree range. A thin rule separates the header from one explanatory paragraph about organizing the Formula and the role of the right eye in visualization and projection. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A large gray “17” stands at upper left; a magenta Aries glyph appears at upper right; a small blue “17” sits near the lower corners. Side index at left reads “24 ◁ 17 ▷ 21.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Opinions

Channel 17-62 Acceptance  
A design of an organizational being  
Harmonic Gate 62 Preponderance of the Small  
Center Ajna  
Circuit Understanding  
Astrologic Position 03° 52′ 30″ ♈ – 09° 30′ 00″ ♈

The possibility of being able to organize the Formula, the understanding, and prepare it for expression. This is the  
gate of the student and the teacher. It is the formula brought into focus. This is the gate of Opinion. The Circuit  
of Understanding demands proof and opinions must have a logical foundation to withstand testing and criticism.  
This by the way isn’t to say whether these opinions, logical or not, are correct. Facts are regularly used to support  
lies and lies are proffered as formulas. This is about the form of the concept. The Collective mental possibilities,  
gates 11 and 17, are associated in our biology with the Eyes. The 17th gate is the right Eye. It is the eye which  
sees clearly in the Now. It is the best eye for experimentation. The left eye never sees everything at once\! These  
mental concepts are about visualization. The right eye seeks the fixed pattern and projects out of this pattern.  
Opinion is like a crystal ball. Fixed visually on the pattern in the now and projecting into the future. By design, the  
question (63), the answer (4) and the Opinion have no voice much less any power to act. The mind is far away  
from a motor.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF SERVICE  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF OPINIONS  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF UPHEAVAL

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Aries ♈ U+2648

Notes:  
Yellow baseline near footer; side index “24 ◁ 17 ▷ 21”; blue page number “17”.

## Gate 17 — FOLLOWING, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 17 “FOLLOWING” lines page showing six themes with Aries degree ranges and paired notes per line marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment).

Long description:  
Heading reads “FOLLOWING.” A gold subtitle states: “THE ANCIENT LAW THAT THOSE WHO WISH TO RULE MUST KNOW HOW TO SERVE.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with degree spans. Each line has a blue subheading and two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each preceded by a small glyph. A large gray “17” sits at the top right; a blue page number “17” appears near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aries):  
6 The bodhisattva — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
5 No human is an island — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″  
4 The personnel manager — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
3 Understanding — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″  
2 Discrimination — 04° 48′ 45″ – 05° 45′ 00″  
1 Openness — 03° 52′ 30″ – 04° 48′ 45″

Main text:  
FOLLOWING

THE ANCIENT LAW THAT THOSE WHO WISH TO RULE MUST KNOW HOW  
TO SERVE.

Perfected following, one with and the same as, perfected leading.  
• C ▲ The great nurturer. The nature of this position is always positive. The possibility in understanding the nature of interdependency to express opinions of value to others.  
• 2 ▽ The tendency on the perfected path it is a straight line that ends rather than a circle. Where understanding is achieved, the possibility of having the opinion that there is nothing else to learn.

The recognition, however understood that there is no end to hierarchies.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ The ultimate creative expression of interdependency, whether as God’s will or global synthesis. Opinions that can express the value of being organized whether mundane or spiritual.  
• ♂ ▽ The arrogance, despite all the evidence to claim, that the buck stops here\! The possibility of opinions that refuse to see the value of being organized.

The ability to probe and discover the underlying motivation and resources of those who wish to follow.  
• ω ▲ The ability to probe and discover the underlying motivation and resources of those who wish to follow. The possibility of opinions based on the understanding of others.  
• 2 ▽ The overgenerous and often misguided acceptance of would-be followers, often with disastrous results. The possibility of opinions attracting others.

The awareness that the best road is not necessarily the most interesting.  
• ω ▲ The following of the best road provides the experience necessary to meet the challenge of the road’s end. The understanding that the best opinions are grounded in detail.  
• ⊕ ▽ The taking of short cuts. One may get to the end quicker, but essential experience will be missing. The possibility of skipping details and limiting the value of the opinion.

The benefit of associations based on the highest values.  
• ☉ ▲ The successful achievement of purpose through proper alignment. The possibility to develop opinions through relationships.  
• C ▽ An overactive discrimination that leaves one virtually alone. The possibility of being opinionated at the expense of relationships.

The ability to confront any challenge without expectations.  
• ♂ ▲ The energy to sustain a broad spectrum of stimuli. The possibility of having many opinions.  
• ♀ ▽ A tendency to limit openness to aesthetically pleasing stimuli. The possibility to limit opinions to what is pleasing.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; C \= stylized Moon (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); ♂ U+2642; ♀ U+2640; ω U+03C9; ⊕ U+2295.  
Unclear at this resolution: the exaltation glyph for line 5 (first bullet); line 6 detriment uses the “2-like” symbol seen elsewhere in this edition.

Notes:  
Degree spans align with Gate 17’s Aries band (03° 52′ 30″ → 09° 30′ 00″). Blue page number “17”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 18 — The Gate of Correction

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
 Alt text:  
 Gate 18 header page. Channel 18–58 “Judgment” (a design of insatiability), Harmonic Gate 58 “The Joyous,” Center Spleen, Circuit Understanding (Logic). Astrologic span **03° 52′ 30″ – 09° 30′ 00″ ♎**. The page explains Gate 18 as the focus of **judgment/dissatisfaction**—the imprinting of standards through parental conditioning and the lifelong drive to **correct** and **improve**; from this critique and comparison is born the Collective’s **Taste**.

Long description:  
 Gate 18 is “Work on what has been spoilt.” It’s where we receive our **deepest conditioning** from parents—sometimes called the **Oedipus/Electra** gate (mother/son, father/daughter dynamics). This isn’t a negative label: it’s **essential learning**, the **gate of Correction**, where standards are **imprinted** generation to generation. These conditioned standards become the basis for **comparison** applied to everything—lovers, work, even oneself. The vitality of 18 **denies resignation**: correction and improvement are **lifelong processes**. Without dissatisfaction and the skill-honing drive for **perfection**, there is no **Understanding**; here the Collective’s **Taste** takes form.

Figures (bottom captions):

* THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF SERVICE (3)

* THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF CORRECTION

* THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF UPHEAVAL (2)

## Gate 18 — WORK ON WHAT HAS BEEN SPOILT

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 18 lines page titled “WORK ON WHAT HAS BEEN SPOILT,” with six themes and paired notes (▲ exaltation, ▽ detriment). Emphasis on correction, diagnosis, and human rights.

Long description:  
The page heading reads “WORK ON WHAT HAS BEEN SPOILT.” A gold subtitle states: “THE VIGILANCE AND DETERMINATION TO UPHOLD AND DEFEND BASIC AND FUNDAMENTAL HUMAN RIGHTS.” The left margin lists lines 6 → 1 with degree spans. For each line, a blue subheading is followed by two bullets, an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “18” sits at the top right; a blue page number “18” is near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve in-page emphasis):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges):  
6 Buddhahood — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
5 Therapy — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″  
4 The incompetent — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
3 The zealot — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″  
2 Terminal disease — 04° 48′ 45″ – 05° 45′ 00″  
1 Conservatism — 03° 52′ 30″ – 04° 48′ 45″

Main text:  
WORK ON WHAT HAS BEEN SPOILT

THE VIGILANCE AND DETERMINATION TO UPHOLD AND DEFEND BASIC AND  
FUNDAMENTAL HUMAN RIGHTS.

The perfected form.  
• ♂ ▲ The Buddha state of the eternal child and the energy to find new horizons to avoid stasis. The potential of the perfected form through correction.  
• C ▽ The mundane application of the above. The ability to tap public opinion and share methodology. The potential to share the values of the correction with others.

The strength to recognize a problem and to accept that it is beyond one’s power to solve it alone.  
• 2 ▲ The wisdom to both seek and provide guidance. The potential for correction and judgment through relationships.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ The mental patient. Chronic instability and potential madness. Where relationships cannot assist in correction the potential of mental instability.

Difficulties as a result of inadequacies that cannot be resolved because of inadequacies.  
• ⊕ ▲ Given this negative position, survival through suffering. The inability to correct and its potential for suffering.  
• ☿ ▽ Indecision and anxiety and no escape from misfortune. The demands of correction and its potential to generate anxiety.

The energetic obsession to clean house.  
• ♆ ▲ The dissolution of old forms at an acceptable price. An obsession with correction and its critical potential.  
• 2 ▽ Rigid judgementation that creates as many problems as it solves. An obsession with correction that does not bring satisfaction.

The recognition that what has been spoilt is irreversible.  
• ω ▲ Acceptance and strength derived from a faith in spiritual regeneration. The acceptance that there is no potential for correction.  
• C ▽ The futile raging against the wind. The refusal to accept that there is no potential for correction.

The adherence to traditional patterns despite and/or in spite of changing circumstances.  
• ⊕ ▲ Gradual modification to avoid eventual upheaval. The potential to correct through gradual modification of judgments.  
• 2 ▽ The Patriarch whose rigidity guarantees deterioration. The potential to refuse to correct.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♂ U+2642 Mars; C \= stylized Moon mark (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; ω U+03C9 omega.  
Unclear at this resolution: detriment glyph on line 5; the “2-like” symbol used on lines 5, 3 and 1\.

Notes:  
Degree spans match the gate band shown on this page. Blue page number “18”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 19 — The Gate of Wanting

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 19 page. Header lists Channel 49-19 “Synthesis,” Harmonic Gate 49 “Revolution,” Center Root, Circuit Ego, and an astrologic span in Aquarius. One paragraph explains Gate 19 as social need and support for the community, with a note about food and keeping the “refrigerator full.”

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Wanting.” A compact specification block beneath the title shows the channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and Aquarius-to-Aquarius degree range with a quarter mark. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about the Ego (Tribal) circuit, sensitivity, community access, revolution, and biological need. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A large gray “19” stands at upper left; a green Aquarius glyph appears at upper right; a blue page number “19” sits near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs above the footer. Side index at left shows neighboring gates around the rim.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Wanting

Channel 49-19 Synthesis  
A design of being sensitive  
Harmonic Gate 49 Revolution  
Center Root  
Circuit Ego  
Astrologic Position 07° 37′ 30″ ♒ – 13° 15′ 00″ ♒ \[I\]

The Ego Circuit, the circuit of the material Plane is not to be underestimated, nor is it specifically mundane. This is  
the Tribal circuitry, where the keynote is ‘support’. This is the circuit of our communities and their survival. This  
channel of Synthesis, is one of the three Mystical Channels (51/25, 10/20) and the only one that is potentially  
aware and not strictly mechanical. This gate is the fuel of the sensitivity process. At its most mundane, it is hardly  
spiritual. It is potentially over-sensitive and easily disturbed. The 19th gate fuels our social needs. It fuels the drive  
to make sure that the community exists and that there is a place within the community for this energy. It is not  
about wanting or needing ‘someone’ specifically. It is the energy to support and the need to have the support of  
the community available. It is about having access and not be socially restricted. It is the force which drives all  
revolutions. At a biological level, it is about food. To avoid over-sensitivity, it’s always necessary for the 19 to have  
a full refrigerator\!

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE FOUR WAYS (4)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF NEED  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF REFINEMENT (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Aquarius ♒ U+2652

Notes:  
Side index: “41 ◁ 19 ▷ 13.” Three mandalas along the bottom row; yellow baseline rule near footer.

## Gate 19 — APPROACH

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 19 “APPROACH” lines page with six themes in Aquarius degree ranges, each showing paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) with small glyphs.

Long description:  
The heading reads “APPROACH.” A gold subtitle states: “THAT ALL THINGS ARE INTERRELATED IS APPARENT AND MANIFESTED THROUGH THE ACTION OF APPROACH.” The left margin lists lines 6 to 1 with their degree spans. For each line, a blue subheading is followed by two bullets, an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “19” appears at the top right; a blue page number “19” is printed near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve in-page emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aquarius):  
6 The recluse — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
5 Sacrifice — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″  
4 The team player — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
3 Dedication — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″  
2 Service — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
1 Interdependence — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″

Main text:  
APPROACH

THAT ALL THINGS ARE INTERRELATED IS APPARENT AND MANIFESTED  
THROUGH THE ACTION OF APPROACH.

The avoidance of contact in general but not exclusively.  
• 2 ▲ The fool on the hill, the sage, that if you can find him, will talk to you. The energy which generally fuels avoidance.  
• ♂ ▽ The sulking child. The self-imposed exile that will only end when it attracts appropriate and soothing reaction. Oversensitivity to rejection that fuels avoidance.

The need to limit personal potential in order to achieve a larger goal.  
• ⊕ ▲ The self-restraint fundamental to such a nature. Energy to keep one’s sensitivities restrained.  
• 2 ▽ A tendency in sacrifice to condescend. Sacrifice can fuel a lack of sensitivity.

Individual approach which attracts and accepts cooperation.  
• ♂ ▲ The power and energy for outward activity and the ability to accept others as long as they can keep up. A driving force that can benefit the whole group. The energy to seek out and exalt in the company of others.  
• ♀ ▽ Attractive and cooperative but a tendency to dissatisfaction with the contribution of others. A sensitivity energized by the limitation of others.

Receptivity to approach can only be maintained through vigilance.  
• ♀ ▲ The natural ease with which communion is maintained. Sensitivity and ease fueled by acceptance by others.  
• C ▽ A tendency to moodiness that may lead to carelessness. The need to be wanted hampered by oversensitivity.

The dedication of personal resources as a result of external contact.  
• 2 ▲ Dedication and service to the highest values. The energy to want to be of service.  
• ☿♀ ▽ Protracted indecision, but given the nature of this position, eventual compliance. The need to be wanted that will eventually turn its energy to service.

The successful approach that does not loose its individual character in acceptance.  
• ☉ ▲ The successful approach that does not loose its individual character in acceptance. The pressure of wanting without losing one’s identity when being accepted by others.  
• C ▽ The tendency once an approach is accepted to get stuck in continued reflection at the expense of continued development. The pressure for acceptance which fears eventual rejection.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ☉ U+2609 Sun; “C” \= stylized Moon (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Unclear at this resolution: the “2-like” node symbol (appears on lines 6, 5, and 2).

# Gate 20 — The Gate of the Now

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 20 page. Header lists three channels (20-10 Awakening, 20-57 The Brain Wave, 20-34 Charisma), harmonic gates 10/57/34, Center Throat, Circuit Knowing, and a Gemini degree span. One paragraph explains Contemplation as the Throat’s existential voice “I am Now,” and how defining Intuition to the Throat changes the voice to “I know I am Now.”

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of the Now.” A compact specification block at top center shows channels (20-10, 20-57, 20-34) with subtitles, harmonic gates (10 Treading; 57 The Gentle; 34 The Power of the Great), Center Throat, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic range within Gemini (quarter mark I). A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about the Throat’s mechanical voice, the Stream of Intuition, and the strictly verbal nature of the Channel of Penetrating Awareness. Three labeled Rave mandalas line the bottom: Right Angle Cross of the Sleeping Phoenix (2), Juxtaposition Cross of the Now, and Left Angle Cross of Duality. A large gray “20” sits at the upper left; a green Gemini glyph is at the upper right; a blue page number “20” appears near the lower margin. Side gutter shows “8 ◁ 20 ▷ 16”.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Now

Channel 20-10 Awakening  
A design of commitment to higher principles  
20-57 The Brain Wave  
A design of awareness  
20-34 Charisma  
A design where thoughts must be deeds

Harmonic Gates 10 Treading  
57 The Gentle  
34 The Power of the Great  
Center Throat  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 00° 07′ 30″ ♊ – 05° 45′ 00″ ♊ \[I\]

Contemplation is the only purely existential gate. The Throat Center is the final stage in the process of expressing  
the Intuition. The Throat Center is not aware. It has a mechanical function, to speak or act, which can be condi-  
tioned by Awareness. Each gate of the Throat has its own unique voice. The voice of Contemplation, unaware and  
free of maia, says, “I am Now”. When the Intuition is Defined to the Throat, then the voice says, “I know I am Now”.  
It is important to remember that the Splenic Center is not a motor. The Channel of Penetrating Awareness, re-  
mains strictly verbal and though the knowing in the now exists as Awareness, it cannot be transformed into action.  
When the entire Stream of Intuition is Defined, the Throat can then manifest all of the potential of Intuition. “I know  
what I am struggling for in the Now”.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE SLEEPING PHOENIX (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF THE NOW  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF DUALITY

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
♊ U+264A Gemini

Notes:  
Side index: “8 ◁ 20 ▷ 16”. Yellow baseline near footer.

## Gate 20 — CONTEMPLATION, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 20 “CONTEMPLATION” lines page with six themes and paired notes (▲ exaltation, ▽ detriment). Degrees span early Gemini.

Long description:  
The heading reads “CONTEMPLATION.” A gold subtitle states: “RECOGNITION AND AWARENESS IN THE NOW WHICH TRANSFORMS UNDERSTANDING INTO RIGHT ACTION.” Along the left margin the six lines (6→1) list names and degree spans. Each line has a blue subheading followed by two bullets, an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each preceded by a small glyph. A large gray “20” appears at the top right, a blue “20” at the lower corners, and a thin yellow rule near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Gemini):  
6 Wisdom — 04° 48′ 45″ – 05° 45′ 00″  
5 Realism — 03° 52′ 30″ – 04° 48′ 45″  
4 Application — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
3 Self-awareness — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″  
2 The dogmatist — 01° 03′ 45″ – 02° 00′ 00″  
1 Superficiality — 00° 07′ 30″ – 01° 03′ 45″

Main text:  
CONTEMPLATION

RECOGNITION AND AWARENESS IN THE NOW WHICH TRANSFORMS  
UNDERSTANDING INTO RIGHT ACTION.

Contemplation which results in the ability to apply understanding.  
• ♀ ▲ The establishment for the benefit of society, values, ideals and their patterns and how they can be understood and applied. The ability to transform individual awareness for general application and understanding.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ The same as above but motivated by the self-satisfying mental challenge rather than altruism. The ability to transform individual awareness for general application for the mental challenge.

Contemplation, in and of itself, is no guarantee of success.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ Where concentration on detail results in a perfected form. The success of expressing awareness through detail.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ Where reality creates dissatisfaction and adds to instability. The expression of awareness in the now through dissatisfaction with the reality one sees.

Recognition and awareness that can only be transformed into action in coop-  
eration with those who have the ability to act upon the understanding.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ The teach whose students transcend him. Where the expression of awareness can only be turned into action through others. The teacher.  
• ☿ ▽ A tendency to prefer theory to application. The expression of the awareness as theory with little interest in its application.

Understanding derived from analysis of personal actions and effects.  
• ☉ ▲ The proper attunement and development of the personality through self-consciousness. The expression of self-conscious awareness in the now.  
• ⊕ ▽ Self-consciousness in the extreme that hampers development. The expression of self-consciousness in the extreme.

Restrictive and intentionally limited understanding.  
• ♀ ▲ The limitation if personal and exclusive is less negative through ascetic withdrawal. A restrictive awareness of the now.  
• C ▽ The power to lead others down a narrow path. The gift through expression of leading others down a narrow and restrictive path.

A reliance on shallowness.  
• ♀ ▲ The raising of superficiality to an art form. The sloganeer. Superficial expression as an art form.  
• C ▽ The superficial expression of the personality. The expression of the superficial personality.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2; ▽ U+25BD; ☿ U+263F (Mercury); ☉ U+2609 (Sun); ⊕ U+2295 (Earth); ♀ U+2640 (Venus); “C” \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Several exaltation/detriment glyphs on lines 6–4 are unclear at this resolution.

# Gate 21 — The Gate of the Hunter/Huntress

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 21 page. Header lists Channel 21-45 “The Money Line” with the note “A design of a materialist,” Harmonic Gate 45 “Gathering Together,” Center Heart, Circuit Ego, and an Aries degree span. One paragraph explains control on the material plane, the voice “I control,” and why this channel is keynoted as the Money Line. Three mandalas at the bottom are captioned Tension, Control, and Endeavour.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of the Hunter/Huntress.” A compact specification block beneath the title gives channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an Aries-to-Aries astrologic range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about materialism, domination, and work autonomy. A magenta Aries glyph appears at upper right; a large gray “21” at upper left; a blue page number “21” near the lower margin. Side index at left reads “17 ◁ 21 ▷ 51.” Three labeled Rave mandalas line the bottom row.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Hunter/Huntress

Channel 21-45 The Money Line  
A design of a materialist  
Harmonic Gate 45 Gathering Together  
Center Heart  
Circuit Ego  
Astrologic Position 09° 30′ 00″ ♈ – 15° 07′ 30″ ♈

The Channel of Materialism is unique in the Body Graph. It stands alone as a single channel, self-enclosed unit.  
It is the only outlet for the two Streams of Awareness, which determine the nature of the Ego; its strength and its  
will. In this sense, it rules our Tribal life. Biting Through is the power for life on the material plane. It is a great con-  
ditioning force in the world. To have this gate in your Design, demands that you control, where you live, what you  
wear and what you eat\! This gate can never afford to have a boss leaning over their shoulder. This is the gate of  
the ‘hunter/huntress’ and it is their ego drive to dominate. If this gate had a voice, it would say, ‘I control’. If it is in  
a situation where it does not control, its power can never be fulfilled. This channel is keynoted as The Money Line.  
For the 21, if they are not in control of how they make their living, they are always far from success.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF TENSION  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF CONTROL  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF ENDEAVOUR

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Aries ♈ U+2648

Notes:  
Gate header page; yellow baseline near footer; page number “21” printed in blue.

## Gate 21 — BITING THROUGH

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 21 “BITING THROUGH” lines page with six themes in Aries and paired notes per line marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment), each preceded by a small glyph.

Long description:  
Heading reads “BITING THROUGH.” A gold subtitle states: “THE JUSTIFIED AND NECESSARY USE OF POWER IN OVERCOMING DELIBERATE AND PERSISTENT INTERFERENCE.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with degree spans. Each line shows a blue subheading followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽). A large gray “21” appears at the top right; a blue “21” sits near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs above the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads preserved; italics reproduce in-page emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aries):  
6 Chaos — 14° 11′ 15″ – 15° 07′ 30″  
5 Objectivity — 13° 15′ 00″ – 14° 11′ 15″  
4 Strategy — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
3 Powerlessness — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″  
2 Might is right — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
1 Warning — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″

Main text:  
BITING THROUGH

THE JUSTIFIED AND NECESSARY USE OF POWER IN OVERCOMING  
DELIBERATE AND PERSISTENT INTERFERENCE.

Where ineffective action leads to disorder.  
• ω ▲ The war of attrition. The continuance of legitimate action in the hope of eventually succeeding despite all odds and increasing disorder. Where the ego is out of touch, the display of will power leads to disorder.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ Withdrawal and reliance on inner order in a chaotic situation. Where the material direction is chaotic, the ego will withdraw and use its power to find an inner order.

The use of force must be based on impartiality and not emotionally motivated.  
• 2 ▲ A principled and legal character that ensures objectivity. The balanced ego whose will power is applied objectively.  
• ω ▽ The drive for elimination is so powerful that objectivity is related purely to cause and not effect. The ego whose will power is applied subjectively.

Careful appraisal of opposing forces to establish a proper response.  
• 2 ▲ Success in action through clarity. The ego to succeed on the material plane and the instinct to use will power effectively in response to conditions.  
• ⊕ ▽ A tendency, when in the right, to misjudge the power of one’s opponents. The drive when in the right to follow one’s ego rather than one’s instincts.

Where the just are condemned to a futile confrontation with superior forces.  
• Ψ ▲ Defeat as a survivable humiliation often acceptable through the abuse of drugs and alcohol. Unless one follows one’s own material path, the breaking of the ego by superiors.  
• 2 ▽ Total withdrawal. A lack of will for the material path in order to protect the ego.

The legitimacy of action in response to flagrant and persistent interference.  
• ♂ ▲ Powerful and extreme reaction. Here, the severest action will be the most successful. The legitimate rejection of interference on the material plane.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ A tendency to regret severity, no matter how legitimate. The ego uncomfortable with severity.

The use of force as a last resort.  
• ♂ ▲ The fierceness to be respected without recourse to action. The will power and ego to ensure respect.  
• C ▽ An inherent peacefulness that all too often turns a necessary warning into a plea. A lack of will power that is forced to ask for respect.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ω U+03C9; ⊕ U+2295 (Earth); ♂ U+2642; Ψ U+03A8; “C” \= stylized Moon used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Unclear at this resolution: the “2-like” node symbol (appears on several bullets) and the detriment glyphs on lines 6 and 2\.

# Gate 22 — The Gate of Openness

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 22 page. Header lists Channel 12-22 “Openness,” Harmonic Gate 12 “Standstill,” Center Solar Plexus, Circuit Knowing, and a Pisces degree span. One long paragraph explains public access through emotion (“Grace”), the role of social skills in disseminating individual awareness, and how the left ear hears in a wave. Three mandalas at the bottom are captioned Rulership, Grace, and Informing.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Openness.” A compact specification block beneath the title shows: Channel 12-22 Openness (The design of the social being), Harmonic Gate 12 Standstill, Center Solar Plexus, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic position from 17° 00′ 00″ to 22° 37′ 30″ in Pisces. A thin rule separates the header from one explanatory paragraph about the Ego/Knowing process, social interaction, and hearing (right/inner/left ear). Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas: “THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF RULERSHIP,” “THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF GRACE,” and “THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF INFORMING.” A purple Pisces glyph sits at upper right; a large gray gate number appears at upper left; a small blue page number is printed near the lower margin above a thin yellow baseline rule.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Openness

Channel 12-22 Openness  
The design of the social being  
Harmonic Gate 12 Standstill  
Center Solar Plexus  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 17° 00′ 00″ ♓ – 22° 37′ 30″ ♓

The potential to realize the spirit becomes the possibility to share that spirit with others. The role of the individual is  
incomplete without social skills. On the Mental plane through the 43rd gate, the individual has the conceptual  
capacity to communicate its insight to others. The task is made more difficult by its strangeness. The Channel of  
Openness provides the individual with the gift for public access. The possibility of this gate is to empower others  
with the individual emotion. It is the quality of the emotional spirit which will condition how receptive the collective  
will be to the individual awareness. The Knowing process requires a certain amount of social interaction to dis-  
seminate its individuality towards the goal of Mutation. Grace is where the spirit of the knower is released in a  
wave.

The Sense of Hearing is associated with the Awareness possibility gates of the Knowing Circuit. The right Ear, the  
existential ear through the 57th gate; the Inner Ear, the inner voice of the individual through the 43rd gate; the left  
Ear is through Grace. What you hear with your left Ear, you hear in a Wave\! If you listen to the telephone with the  
left Ear, you will only hear the good or bad of the hope & pain wave, the rest will come later.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF RULERSHIP  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF GRACE  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF INFORMING

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Pisces ♓ U+2653

Notes:  
Header page for Gate 22 (Openness/Grace). Mentions Gates 43 and 57 in context of hearing. Page number printed at bottom; thin yellow baseline near footer.

## Gate 22 — GRACE

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 22 “GRACE” lines page with six line themes in Pisces degree ranges, each with paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) and small glyphs.

Long description:  
The heading reads “GRACE.” A gold subtitle states: “A QUALITY OF BEHAVIOUR BEST SUITED IN HANDLING MUNDANE AND TRIVIAL SITUATIONS.” The left margin lists lines 6 to 1 with their degree spans. For each line, a blue subheading is followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a glyph. A large gray “22” sits at the top right; a blue page number “22” appears near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserved):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Pisces):  
6 Maturity — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
5 Directness — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″  
4 Sensitivity — 19° 48′ 45″ – 20° 45′ 00″  
3 The enchanter — 18° 52′ 30″ – 19° 48′ 45″  
2 Charm school — 17° 56′ 15″ – 18° 52′ 30″  
1 Second class ticket — 17° 00′ 00″ – 17° 56′ 15″

Main text:  
GRACE

A QUALITY OF BEHAVIOUR BEST SUITED IN HANDLING MUNDANE AND  
TRIVIAL SITUATIONS.

The alignment with experience of form with substance.  
• ☉ ▲ Natural and evident leadership and authority. The possibility that experience in social interaction will result in a leadership capacity.  
• ♂ ▽ The alignment tends to express itself in nonconformist modes. The possibility that experience in social interaction will result in a non-conformist expression of openness.

The disregard of form when required.  
• 2 ▲ The power inherent in higher principles to successfully transgress behavioral codes. The possibility through emotional awareness to behave individualistically in social interaction.  
• ♂ ▽ A tendency to create embarrassing situations, and though invariably successful, the often resulting reputation for crudity and impudence. The possibility that individual behaviour in social interaction will generate negative projections from others.

The modification of behaviour to enrich interactions.  
• ♆ ▲ A mediumistic simplicity that rejects elaborate rituals. The possibility of social openness through the rejection of formality.  
• ♂ ▽ An over-reliance on the mechanics of style that can abort potentially significant relationships. The limitation of social openness through the need for formality.

Perfected grace.  
• 2 ▲ Form as a definition and actualization of substance. The possibility for perfected openness through the alignment of emotional energy and awareness.  
• ♂ ▽ Unconscious grace. An innate openness.

The belief that style can mask nature.  
• ☉ ▲ The ability to successfully delude oneself and others. The possibility to attract others with an emotional style.  
• 2 ▽ The legalization of form over substance. Where the style is energized at the expense of awareness.

A reliance on shallowness.  
• C ▲ The ability to accept and enjoy a subordinate position. The emotional awareness to enjoy a subordinate position.  
• ♂ ▽ The inevitable humiliation that comes with claiming a first class seat with a second class ticket. Where the emotional energy challenges the awareness and can result in humiliation socially.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; “C” \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Note: a “2-like” symbol is used on several bullets (lines 5, 3, and 2).

# Gate 23 — The Gate of Assimilation

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 23 page. Header lists Channel 43-23 “Structuring,” Harmonic Gate 43 “Breakthrough,” Center Throat, Circuit Knowing, and a Taurus degree span. One paragraph explains the need to communicate unique insight to the collective—genius to freak. Three mandalas at the bottom are captioned with the Right Angle, Juxtaposition, and Left Angle crosses.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Assimilation.” A compact specification block beneath the title shows channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an astrologic position range within Taurus. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about expressing individual insight so the collective can assimilate it. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Taurus glyph appears at the upper right; a large gray “23” stands at the upper left; a small blue “23” is near the lower corners. Side gutter shows neighboring gates.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Assimilation

Channel 43-23 Structuring  
A design of individuality (Genius to Freak)  
Harmonic Gate 43 Breakthrough  
Center Throat  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 18° 52′ 30″ ♉ – 24° 30′ 00″ ♉

The very fact that an insight is unique and individual requires that its expression be able to communicate its es-  
sence to the collective. The purpose of individuation is to leave the door open to mutation. If an insight is truly of  
value to others , it has to be successfully communicated. This gate is about eliminating intolerance. In an intoler-  
ant world, the individual cannot survive. When the possibility of insight and the drive for assimilation come to-  
gether, are connected, defined, we have the Channel of Structuring, a design of Individuality, from genius to freak.  
If the individual can express their insight clearly to the collective they will eventually earn the collective's respect. If  
they cannot clearly express their insight, they will be shunned and dismissed as outsiders or freaks. This descrip-  
tion is of course the extreme of the polarity. This is the voice, that says, ‘I know (whether they do or not)’

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF EXPLANATION (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF ASSIMILATION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF DEDICATION

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Taurus ♉ U+2649

Notes:  
Yellow baseline near footer; side index shows neighbors; blue page number “23.”

## Gate 23 — SPLITTING APART, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 23 “SPLITTING APART” lines page with six line themes in Taurus degree ranges, each with paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment).

Long description:  
The heading reads “SPLITTING APART.” A gold subtitle states: “AMORALITY. THE AWARENESS AND UNDERSTANDING WHICH LEADS TO THE ACCEPTANCE OF DIVERSITY.” The left margin lists the six lines (6→1) with their degree spans. For each line, a blue subheading is followed by two bullets, an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “23” appears at the top right; a blue page number “23” is printed near the lower corners; a thin yellow baseline runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve the in-page emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Taurus):  
6 Fusion — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
5 Assimilation — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″  
4 Fragmentation — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
3 Individuality — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″  
2 Self-defense — 19° 48′ 45″ – 20° 45′ 00″  
1 Proselytization — 18° 52′ 30″ – 19° 48′ 45″

Main text:  
SPLITTING APART

AMORALITY. THE AWARENESS AND UNDERSTANDING WHICH LEADS TO  
THE ACCEPTANCE OF DIVERSITY.

The gradual attunement of diversity through synthesis.  
• ♂ ▲ The exponential growth of energy and its power of assertion engendered by fusion. Individual knowing which brings diversity to synthesis.  
• 2 ▽ The principled but futile withdrawal from fusion that leads to atrophy. Individual knowing that holds on to diversity and loses its power in expression.

The practical acceptance of the values of another path.  
• 2 ▲ Expansion and contribution through assimilation. The gift of communicating individual insight to the collective.  
• C ▽ Motive driven assimilation from an inferior position, i.e. for protection or nourishment. Motive driven assimilation for acceptance and protection from the collective.

Diversification without a perceived potential for synthesis.  
• ⊕ ▲ Fatalism and egoism and damn the consequences. Individual expression which has no collective value.  
• C ▽ Atheism and paranoia. Individual expression which engenders isolation and fear.

Independent expression that is not by its nature detrimental to others.  
• ☉ ▲ Vitality and personal power that can engender jealousy but not threat. Individual expression which attracts attention but not threat.  
• ω ▽ An individual mysteriousness that attracts active suspicion and threat. The freak. Individual expression that attracts suspicion and threat.

The need to abandon tolerance when survival is threatened.  
• 2 ▲ The principle of preservation at its most acute. The abandonment of tolerance when individual expression is threatened.  
• C ▽ Where Jupiter will strike out to preserve its integrity, the Moon is often satisfied with just protecting itself by fending off hostility. The defense of individual expression in the face of hostility.

The attempt to undermine one set of values for another.  
• 2 ▲ The sage, who in the extreme can defend evil as a part of the greater good. The powerful expression of an insight which will undermine established values.  
• ♂ ▽ The missionary whose very light will bring darkness. The powerful expression of an insight which will produce negative effects.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ω U+03C9 omega; “C” \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Note: the edition’s “2-like” node symbol (shown here as “2”) appears multiple times.

# Gate 24 — The Gate of Rationalizing

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 24 page. Header lists Channel 61-24 “Awareness” with the note “A design of the thinker,” Harmonic Gate 61 “Inner Truth,” Center Ajna, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic span in Taurus. One paragraph explains “Return” as pondering that gives inspiration a rational form, noting the risk and test of Spirit.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Rationalizing.” A compact specification block beneath the title shows channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Taurus-to-Taurus degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph describing individual conceptualizing and rationalization. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A gold Taurus glyph appears at the upper right; a large gray “24” is at the upper left; a small blue “24” appears near the lower margin above a thin yellow baseline. Left gutter shows neighboring gates around the rim.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Rationalizing

Channel 61-24 Awareness  
A design of the thinker  
Harmonic Gate 61 Inner Truth  
Center Ajna  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 07° 37′ 30″ ♉ – 13° 15′ 00″ ♉

The Return is a conceptualizing gate. Individual conceptualizing is a complex process. The role of the mental indi-  
viduality is to find a way to communicate a unique awareness to the Collective mind. Before any attempt at com-  
munication is possible, the inspiration must be given a rational form. The word rationalizing often has a negative  
connotation attached to it which is a projection of the collective on the individual. The Return is just that, returning  
over and over again, what is poetically called pondering, until the energy is transformed into a rational form. In the  
symmetry of this circuit, there are three gates of Awareness potential; 28: The Risk Taker and 55: the gate of the  
half empty or half full cup, Abundance. The design to rationalize is a risk and a test of the Spirit. There is no proof,  
logical collective or experience, abstract collective, there is only the potential that what has been rationalized may  
be a true knowing.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE FOUR WAYS  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF RATIONALIZATION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF INCARNATION

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Taurus ♉ U+2649

Notes:  
Header page for Gate 24\. Blue page number “24”; yellow baseline rule near footer. Side index shows neighboring gates.

## Gate 24 — RETURNING, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 24 “RETURNING” lines page with six line themes in Taurus degree ranges and paired notes per line marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment).

Long description:  
Title reads “RETURNING.” A gold subtitle says: “THE NATURAL AND SPONTANEOUS PROCESS OF TRANSFORMATION AND RENEWAL.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with degree spans. For each line there is a blue subheading, then two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each preceded by a small glyph. A large gray “24” sits at the top right; a blue page number “24” appears near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Taurus):  
6 The gift horse — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
5 Confession — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″  
4 The hermit — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
3 The addict — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″  
2 Recognition — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
1 The sin of omission — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″

Main text:  
RETURNING

THE NATURAL AND SPONTANEOUS PROCESS OF TRANSFORMATION AND  
RENEWAL.

The possibility of being deaf when opportunity knocks.  
• 2 ▲ The conscious participation in a process that prepares one for easy identification of opportunity. Identification and focus with the rational thought process.  
• ω ▽ Innate suspicion which leads inevitably to lost opportunities. Often irrational suspicion that distorts the focus and can lead to missed opportunities.

The courage to admit the mistakes of the past.  
• C ▲ The practical value of starting with a clean slate, symbolized by the New Moon. Rational correction that opens the way to new possibilities.  
• ♂ ▽ The tendency to try to minimize past mistakes through rationalization; turning confession into justification. Irrational justification of past mistakes.

Transformation that can only take place in isolation.  
• 2 ▲ The discipline and focus that assures renewal. Aloneness enriches the potential for rational thought.  
• ♆ ▽ The tendency in isolation to live in a fantasy world. Aloneness encourages the potential of illusion or delusion.

The powerful attraction of regressive forms.  
• ♀ ▲ The ultimate though difficult triumph over regression. The difficult but possible task of overcoming irrationality.  
• 2 ▽ Addiction and regression legitimized by success. Irrationality maintained and legitimized by success.

The proper and spontaneous adaption to new forms.  
• C ▲ The proper and spontaneous adaption to new forms. The potential gift of conceptualizing spontaneously.  
• ♂ ▽ The vanity to see transformation as a personal achievement rather than a socially supported or natural phenomenon. The mental vanity that the gift of conceptualizing spontaneously can produce.

Transformation that requires retrogressive periods before renewal can take place.  
• ☉ ▲ The will to triumph and in this case the faith that the end justifies the means. Inspiration that demands a reassessment of past thinking before a rational concept can be established.  
• ♆ ▽ Self-delusion which unnaturally justifies periods of retrogression. Inspiration that leads to an irrational focus on what is past.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ω U+03C9 omega; C \= stylized Moon (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; ☉ U+2609 Sun.  
Note: a “2-like” node symbol appears on several bullets (rendered here as “2”).

# Gate 25 — The Gate of the Spirit of the Self

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 25 page. Header lists Channel 25-51 “Initiation,” Harmonic Gate 51 “The Arousing,” Center G, Circuit Centering, and an astrologic span crossing late Pisces into early Aries. One paragraph describes innocence, spiritual testing, and the “warrior/fool” landing on its feet. Three mandalas below are captioned the Vessel of Love, Innocence, and Healing.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of the Spirit of the Self.” A compact spec block at upper center details channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an astrologic position from late Pisces to early Aries. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph on innocence, spiritual testing, scars, and transcendent love. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A decorative Pisces glyph appears in the header; a large gray “25” sits at upper left; a blue page number “25” appears near the lower margin; a thin yellow rule runs above the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Spirit of the Self

Channel 25-51 Initiation  
A design of needing to be first  
Harmonic Gate 51 The Arousing  
Center G  
Circuit Centering  
Astrologic Position 28° 15′ 00″ ♓ – 03° 52′ 30″ ♈

The centering of a being depends on the quality of their innocence. The innocence is tested in the ‘fire’ of competi-  
tive energy. Despite the outward appearances the true competition is whether the Self can maintain its innocence  
despite circumstances. This is the gate of the Spiritual Warrior, the Shaman / Priestess. It is one of the four gates  
of the Cross of the Vessel, a gate of Love. Innocence is the gate of the Blood. It is the potential to love existence  
without discrimination. A flower can be loved as profoundly as a human. This quality of love is often projected as  
cool or cold. It is neither. The mystical potential of this love is transcendent and universal. The Spirit is always be-  
ing tested. This is the gate of scars, of the potential of being wounded. When the Warrior / Fool leaps into the  
void, it is Innocence that must land on its feet. The Spirit can be broken but triumph and survival enrich the spirit  
and result in the wonder of being.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE VESSEL OF LOVE  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF INNOCENCE  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF HEALING

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
♓ U+2653 Pisces, ♈ U+2648 Aries

Notes:  
Side index: “36 ◁ 25 ▷ 17.” Yellow baseline near footer.

## Gate 25 — INNOCENCE, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 25 “INNOCENCE” lines page showing six line themes with short blue subheads, each followed by exaltation and detriment notes. Degrees run from late Pisces into early Aries. Layout matches other line pages: title, gold subtitle, left margin line list, two-column text, and a yellow baseline near the footer.

Long description:  
The page title reads “INNOCENCE.” Beneath it a gold subtitle states the theme of perfected action through uncontrived spontaneity. The left margin lists lines 6 through 1 with their degree spans. For each line, a blue subheading summarizes the theme, followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽). The body text emphasizes healing, survival, sensibility, devotion to the present, and motiveless action. Line 4 explicitly notes “No planet in detriment.” A large gray “25” appears at top right; a small blue page number “25” sits at the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs near the bottom.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Pisces → Aries):  
6 Ignorance — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
5 Recuperation — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″  
4 Survival — 01° 03′ 45″ – 02° 00′ 00″  
3 Sensibility — 00° 07′ 30″ – 01° 03′ 45″  
2 The existentialist — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
1 Selflessness — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″

Main text:

INNOCENCE

THE PERFECTION OF ACTION THROUGH UNCONTRIVED AND SPONTANEOUS  
NATURE.

False innocence betrayed by actions.  
• ▲ The least mild of a negative position, where inappropriate action is censured. The loss of innocence through inappropriate action.  
• ▽ Constant inappropriate and destabilizing actions whose very counterproductiveness strips away the facade of innocence. Constant inappropriate action in times of challenges that can break the spirit.

When innocence is sapped of its vitality, healing is the first priority.  
• ▲ The ability to recognize the inner meaning of an affliction and to withdraw until it is healed. The power of the spirit to heal and be healed.  
• ▽ Hypochondria and the need to be healed by others. The weakness of the spirit which requires healing from others.

The nature of true innocence can be maintained regardless of circumstances.  
• ▲ The beauty of the rose in the garbage dump. Jupiter is also exalted. The highest principles even amongst the greatest decadence. The spiritual warrior; the innocence maintained regardless of circumstances.  
• ▽ No planet in detriment.

The recognition that innocent action does not in itself guarantee success.  
• ▲ The power of the ego to withstand failure and still maintain its nature. The power of the spirit to withstand failure and shock.  
• ▽ The potential loss of innocence through misfortune that in the extreme can manifest from crime to suicide. The potential loss of spirit through failure or shock.

Devotion and dedication to the now.  
• ▲ The perfection of the intellect through concentration and focus on what is, rather than, what could be or has been. The innocence of the Self and its protection can only be maintained in the now.  
• ▽ A dedication that can never be free of personal motivation and its attendant projections. A lack of innocence in the now that risks protection through projection.

Motiveless action.  
• ▲ The universalization of activity. Psychic attunement that is its own reward. The potential for centering through attunement to challenges.  
• ▽ A tendency to publicize one’s selflessness. The insecurity of the Self manifested in times of challenge.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment. Jupiter explicitly named as exalted in line 4; the line also states “No planet in detriment.”

Notes:  
Lines page for Gate 25; degrees progress from 28°15′ Pisces (line 1 start) to 03°52′30″ Aries (line 6 end). Blue page number “25”; yellow baseline near footer.
