# Gate 26 — The Gate of the Egoist

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 26 page. Header lists Channel 26-44 “Surrender” with the note “A design of a transmitter,” Harmonic Gate 44 “Coming to Meet,” Center Heart, Circuit Ego, and an astrologic span in Sagittarius. A single paragraph describes the Trickster/Egoist, memory manipulation, and the thymus/immune link. Three Rave mandalas at the bottom are captioned Rulership (4), the Trickster, and Confrontation (2).

Long description:  
Clean white layout with title “The Gate of the Egoist.” Under the title, a compact spec block shows channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and the Sagittarius degree range. A thin rule separates the header from one dense explanatory paragraph about Gate 26’s role in memory, ego strength, and its connection to the Spleen via the 26-44 channel. Bottom row: three labeled mandalas. A pink Sagittarius arrow glyph appears in the header; a large gray “26” stands top-left; blue page number “26” at the lower margin above a yellow baseline rule.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Egoist

Channel 26-44 Surrender  
A design of a transmitter  
Harmonic Gate 44 Coming to Meet  
Center Heart  
Circuit Ego  
Astrologic Position 17° 00′ 00″ ♐ – 22° 37′ 30″ ♐

This is the gate of the Egoist. It is the gate of the Trickster. With the 44th gate, this gate is a specific gate of Mem-  
ory. This is the memory that can be manipulated. It is important to remember, that the Heart center is not aware.  
The manipulation of memory is inborn and a mechanic. ‘Fools are liars and prophets too, for they can never know  
if what they say is true. They just have to wait and see.’ Every gate in Design is by its nature dualistic. From the  
mouth of the great liar can also come the great truth. This is the gate of the Ego’s strength. This channel forms  
one of the main health networks in the body. The 26th gate is the gate of the Thymus Gland, where our Immune  
System is developed during our pre-natal phase. It is the only direct connection between the Ego and the Spleen  
and thusly can cleanse the ego and how its memory is applied.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF RULERSHIP (4)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF THE TRICKSTER  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF CONFRONTATION (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Sagittarius ♐ U+2650

Notes:  
Header/intro page for Gate 26\. Blue page number “26”; yellow baseline near footer.

## Gate 26 — THE TAMING POWER OF THE GREAT

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 26 “THE TAMING POWER OF THE GREAT” lines page for the Ego/Heart center, showing six line themes with Aries–Sagittarius degree spans and paired notes (▲ exaltation, ▽ detriment) referencing memory, authority, censorship, influence, history, and prudence.

Long description:  
White page titled “THE TAMING POWER OF THE GREAT.” A gold subtitle reads: “THE MAXIMIZATION OF THE POWER OF MEMORY APPLIED TO THE NURTURING OF CONTINUITY.” The left margin lists the six lines (6→1) with exact zodiac degree ranges. For each line, a blue subheading is followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a small glyph (Sun, Venus, Mars, Neptune, omega, etc.). A large gray “26” sits at top right; a blue page number “26” appears near the lower corners; a thin yellow baseline runs above the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Sagittarius):  
6 Authority — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
5 Adaptability — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″  
4 Censorship — 19° 48′ 45″ – 20° 45′ 00″  
3 Influence — 18° 52′ 30″ – 19° 48′ 45″  
2 The lessons of history — 17° 56′ 15″ – 18° 52′ 30″  
1 A bird in the hand — 17° 00′ 00″ – 17° 56′ 15″

Main text:  
THE TAMING POWER OF THE GREAT

THE MAXIMIZATION OF THE POWER OF MEMORY APPLIED TO THE NUR-  
TURING OF CONTINUITY.

The natural attainment of influence justified by the correctness of actions.  
• ☉ ▲ The embodiment of reason and purpose that passes the test of time. The strong ego whose influence is justified by the correctness of its actions.  
• C ▽ Authority as a symbol and focus but not necessarily as a true embodiment. The constitutional monarch, stripped of real power but still the symbol of the continuity of authority. The expression of the ego as a role whose influence is symbolic and lacks authority.

The understanding of mechanics and the application of energy to achieve maximum potential.  
• ♂ ▲ The understanding of mechanics and the application of energy to achieve maximum potential. The power of memory which maximizes the potential of the ego to attract others.  
• ♀ ▽ A resistance and dissatisfaction when basic changes to nature are necessary. The resistance of the egoist to adaption.

The alteration of memory through elimination.  
• ω ▲ The ability through censorship to save the collectivity from itself. The power of the ego maintained through forgetting.  
• 2 ▽ The use of censorship to maintain the status quo; selective memory in fear of unrestrained consequences. The power of the ego maintained through selective memory.

The ability, once prepared to gather support.  
• ☉ ▲ The authority to focus communal effort. The power of the ego to gather support.  
• 2 ▽ The leadership, while gathering support will underestimate potential challenges. The egoist unable to recognize the potential challenges in others.

The energy and depth of reflection to learn from the past in anticipation of the future.  
• ☉ ▲ The energy and depth of reflection to learn from the past in anticipation of the future. The power of the ego to mature through experience.  
• ω ▽ The urge to take action despite the evidence of history. The failure of the ego to respect experience.

The ability to enjoy the dreams engendered by accomplishment in order to avoid the delusion of unlikely potential.  
• ♆ ▲ The ability to enjoy the dreams engendered by accomplishment in order to avoid the delusion of unlikely potential. The ego which transcends limitation through dreaming.  
• ♂ ▽ Accomplishment as a licence for foolhardy risk taking. The refusal of the ego to be satisfied.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 (exaltation); ▽ U+25BD (detriment); ☉ U+2609 Sun; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♆ U+2646 Neptune; ω U+03C9 omega; C \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Edition symbol: a “2-like” node marks several detriments.

Notes:  
Lines page for Gate 26 (Ego/Heart, Ego circuit). Large gray “26” at top right; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 27 — The Gate of Caring

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 27 page. Header lists Channel 27–50 “Preservation,” Harmonic Gate 50 “The Cauldron,” Center Sacral, Circuit Defence, and a Taurus degree span. One paragraph describes nourishment, caring, custodianship, and the 27–50 Channel of Preservation.

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of Caring.” Under the title is a compact specification block showing channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and an astrologic range in Taurus. A thin rule separates this header from a single explanatory paragraph about the need to nourish and be nourished, the risks when values are absent, and the custodial nature of the 27–50 channel. At the bottom are three labeled Rave mandalas. A gold Taurus glyph appears at upper right; a large gray “27” at upper left; a blue page number “27” near the lower margin; a thin yellow baseline runs above the footer. Left gutter shows neighboring gates.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Caring

Channel 27-50 Preservation  
A design of custodianship  
Harmonic Gate 50 The Cauldron  
Center Sacral  
Circuit Defence  
Astrologic Position 02° 00′ 00″ ♉ – 07° 37′ 30″ ♉

Without nourishment and caring, nothing can survive, particularly a Human Being, who is so vulnerable for so  
many years. This is the mirror of the pure sexuality of the 59th gate. This is a gate that is both a fuel and an ex-  
pression of sexuality. Through definition the power of the Sacral to move us is initiated. The drive to nourish is also  
in its polarity, the drive to be nourished or cared for. This need to be cared for, driven at the Sacral level can lead  
to all kinds of Sexual health problems. If it is accompanied by a lack of values from the harmonic gate 50, serious  
problems are possible. When the possibility of values and the expression of caring come together, we have the  
Channel of Preservation, a design of Custodianship. Those who preserve and protect. This is the gate of compas-  
sion, the power to care for the weak, the sick and the young and can be impressively altruistic in its manifestation,  
as in the case of Mother Teresa of Calcutta.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE UNEXPECTED  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF CARING  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF ALIGNMENT

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Taurus ♉ U+2649

Notes:  
Side index shows neighbors: “3 ◁ 27 ▷ 24”. Three mandalas along the bottom with the captions above.

## Gate 27 — NOURISHMENT

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 27 “NOURISHMENT” lines page with six themes (Wariness, The executor, Generosity, Greed, Self-sufficiency, Selfishness). Each line shows degree ranges within Taurus and two notes marked ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment). The page emphasizes caring, distribution, sharing, greed, the law of having before giving, and ego-care.

Long description:  
Title reads “NOURISHMENT.” A gold subtitle states: “THE ENHANCEMENT OF THE QUALITY AND THE SUBSTANCE OF ALL ACTIVITIES THROUGH CARING.” Left margin lists lines 6→1 with Taurus degree spans. For each line a blue subheading is followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a small glyph. Large gray “27” at top right; blue page number “27” at the bottom; thin yellow baseline near the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Taurus):  
6 Wariness — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
5 The executor — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″  
4 Generosity — 04° 48′ 45″ – 05° 45′ 00″  
3 Greed — 03° 52′ 30″ – 04° 48′ 45″  
2 Self-sufficiency — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
1 Selfishness — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″

Main text:

NOURISHMENT

THE ENHANCEMENT OF THE QUALITY AND THE SUBSTANCE OF ALL AC-  
TIVITIES THROUGH CARING.

A protection against an abuse of generosity.  
• C ▲ A practical and realistic approach to nurturing, whose appropriateness is guided by feelings and instinct. The power and strength to be realistic in one’s capacities to care and nurture.  
• ω ▽ A tendency to oversuspiciousness. The power of suspicion in limiting the expression of caring.

The ability to distribute effectively the resources of others.  
• 2 ▲ Either, the gifted and principled agent of distribution or the good sense and ability to find one. The power and strength to care for the resources of others.  
• 2 ▽ A restrictive nature that hampers distribution or the seeking of advice and assistance. Weakness and the risk of loss of power restrict caring.

The natural sharing of attained abundance.  
• 2 ▲ Magnanimous and qualitative sharing. The gift of rewarding those who are deserving. The power and strength to share generously.  
• ♂ ▽ Indiscriminate sharing. The potential loss of power and strength through indiscriminate sharing.

The obsession with having much more than one needs.  
• ω ▲ Here, the psychological manifestation. The obsession and dependency on knowing what is hidden. The secret policeman. The power derived in having more than one needs, whether sexually, mentally, or materially.  
• ♂ ▽ Mundane and wholly without redeeming value, greed, a lust that inevitably cripples and addicts. The lust for power to get more than one needs.

The obvious law that to give, one must have.  
• C ▲ The Mother. The great nourisher. The strength to nurture and the power to care.  
• ♂ ▽ The child depleting the resources of others. Weakness that can sap the strength and power of others.

Motiveless action.  
• ☉ ▲ The ego-driven first law of caring for oneself which is not necessarily at the expense of others. The power to care for oneself first.  
• ⊕ ▽ Envy and its attendant misfortunes. The power of selfishness that is manifested through envy.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ω U+03C9 omega; C \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).

# Gate 28 — The Gate of the Game Player

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 28 page. Header lists Channel 28–38 “Struggle” (A design of stubbornness), Harmonic Gate 38 “Opposition,” Center Spleen, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic span in Scorpio. One paragraph explains individual, trial-and-error struggle, the fear of death, and mutation, with three mandalas captioned Unexpected (3), Risks, and Alignment (2).

Long description:  
White page headed “The Gate of the Game Player.” A compact specification block details the channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Scorpio-to-Scorpio degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about Splenic awareness and struggle (trial and error, risks taken alone, convincing Tribe and Collective), finishing with three labeled Rave mandalas. A purple Scorpio glyph appears at upper right; a large gray “28” is at upper left; a blue page number “28” sits near the lower margin; a thin yellow baseline runs above the footer. Left gutter shows “50 ◁ 28 ▷ 44”.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Game Player

Channel 28-38 Struggle  
A design of stubbornness  
Harmonic Gate 38 Opposition  
Center Spleen  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 02° 00′ 00″ ♏ – 07° 37′ 30″ ♏

The Spleen is the home of our oldest and only existential awareness, our body consciousness, the ‘gut’ feeling.  
The 28th gate is a conceptualizing gate, the first step of any awareness process. The potential of intuition is to  
know in the now, whether it is of value to struggle or not. Where the mechanical fighter will not listen, the game  
player must. The fear of this gate is of death. The challenge is life itself. The development of intuitional potential is  
based on trial and error. Being a part of the Knowing Circuit, this is a deeply individual process. The risks are al-  
ways taken alone and so are the consequences. The individual in society is the key to direction, but that role is  
fraught with difficulties. Any change in direction is the direct result of Mutation, the Format (3 / 60\) of this circuit. If  
the mutation is going to be successful and replace the old with the new, the Tribe and the Collective must be con-  
vinced of its value. The knower who does not know what knowing to fight for, or how to fight for it, will face enor-  
mous resistance.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE UNEXPECTED (3)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF RISKS  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF ALIGNMENT (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Scorpio ♏ U+264F

Notes:  
Header/intro page for Gate 28 (Spleen, Knowing circuit). Side index “50 ◁ 28 ▷ 44”. Three mandalas along the bottom row.

## Gate 28 — PREPONDERANCE OF THE GREAT

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 28 “PREPONDERANCE OF THE GREAT” lines page (Scorpio degrees). Six themes—Blaze of glory, Treachery, Holding on, Adventurism, Shaking hands with the devil, Preparation—each with paired notes (▲ exaltation, ▽ detriment) describing intuition under struggle, risk, alliance, and detail.

Long description:  
White page with the title “PREPONDERANCE OF THE GREAT.” Gold subtitle: “THE TRANSITORINESS OF POWER AND INFLUENCE.” The left margin lists six line names with degree ranges. Each blue subheading is followed by two bullets: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “28” appears at the top right; a small blue page number “28” is printed near the lower margin; a thin yellow rule runs above the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; italics preserve in-page emphasis):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Scorpio):  
6 Blaze of glory — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
5 Treachery — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″  
4 Holding on — 04° 48′ 45″ – 05° 45′ 00″  
3 Adventurism — 03° 52′ 30″ – 04° 48′ 45″  
2 Shaking hands with the devil — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
1 Preparation — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″

Main text:

PREPONDERANCE OF THE GREAT

THE TRANSITORINESS OF POWER AND INFLUENCE.

Sacrifice rather than capitulation to the law of deterioration.  
• ω ▲ Regeneration and renewal no matter what the price. The deep intuitive drive to win no matter what the cost.  
• Ψ ▽ Self-destruction. The deep intuitive fear of defeat and a potentially profound hopelessness in times of struggle.

The abuse of trust.  
• ω ▲ The manipulation of the collective, that while pitting one faction against the other, does not directly support or reject either. The intuitive capacity of the game player to provoke struggle amongst others.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ The breaking of alliances with trusted forces to align with more powerful forces and its attendant destabilization of the whole. The intuitive recognition in times of struggle to know when alliances must be broken and its destabilizing effect on others.

The ability through whatever means to keep one's grip.  
• 2 ▲ The application of knowledge to exploit opportunities and usually for the greater good. A depth of intuition at its best in struggle and often of value to others.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ The application of intelligence to hold on, exclusively out of self-interest. A depth of intuition that is stubbornly selfish in its capacity to hold on.

Unfounded risk taking.  
• 2 ▲ A basic conservatism that even in adventurous acts is necessarily prudent. An intuitive caution in risk taking in times of struggle.  
• 2 ▽ Here a perverse manifestation of Jupiter’s expansiveness where risk taking is rationalized and failure ensured. Intuitive rationalizing of risk taking in times of struggle.

Distasteful alliance.  
• ☉ ▲ A means, however unsavoury, that is justified by its end. When the game turns to a struggle, the intuitive acceptance of any alliance in order to win.  
• 2 ▽ The anxiety engendered by sacrificing higher principles when there is no guarantee of success. The risk in sacrificing principles when there is no guarantee of victory.

Preparation.  
• ♂ ▲ The desire to be effective manifested in the application of energy to detail. The intuition to potentially apply energy to detail.  
• ♀ ▽ The aesthetic appreciation of planning that may have no real application. An intuition for detail without the potential for application.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ω U+03C9; ☉ U+2609; ♂ U+2642; ♀ U+2640.  
Unclear at this resolution: the trident-like “Ψ/Neptune-style” glyph on line 6 detriment; detriment glyphs on lines 5 and 4\.

Notes:  
Lines span Scorpio 02° 00′ 00″ to 07° 37′ 30″. Page shows large gray “28,” blue page number “28,” and the standard yellow baseline rule.

# Gate 29 — The Gate of Saying Yes

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 29 header page. Channel 46–29 “Discovery,” Harmonic Gate 46 “Pushing Upward,” Center Sacral, Circuit Sensing, and an astrologic span from late Leo into early Virgo. One paragraph explains the sacral “yes,” perseverance, waiting, cyclical commitment, and discovery through process. Three mandalas are captioned Contagen (3), Commitment, and Industry (2).

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of Saying Yes.” A compact specification block lists the channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Leo→Virgo degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph describing this tantric fuel to persevere, the mechanical drive of the Sacral to say “yes,” the importance of waiting and correctness, and that discovery lies in the process rather than the commitment. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A pink Leo glyph appears in the header area; a large gray “29” sits at the upper left; a blue page number “29” appears near the lower margin; a thin yellow baseline runs above the footer. Side index at left reads “4 ◁ 29 ▷ 51”.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Saying Yes

Channel 46-29 Discovery  
A design of succeeding where others fail  
Harmonic Gate 46 Pushing Upward  
Center Sacral  
Circuit Sensing  
Astrologic Position 24° 30′ 00″ ♌ – 00° 07′ 30″ ♍

This is the Tantric energy to empower the Determination of the Self. It is the gate of the Sacral saying, ‘Yes’. This is  
the fuel to persevere despite the circumstances. This is the gate of loading up on piles of commitments and re-  
sponsibilities so there will be something to persevere about. The trick is to say yes to what is correct. The secret,  
as always with this Circuit, is waiting. This power to persevere is conditioned directly by the Format energy. Per-  
severance is also cyclical and what we are committed to one day, may no longer be of interest the next. The  
wave dominates. This center is not aware and the mechanical drive to say yes can be overwhelming. It is essential  
to remember, that experientially, what one commits oneself to, must be completed. After all, this is the energy of  
the channel of Discovery and the discovery does not lie in the commitment but in the process.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF CONTAGEN (3)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF COMMITMENT  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF INDUSTRY (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Leo ♌ U+264C

Virgo ♍ U+264D

Notes:  
Header/intro page for Gate 29 (Sacral, Sensing circuit). Side index: “4 ◁ 29 ▷ 51.”

## Gate 29 — THE ABYSMAL, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 29 “THE ABYSMAL” lines page. Six themes (Confusion, Overreach, Directness, Evaluation, Assessment, The draftee) with Leo→Virgo degree spans. Each line has two keyed notes marked ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment), focused on perseverance, caution, simplicity, waiting, and adapting to struggle.

Long description:  
The page title is “THE ABYSMAL.” A gold subtitle reads: “THE DEEP WITHIN THE DEEP. PERSISTENCE DESPITE DIFFICULTIES HAS ITS INEVITABLE REWARDS.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with their degree ranges. Each line presents a blue subheading followed by two bullets—exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽)—each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “29” sits at the top right; a blue page number “29” is at the lower corners; a thin yellow baseline runs near the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads preserved; italics indicate in-page emphasis):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Leo → Virgo):  
6 Confusion — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
5 Overreach — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″  
4 Directness — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
3 Evaluation — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″  
2 Assessment — 25° 16′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
1 The draftee — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 16′ 15″

The state that exists when momentum outstrips awareness.  
• ♂ ▲ Driving blind and given Mars’ energy and determination, often blind luck. The power to persevere that makes no sense.  
• 2 ▽ A tendency in confusion to withdraw rather than accept the condition and continue to persevere. The power in confusion to caution rather than saying yes.

The tendency to bite off more than one can chew.  
• ☉ ▲ The Sun exalted, where the drive is in the design and not ambition driven. The uncontrollable drive to say yes.  
• ⊕ ▽ Failed ambition. Saying yes, overextending one’s resources, and failing to persevere.

The shortest distance between two points is a straight line.  
• 2 ▲ The wisdom to use the simplest and most direct approach to solve difficulties. The power to commit oneself to the simplest and most direct process.  
• ♀ ▽ Simplicity and directness all too often seen as inharmonic and aesthetically crude. The power of directness often offends others.

In this context, properly assessed inaction.  
• ♂ ▲ Despite the urge and cost of inaction the knowledge that it is sometimes better to fight another day. The power to wait.  
• 2 ▽ A preference for withdrawal in principle with little regard for effect. The inability to make commitments. The power of caution.

Persistence tempered by caution.  
• ☉ ▲ The power of sustainment as a guiding light. Saying yes and the power to persevere.  
• ♀ ▽ A tendency to overcautiousness when persistence is perceived as adding to rather than ending disharmony. Caution in saying yes, when perseverance leads to disharmony.

The ability to adapt to struggle when necessary but not as a permanent state.  
• ♂ ▲ The innate nature to apply energy in times of war and peace. The power to persevere when necessary but not generally.  
• Ψ ▽ A deep impressionability whose mark from times of struggle may make a return to normal conditions extremely difficult. Hesitation in making commitments based on past experience.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ♀ U+2640 Venus; Ψ U+03A8 (psi/“trident” glyph); “2” \= edition’s node-like symbol used on several bullets.

# Gate 30 — The Gate of Recognition of Feelings

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 30 header page. Channel 30–41 “Recognition,” Harmonic Gate 41 “Decrease,” Center Solar Plexus, Circuit Sensing, with an astrologic span from late Aquarius into early Pisces. One long paragraph describes feeling as the emotional equivalent of realization, the hope↔pain wave, and how recognition of change must move through the emotional wave.

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of Recognition of Feelings.” Beneath the title a compact spec block lists channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and the degree range (Aquarius → Pisces). A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about Feeling as fate, thirst for change, the difference between judgment (now) and recognition (in the wave), and the poles of Pain and Hope. It emphasizes that only emotional awareness has direct access to the Throat; recognition must move through the wave, and the awareness that can recognize the feeling leaves the outcome to the Fates. Three labeled Rave mandalas line the bottom. A green Aquarius-style glyph appears in the header; a large gray “30” sits at upper left; a small blue page number “30” appears above a thin yellow baseline near the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Recognition of Feelings

Channel 30-41 Recognition  
A design of focused energy  
Harmonic Gate 41 Decrease  
Center Solar Plexus  
Circuit Sensing  
Astrologic Position 24° 30′ 00″ ♒ – 00° 07′ 30″ ♓

\[Body paragraph:\]  
This gate of Feeling is the Emotional equivalent of mental realization. Like its mirror, the 18th gate, the gate of conditioning from the parents, the 30th is the gate of Fate. Feeling is a thirst for destiny and a hunger for change. Just as judgment leads to the conceptual potential to correct, the recognized feeling leads to the conceptual potential for change. However, there are distinct differences. Judgment and the capacity to correct take place in the now or not. Recognition of the potential for change takes place in the wave. The emotional wave moves from pain to hope, and the true potential of emotional awareness lies between these highly energetic poles. It is important to realize, that only emotional awareness has direct access to the Throat that is by its very nature energized. The feeling for change must move through the wave. The feeling of Pain which initiates the yearning for change and a cursing of the Fates. The feeling of Hope which initiates the yearning for change and prays to the Fates. The potential awareness that can recognize the feeling and leaves its outcome in the hands of the Fates.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF CONTAGEN  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF FATES  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF INDUSTRY

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
♒ U+2652 Aquarius, ♓ U+2653 Pisces

Notes:  
Side index shows neighbors: “49 ◁ 30 ▷ 55.” Three mandalas appear along the bottom row.

## Gate 30 — THE CLINGING FIRE

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 30 “THE CLINGING FIRE” lines page in the Sensing circuit, showing six themes with Aquarius→Pisces degree spans and paired notes marked ▲ and ▽ about perseverance, caution, pace, acceptance, pragmatism, and composure.

Long description:  
Title “THE CLINGING FIRE.” Gold subtitle: “FREEDOM RECOGNIZED AS AN ILLUSION AND LIMITATION ACCEPTED AS A FATE.” Left margin lists lines 6→1 with degree ranges. For each line there’s a blue key phrase, then two bullets—exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽)—each prefixed by a small glyph. Large gray “30” at top-right; blue page number “30” at foot with a thin yellow baseline rule.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis):

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aquarius → Pisces):  
6 Enforcement — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
5 Irony — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″  
4 Burnout — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
3 Resignation — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″  
2 Pragmatism — 25° 26′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
1 Composure — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 26′ 15″

The discipline to maintain right action.  
• ♂ ▲ The assertiveness to purge the inferior while accepting to lead the weak. The strength to eliminate negative feelings.  
• C ▽ An inherent peacefulness that all too often will abide the presence of inferior forces. A lack of strength in eliminating negative feelings.

Recognition and dedication to transitory goals.  
• 2 ▲ The strength derived from knowledge and experience that two steps forward and one step back, is still one step forward. Each new feeling brings back an old feeling before progress can take place.  
• ω ▽ The anger engendered by the irony, frustration with its limitations and a desire to eliminate the entire process. Frustration and anger with the old feelings brought into the emotional awareness with each new experience.

An unrealistic pace that begs misfortune.  
• ω ▲ A compulsive and hyperactive nature that carries all the earmarks of burnout but doesn't necessarily arrive at it. A positive outcome is often assisted through analysis. Highly energized feelings that may lead to emotional collapse.  
• 2 ▽ Uncontrollable expansion with the inevitable bursting of the bubble. Uncontrollable feelings and accompanying emotional outbursts.

The acceptance of what is.  
• ω ▲ The awareness and manifestation of the law of regeneration. The laws of Karma, reincarnation and resurrection. The feeling to accept what is.  
• 2 ▽ The tendency by its very knowledge to encourage or avoid despair. The positive or negative feelings which come with acceptance of what is.

The balance between extremes.  
• ☉ ▲ The productive application of energy without wastage. Not wasting energy on feelings.  
• ♂ ▽ Overaggressiveness that chafes against limitations. Feelings which demand energy.

Balance in the face of disorder.  
• ☉ ▲ The maximization of limitation in all cases. Stability through feelings, no matter what the situation.  
• 2 ▽ The ability to maintain composure but at the expense of progress. Balanced through feelings but unable to let go of them.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 (exaltation); ▽ U+25BD (detriment); ♂ U+2642 Mars; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ω U+03C9 omega; “C” \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Edition note: a “2-like” node symbol appears on several bullets.

# Gate 31 — The Gate of Leading

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 31 header page. Channel 31–7 “The Alpha,” Harmonic Gate 7 “The Army,” Center Throat, Circuit Understanding, with an astrologic span in Leo. A short paragraph explains this Throat voice as “I lead,” noting it is about manifesting influence (verbal leadership) rather than direct motorized action.

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of Leading.” A compact specification block lists the channel (31–7 The Alpha), harmonic gate (7 The Army), center (Throat), circuit (Understanding), and Leo-to-Leo degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a paragraph explaining that the Throat has many voices; here it speaks “I lead.” This gate manifests influence and is not a primary action gate; only a minority of Throat gates connect directly to a motor. The remaining Throat gates are primarily verbal, so “I lead” can be little more than a spoken claim. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas: Right Angle Cross of the Unexpected (2), Juxtaposition Cross of Influence, and Left Angle Cross of the Alpha. A pink Leo glyph appears at upper right; a large gray “31” stands at upper left; a small blue page number “31” sits above a thin yellow baseline near the footer. Left gutter shows neighboring gates.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Leading

Channel 31-7 The Alpha  
For ‘good’ or ‘bad’ the design of leadership  
Harmonic Gate 7 The Army  
Center Throat  
Circuit Understanding  
Astrologic Position 02° 00′ 00″ ♌ – 07° 37′ 30″ ♌

The Throat has its many voices, here it says, ‘I lead’. This is the gate of manifesting influence. This is not a gate of  
primary action. Gates of the Throat that can be defined directly to a motor, are a minority (20→34, 45→21, 12→22  
and 35→36). The remaining seven gates are primarly for verbal manifestation. ‘I lead’ often is little more then a  
verbal claim.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE UNEXPECTED (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF INFLUENCE  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF THE ALPHA

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Leo ♌ U+264C

Notes:  
Side index shows neighbors (“56 ◁ 31 ▷ 33”). Three mandalas along the bottom with captions as listed.

## Gate 31 — INFLUENCE

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 31 “INFLUENCE” lines page (Throat • Understanding). Six themes with Leo degree spans and paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment), describing leadership-by-voice, application, self-righteousness, intent, selectivity, arrogance, and manifestation.

Long description:  
The title reads “INFLUENCE.” A gold subtitle states: “THE LAW OF FRICTION, WHETHER ACTIVE OR PASSIVE, THAT ENGENDERS TRANSFERENCE AND THUS INFLUENCE.” The left margin lists the six lines (6→1) with degree ranges. Each blue subheading is followed by two bulleted notes—one exaltation and one detriment—each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “31” appears at top right; the blue page number “31” sits near the lower corners; a thin yellow rule runs above the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Leo):  
6 Application — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
5 Self-righteousness — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″  
4 Intent — 04° 48′ 45″ – 05° 45′ 00″  
3 Selectivity — 03° 52′ 30″ – 04° 48′ 45″  
2 Arrogance — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
1 Manifestation — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″

The natural attainment of influence justified by the correctness of actions.  
• ☉ ▲ Actions which match the words and thus guarantees success. Leadership whose expression and action must be one and the same.  
• C ▽ A superficiality in application that borders on hypocrisy and is justifiably treated accordingly. The hypocrisy according to leading in words but not action.

Lack of external influence guaranteed by attitude.  
• ω ▲ A natural specialization that only develops in isolation. However, when the development is complete, the extremely difficult and generally impossible task of externalizing the influence. A specialization that demands that one leads oneself.  
• C ▽ A deep focus on personal experience that is self-fulfilling and has no external ambitions. A lack of ambition where one is content to lead oneself.

The success of influence based on how it is perceived.  
• C ▲ Public acknowledgement of a nurturing and protective influence. External positive recognition of one’s capacity to lead.  
• ♂ ▽ Perceived ego aggrandizement and attempted manipulation. External negative projection of one’s capacity to lead.

The ability to carefully assess and choose the proper influence and to tailor one’s behaviour accordingly.  
• ☉ ▲ The capacity to lead enhanced through association with the proper influences.  
• 2 ▽ Qualitative selectivity with the risk of a runaway enthusiasm that may lead to humiliation. The drive to lead that will accept any follower and the risks.

Independent action without guidance.  
• 2 ▲ The dedication to higher principles that cannot wait for consensus. Leadership that cannot wait for consensus.  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▽ A reasoned arrogance that out of nervous tension jumps the gun and often misfires. The drive for expression that cannot wait and may cost leadership.

Influence cannot exist in a vacuum.  
• ☉ ▲ The Sun does not/cannot hold back its light and thus its influence on every life. The natural expression of leadership.  
• ⊕ ▽ The absorption of light, that in darkness can only promise manifestation. The unnatural expression of leadership.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ω U+03C9 omega; “C” \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).  
Unclear glyph: line 2 detriment.

# Gate 32 — The Gate of Continuity

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 32 header page. Channel 32–54 “Transformation,” Harmonic Gate 54 “The Marrying Maiden,” Center Spleen, Circuit Ego, and a Libra degree span. One paragraph explains instinctive awareness that adapts without losing continuity, its conservative nature, the brake it puts on Gate 54’s drive, and the fear of failure.

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of Continuity.” A compact specification block lists channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and the degree range in Libra. A thin rule separates the header from one explanatory paragraph about Duration, instinctive imprinting, adaptation after change is established, and the role of stored instinctive memory in strengthening the Ego. Three labeled Rave mandalas line the bottom: “THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF MAIA (3),” “THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF CONSERVATION,” and “THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF LIMITATION (2).” A green Libra glyph appears in the header; a large gray “32” at upper left; a small blue page number “31” sits above the yellow baseline; the left gutter shows “57 ◁ 32 ▷ 50.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Continuity

Channel 32-54 Transformation  
A design of being driven  
Harmonic Gate 54 The Marrying Maiden  
Center Spleen  
Circuit Ego  
Astrologic Position 20° 45′ 00″ ♎ – 26° 22′ 30″ ♎

The awareness potential is the instinct to adapt in the now without losing continuity. This is the gate of Duration,  
where all of our instinctive imprinting is rooted. By its nature, it is extremely conservative. Where the Sensitivity  
Stream is social and communal, the Instinctive Stream is social and individual. Rooted in the Splenic system, it is  
first and foremost about individual survival on the material plane. It will not rush to embrace a revolution, but if it  
succeeds and is established in the general community, then it will instinctly adapt, maintaining its basic nature.  
This is the potential awareness of what can or cannot be transformed. It is the brake on the drive of the 54\. What is  
transformed and stored as instinctive memory, determines the ultimate strength of the Ego. Each gate of the  
Splenic Center has an underlying fear, here, it is the fear of failure. It can be a gate of prolonged indecision, the  
conservative reliance agonizing over the risks of being driven towards transformation.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF MAIA (3)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF CONSERVATION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF LIMITATION (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Libra ♎ U+264E

## Gate 32 — DURATION

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 32 “DURATION” lines page (Spleen • Ego circuit). Six line themes with Libra degree spans; each has exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽) notes about facing impermanence, adapting to circumstances, maintaining principles, continuity vs. reevaluation, restraint, and conservation.

Long description:  
Title “DURATION.” Gold subtitle: “THE ONLY THING WHICH ENDURES IS CHANGE.” Left margin lists lines 6→1 with their degree ranges. For each line there’s a blue key phrase, then two bullets—exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽)—each prefixed by a small glyph. Large gray “32” at top right; blue page number “31” at the foot with a thin yellow baseline.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Libra):  
6 Tranquillity — 25° 26′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
5 Flexibility — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 26′ 15″  
4 Right is might — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
3 Lack on continuity — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″  
2 Restraint — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
1 Conservation — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″

The need to calmly face impermanence.  
• ω ▲ An underlying acceptance of change that may or may not lead to tranquillity. The instinctive awareness to accept change and transformation.  
• Ψ ▽ Impermanence as proof of meaninglessness with its attendant manifestations, depression, delusion, and in the extreme, self-destruction. The fear engendered when change is experienced as impermanence, and the potential for depression.

Easy adaption to circumstances.  
• C ▲ The Moon exalted, where superficiality is a valuable tool that while masking the inner light permits adaption to prevailing conditions. An instinct for adaption in times of change.  
• ♂ ▽ The urge to express oneself in direct and often violent rejection of conformity. The potential of the instinct to reject adaption and conformity in times of change.

Right is might.  
• 2 ▲ Even in times of change certain underlying principles endure. The instinct to maintain one’s principles in times of change.  
• 2 ▽ Where Jupiter will establish right action in the larger social realm, Saturn’s will lead to inner strength and endurance as long as it is not threatened from the outside. The instinct to maintain one’s principles as long as one’s security is not threatened.

Lack on continuity.  
• ☿ ▲ Indecision and persistent reevaluation, that only because of its basic intelligence manages to endure. Indecision in times of transformation.  
• 2 ▽ An overreliance on traditionally legitimized standards that in times of change can be totally out of step and suffer unexpected humiliation. A lack of instinct in times of transformation.

Restraint.  
• ♀ ▲ The control of power for the benefit and enhancement of harmony. The potential for transformation that may be beneficial to others.  
• 2 ▽ A tendency in frustration, particularly from a position of strength, to social withdrawal rather than persistent control. The frustration with controls or being controlled.

Conservation.  
• ☉ ▲ A life sustaining respect and attention to all aspects of a process. The potential to develop the instinct through detailed attention to a process.  
• ♂ ▽ The uncontrollable urge to bypass essentials which inevitably leads to the disruption of continuity. The fear of lack of potential, and the corresponding lack of attention.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ω U+03C9 omega; Ψ U+03A8 psi; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ☉ U+2609 Sun; C \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).

# Gate 33 — The Gate of Privacy

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 33 header page. Channel 33–13 “The Prodigal,” Harmonic Gate 13 “The Fellowship of Man,” Center Throat, Circuit Sensing, and a Leo degree span. One paragraph explains “I remember,” retreat, privacy, revelation, and the cultural value of stored memory.

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of Privacy.” A compact specification block lists channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Leo-to-Leo degree range. Below a thin rule, a single paragraph describes this Throat voice as remembrance—“I remember”—linking it to retreat, reflection, and then sharing experience as revelation. It contrasts Gate 33 with Gate 13 and emphasizes the Sensing circuit’s role in storing cultural memory for human progress. A pink Leo glyph appears at upper right; a large gray “33” stands at upper left; a blue page number “33” sits above a gold baseline. Side index at left reads “31 ◁ 33 ▷ 7.” Three labeled Rave mandalas line the bottom: Four Ways (2), Retreat, and Refinement.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Privacy

Channel 33-13 The Prodigal  
The design of the witness  
Harmonic Gate 13 The Fellowship of Man  
Center Throat  
Circuit Sensing  
Astrologic Position 07° 37′ 30″ ♌ – 13° 15′ 00″ ♌

The Throat has many voices, here it says, ‘I remember’. It is the voice of the Witness captured in the biblical story  
of the prodigal child, who leaves home and gives up their birthright to wander and learn and then returns many  
years later much the wiser to be accepted back into the fold so that the wisdom garnered in the experience can  
be shared through remembrance. This is the expression of the Sensing Circuit. This is the gate of Retreat, of the  
need for privacy. Before a new cycle can begin, a period of reflection on the old is necessary. Unlike the 13th  
gate, where the secrets are locked away in memory, the 33rd gate is in the Throat and its nature is to share its  
experience and to reveal the secrets. This is the gate of revelation. Despite what appears to be an ordeal, the  
Sensing Circuit is of enormous value to Human Progress. The storing of cultural memory has resulted in the ex-  
ponential expansion of Human capacity. Without the storyteller or the historian, every new generation would have  
to start from scratch.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE FOUR WAYS (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF RETREAT  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF REFINEMENT

## Gate 33 — RETREAT

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 33 “RETREAT” lines page for the Throat (Sensing circuit), showing six line themes with Leo degree spans and paired notes marked ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment) about privacy, timing, dignity, spirit, surrender, and wise avoidance.

Long description:  
Title “RETREAT,” with a gold subtitle: “ACTIVE WITHDRAWAL AND THE TRANSFORMATION OF A WEAK POSITION INTO A STRENGTH.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with their degree ranges. Each line presents a blue key phrase and two notes—an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽)—each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “33” sits at top right; the blue page number “33” appears near the lower corners; a thin gold baseline runs above the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Leo):  
6 Disassociation — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
5 Timing — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″  
4 Dignity — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
3 Spirit — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″  
2 Surrender — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
1 Avoidance — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″

The ability to let go.  
• ☉ ▲ The will to concentrate on revitalization and not be handicapped by persistent recriminations. The ability to let go in retreat, to enjoy privacy.  
• 2 ▽ The ability to shed the larger framework that precipitated the retreat, leaving nagging doubts that hamper revitalization. The inability to completely let go.

Timing.  
• ω ▲ As important as the timing itself, is the ability to keep secret one’s intentions until the appropriate moment. The ability to keep one’s intentions secret.  
• 2 ▽ The tendency to want others to participate in the selection of the timing, which can lead to confusion. Without a sense of timing, letting others in on the secret prematurely with resulting confusion.

A lack of turmoil in retreat.  
• ω ▲ The underlying faith in resurrection that turns retreat into an opportunity for renewal and regeneration. The healthy retreat for regeneration.  
• Ψ ▽ Without the guiding light of resurrection, the inevitable dissolution that leads to degeneration. Forced to retreat, and unable to see its regenerative qualities.

The attitude that turns retreat into victory.  
• 2 ▲ The responsible and principled retreat based on preservation but with the determination to persevere. Privacy as a path to success.  
• ♂ ▽ A lack of responsibility in retreat. The bridge burner. A drive for privacy that will cut off its relationships, often abruptly.

The recognition that surrender to superior forces can be an opportunity to expand one’s own strengths and eventually triumph.  
• 2 ▲ Embracing powerful forces in order to lay the foundation for future success.  
• Ψ ▽ Unlike the reasoned and calculated surrender above, the deeper and personal surrender. The feeling that one’s original position was a delusion and the impressionability that makes might right. A public embrace of powerful forces, and a private resentment of their power.

The wisdom in a weak position to recognize that survival demands complete withdrawal.  
• ☉ ▲ Retreating when one realizes that they are in a weak position.  
• ♂ ▽ Where courage is just plain foolishness. Unable to retreat when overwhelmed by stimulation.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
☉ U+2609 Sun; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ω U+03C9 omega; Ψ U+03A8 psi; “2” \= edition’s node symbol; ▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment.

# Gate 34 — The Gate of Power

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 34 header page. Channels shown: 34-57 “Power,” 20-34 “Charisma,” and 10-34 “Exploration.” Harmonic gates: 57 “The Gentle,” 20 “Contemplation,” 10 “Treading.” Center Sacral, Circuit Centering. Astrologic span 00° 07′ 30″ ♐ to 05° 45′ 00″ ♐. A long paragraph describes the Sacral’s unconditional power and its expression through Charisma; three mandalas below are captioned Sleeping Phoenix (4), Power, and Duality (2).

Long description:  
Clean white layout with “The Gate of Power” title and a compact spec block listing three channels that 34 can fuel, its harmonic gates, Sacral center, Centering circuit, and a Sagittarius degree range. A thin rule separates this header from a dense body paragraph about 34 as unconditional, always-available Sacral power; its “grunting/growling” voice; independence and energy of conviction; and how 34 can drive three channels (Power/Charisma/Exploration). It stresses the need for guidance from awareness (57) lest power run amok, and explains Charisma (20-34) as the compulsion for thoughts to become deeds—especially destabilizing when connected to the Throat. The text clarifies that “thoughts” refers to any awareness center (Ajna/Spleen/Solar Plexus) and warns that Charisma without an awareness center is open to conditioning, turning awareness into deeds by instant identification. The irony noted: highly charismatic people often become profoundly manipulated. Bottom row shows three Rave mandalas labeled “THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE SLEEPING PHOENIX (4),” “THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF POWER,” and “THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF DUALITY (2).” Left gutter index reads “14 ◁ 34 ▷ 9.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Power

Channel 34-57 Power, A design of an archetype  
20-34 Charisma, A design where thoughts must be deeds  
10-34 Exploration, A design of following one’s convictions

Harmonic Gates 57 The Gentle  
20 Contemplation  
10 Treading  
Center Sacral  
Circuit Centering  
Astrologic Position 00° 07′ 30″ ♐ – 05° 45′ 00″ ♐

The only purely asexual gate out of the Sacral center. This is a gate of pure unconditioned power. It is power ready for  
display, available for use. The 34th gate is one of the four gates of the Integration Channels (20,10 & 57). As energy, it is  
powerful enough to fuel three different channels (Power, Charisma & Exploration). Such power is never to be underesti-  
mated. This is the essential fuel of Individuation. The Sacral is second only to the Throat Center in complexity. Like the  
Throat, it both moves us and provides us with a voice. The movement is rooted in what is termed ‘sex drive’. It is in fact a  
repetitive frequency. The defined Sacral takes its blind walks, shuffles its papers and cleans the house compulsively.  
This is frequency and not the metamorphosis possible through the Throat. The Sacral also has its voices, the moans and  
sighs. The 34th gate is the grunting / growling voice. The power to be independent and the energy of conviction.

It is the Power of the Great which determines whether Individuation can be maintained. This is an enormous energy re-  
source and not aware. Its power allows it to fuel three different channels. The empowerment of the Human Archetype is  
its primary function and the definition to 57 is essential. This power without the guidance of awareness can wreck havoc.  
It is through the Channel of Charisma, a design where thoughts must become deeds, that its destabilizing impact is so  
common. The power of 34 is relentless, when defined to the Throat, it must manifest. Charisma is what we call the re-  
lease of Individual Power in the Now. In the design keynote, the term ‘thoughts’ is used. Do not misinterpret this illusion to  
the mind only. It is in fact a reference to any of the three awareness centers (Ajna / Mind, Spleen / Body and Solar Plexus  
/ Spirit). The Channel of Charisma not defined to an Awareness center is open to conditioning from any awareness and  
in the instant of identification with the awareness, it will turn the awareness into deeds. The irony is that charismatic indi-  
viduals, who seem to epitomize personal power, are so often profoundly manipulated in their lives.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE SLEEPING PHOENIX (4)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF POWER  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF DUALITY (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Sagittarius ♐ U+2650

Notes:  
Side index: “14 ◁ 34 ▷ 9.” Three mandalas along the bottom with captions as listed.

## Gate 34 — THE POWER OF THE GREAT

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 34 “THE POWER OF THE GREAT” lines page for the Sacral (Centering circuit). Six line themes with Sagittarius degree spans, each with paired notes (▲ exaltation, ▽ detriment) about restraint, annihilation, victory, display of power, momentum, and bullying.

Long description:  
Title reads “THE POWER OF THE GREAT.” The gold subtitle states: “POWER IS ONLY GREAT WHEN ITS DISPLAY OR USE SERVES THE COMMON GOOD.” The left margin lists the six lines (6→1) with degree ranges. For each line there is a blue key phrase, followed by two bullets—an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽)—each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “34” sits at top right; a blue page number “34” is printed near the lower corners; a thin yellow baseline runs above the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Sagittarius):  
6 Common sense — 04° 48′ 45″ – 05° 45′ 00″  
5 Annihilation — 03° 52′ 30″ – 04° 48′ 45″  
4 Triumph — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
3 Machismo — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″  
2 Momentum — 01° 03′ 45″ – 02° 00′ 00″  
1 The bully — 00° 07′ 30″ – 01° 03′ 45″

Knowing when enough is enough.  
• ⊕ ▲ The good sense when having bitten off more than one can chew to spit part of it out. The restriction of the release of power when one does not have the strength to maintain it.  
• 2 ▽ Enthusiasm overriding better judgment, invariably leads to complications. The lack of restrictions that can sap one’s strength.

The total elimination of resistance.  
• ♂ ▲ The power to destroy completely and once this is accomplished, the ability to transfer the power to normal purposes. The resistance to release power other than when necessary.  
• C ▽ A difficulty in shedding the patterns established to deal with resistance. Symbolized by its phases, the feeling that nothing is permanently annihilated. The discomfort with the need to always release power.

The freedom in absolute victory to unlimited use of power.  
• ω ▲ A tendency in victory to restrain excessive power in favour of more subtle and covert styles. The inherent confidence to use power subtly.  
• ♂ ▽ Ego gratification in the unrestrained use of power with impunity, inevitably leading to abuse. A lack of confidence that can lead to abuse of power.

The indiscriminate display of power.  
• 2 ▲ The great malefic, where machismo unfortunately is invariably backed up by power. This is an exaltation only in that it is natural and not contrived. The display of power that defines any role.  
• ♀ ▽ A reasoned and calculated display. The communication of disinformation. The calculated display of power in order to define the role.

Momentum.  
• ♂ ▲ The ability, when victory is in sight, not to loose perspective. The power that grows when victory is in sight.  
• ♀ ▽ A tendency to be emotionally carried away by the smell of victory. The power for growth limited by impatience.

The indiscriminate use of power.  
• 2 ▲ The less negative, the resort to power as a manifestation of frustration. The energy to display power as a response to frustration.  
• ω ▽ The comeuppance that is the inevitable destiny of the bully. The ever present risk of retaliation to power displays.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ω U+03C9 omega; “C” \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); “2” \= edition-specific node symbol.

Notes:  
Degrees span Sagittarius 00° 07′ 30″ → 05° 45′ 00″. Large gray “34” at top right; blue page number “34”; yellow baseline near footer.

# Gate 35 — The Gate of Change

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 35 header page. Channel 35–36 “Transitoriness,” Harmonic Gate 36 “The Darkening of the Light,” Center Throat, Circuit Sensing, with a Gemini degree span. A paragraph explains cyclical change, the appetite for new experiences, “I feel it’s time for a change,” and the warning against goal-orientation in this channel of expectations. Three mandalas are captioned Consciousness (2), Experience, and Separation.

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of Change.” A compact specification block lists the channel (35–36 Transitoriness — a design of a “Jack of all Trades”), harmonic gate (36 The Darkening of the Light), center (Throat), circuit (Sensing), and an astrologic position range in Gemini (quarter mark I). Below a thin rule is a single explanatory paragraph about the Sensing circuit’s cyclical nature (beginnings–middles–ends), Gate 35’s role in change and in satiating the appetite for change, its experiential talent mirroring Gate 16, the voice “I feel… time for a change,” and why this channel of expectations can’t be goal-oriented. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. Left gutter index shows “16 ◁ 35 ▷ 45.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Change

Channel 35-36 Transitoriness  
A design of a ‘Jack of all Trades’  
Harmonic Gate 36 The Darkening of the Light  
Center Throat  
Circuit Sensing  
Astrologic Position 11° 22′ 30″ ♊ – 17° 00′ 00″ ♊ \[I\]

The Sensing Circuit is rooted in its format of cyclical energy. It always requires beginnings, middles and ends. The  
35th gate is a key to this energy functioning properly. This is a gate of change conditioned by the cycle. This is the  
gate of satiating the appetite for change. This is the expression of the Channel of Transitoriness, a design of a  
Jack of all Trades. Like its mirror, the 16th gate of skills, the 35th gate is experientially talented. Its voice says,  
‘I feel’ and usually, ‘I feel it’s time for a change.’ This is the channel of expectations but there is no light at the end  
of this tunnel, none. ‘I feel if I do this, I’ll get that’ is not a formula and an expression of fact. It is a hope that will  
always lead to pain. This is not a design that can afford to be goal oriented. This is a gate of the ‘wheel’ turning.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF CONSCIOUSNESS (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF EXPERIENCE  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF SEPARATION

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Gemini ♊ U+264A

Notes:  
Header/intro page for Gate 35 (Throat • Sensing). Quarter mark: “I”. Side index: “16 ◁ 35 ▷ 45.”

## Gate 35 — PROGRESS)

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 35 “PROGRESS” lines page (Throat • Sensing). Six line themes with degree spans in Gemini, each showing exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽) notes on correction, altruism, hunger for progress, collaboration, creative block, and humility.

Long description:  
The title reads “PROGRESS.” The gold subtitle states: “BY DESIGN, PROGRESS CANNOT EXIST IN A VACUUM AND IS DEPENDENT ON INTERACTION.” The left margin lists the six lines (6→1) with degree ranges. Each line has a blue subheading and two notes—an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽)—each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “35” is at the top right; a blue page number “35” appears near the lower corners; a thin yellow baseline runs above the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads preserved; italics indicate in-page emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Gemini):  
6 Rectification — 16° 03′ 45″ – 17° 00′ 00″  
5 Altruism — 15° 07′ 30″ – 16° 03′ 45″  
4 Hunger — 14° 11′ 15″ – 15° 07′ 30″  
3 Collaboration — 13° 15′ 00″ – 14° 11′ 15″  
2 Creative block — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
1 Humility — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″

The energy to correct.  
• 2 ▲ The process of crystallization coupled with ambition that assures timely and effective correction. Progressive change that results from correction.  
• ♂ ▽ A destructive tendency that in its personal application may be the necessary severity to ensure correction but when applied generally, it will meet with resistance and tend to reinforce rather than rectify situations. Correction which brings change through severity and even destruction, and will always meet resistance.

The sacrifice of personal for communal progress.  
• (glyph unclear) ▲ The principles of interaction and harmony communicated successfully for the benefit of the whole. Progressive communication that can bring beneficial change to the whole.  
• 2 ▽ Jupiter in detriment, though altruistic and cooperative in general, a personal regret that in interaction a greater personal expansion had been lost. Progressive communication, but always the sense that personal progress has been sacrificed.

The insatiable appetite for progress.  
• C ▲ Less severe. Symbolized by the phases of the Moon, the drive may be obsessive when the Moon is full, but like the fading of the Moon the obsession abates. Change for change’s sake. The drive lessens with age.  
• ♂ ▽ The abuse of position to accumulate perks with the inevitable retribution from those unfairly deprived. The drive for progress that will ultimately step on toes and lead to recrimination.

The whole is greater than the sum of its parts.  
• 2 ▲ Benefic encouragement of others that expands personal as well as communal progress. The ability to bring progressive change into others’ lives.  
• C ▽ The need to be the center that ignores the importance of others. The need to be the center for progress.

Lack of inspiration which stops progress.  
• ♀ ▲ Attunement with the vagaries of the muse and the recognition, creativity is energy that ebbs and flows. Creativity and the muse will always come and go.  
• C ▽ The need to take action, however mundane, to overcome the feeling of emptiness. The actions being uninspired do not further progress. The need for change and the fear of stasis.

The ability to accept rejection.  
• ♀ ▲ The artist that accepts rejection as part of the process. Acceptance of change and rejection as part of the process.  
• Ψ ▽ A self-destructive reaction to rejection. Loss of worth. Change and rejection as humiliation.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♀ U+2640 Venus; Ψ U+03A8 psi; “C” \= stylized Moon mark (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D). Edition note: a “2-like” node symbol appears on several bullets.

# Gate 36 — The Gate of Crisis

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 36 header page. Channel 35–36 “Transitoriness,” Harmonic Gate 35 “Progress,” Center Solar Plexus, Circuit Sensing, with a Pisces degree span. One paragraph explains emotional recognition leading to change, the Pain & Hope wave, direct Throat access (action), why 36 is “crisis,” and how shifts of feeling end cycles in the Abstract/Sensing process.

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of Crisis.” A compact specification block beneath the title lists the channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and the astrologic position (Pisces). A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about recognition through feeling, the only two conceptualizing gates with direct Throat access (22/36), action conditioned by the Pain & Hope wave, destabilization, and the witness role of the Abstract circuit. Bottom row shows three labeled Rave mandalas. A purple Pisces glyph sits at upper right; a large gray “36” appears at upper left; a small blue page number “36” sits above a gold baseline. Left gutter shows “12 ◁ 36 ▷ 25.”

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Crisis

Channel 35-36 Transitoriness  
A design of a ‘Jack of all Trades’  
Harmonic Gate 35 Progress  
Center Solar Plexus  
Circuit Sensing  
Astrologic Position 22° 37′ 30″ ♓ – 28° 15′ 00″ ♓

The potential of recognition through feelings leads to the possibility of change. No gates are under greater pres-  
sure than 22 Grace and 36 The Darkening of the Light. These are the only two conceptualizing gates which have  
direct access to the throat and can manifest directly as Action. Since it is emotional energy, the Pain & Hope wave  
that conditions this definition rather than the possible emotional awareness that lies within the wave, the outcome  
is often destabilizing for the individual and for others. Only in this way is the 36th gate the gate of crisis. Change  
and changes in feeling are natural to the Abstract process. This is an experiential circuit. The ultimate role is to be  
a witness, to remember and reflect. The shift of feelings determines the ending of a cycle, nothing more, but this  
can only be recognized through awareness. The energy overwhelms this possibility, and change becomes either  
something that is dreaded or embraced and fuels a life of expectations and inevitable crises.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF EDEN  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF CRISIS  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF THE PLANE

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Pisces ♓ U+2653

Notes:  
Side index: “12 ◁ 36 ▷ 25.” Throat-access conceptualizing gates named: 22 (Grace) and 36 (The Darkening of the Light).

## Gate 36 — THE DARKENING OF THE LIGHT

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 36 “THE DARKENING OF THE LIGHT” lines page (Solar Plexus • Sensing). Six themes with Pisces degree spans; each has exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽) notes about justice, survival “underground,” espionage, transition, support, and resistance during cycles of decline.

Long description:  
Title: “THE DARKENING OF THE LIGHT.” Gold subtitle: “THE RULE OF CYCLES IN WHICH DECLINE IS A NATURAL BUT NOT ENDURING STAGE.” Left margin lists lines 6→1 with degree ranges. Each line presents a blue subheading and two bullets—exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽)—each with a small glyph. A large gray “36” sits at top-right; blue page number “36” appears at the bottom above a gold baseline.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Pisces):  
6 Justice — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
5 The underground — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″  
4 Espionage — 25° 26′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
3 Transition — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 26′ 15″  
2 Support — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
1 Resistance — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″

The inevitable survival of right.  
• ▲ The knowledge and attendant faith that the powers of darkness eventually destroy themselves. ‘Those whom the Gods wish to destroy, they first make mad.’ The correctness of crisis when it arises out of pure feelings.  
• ▽ A profound sorrow and cynicism, that is not diminished by the understanding, that darkness is self-annihilating. The sorrow or cynicism that comes with seeing that despite the correctness of the feelings, there is always crisis.

Perfected survival regardless of conditions.  
• ▲ Immunity to crisis as both generator and survivor.  
• ▽ A nervousness that can lead to self-betrayal. Self-betraying nervousness in times of crisis.

The ability to prepare for and anticipate decline through the accumulation of secret or privileged information.  
• ▲ The realization that knowledge both covert and esoteric is necessary, if one is to be prepared for crisis and change.  
• ▽ The tendency in recognizing the strengths of the opposition to accept the inevitability of decline and rather than resist, to offer one’s services to guarantee survival. The double agent. Crisis knowledge that is available to others for a price.

The point at which decline has exhausted its power.  
• ▲ The ability to establish a new order out of the ashes of the old. The emotional depth to endure crisis and embrace change.  
• ▽ The tendency in renewal to try to assimilate the remains of the old order into the new with the risk that it may rise again over the new once it has regained its strengths. The embrace of the change, but with feelings that will not let go of the past.

Assistance to others in times of decline.  
• ▲ The application of imagination to schemes which benefit others. Feelings that can benefit others in times of crisis.  
• ▽ The more practical and individual assistance but only when unavoidable and not as a general principle. Selective assistance in times of crisis.

The energy and determination to persevere in the face of opposition.  
• ▲ The emotional power to handle crisis.  
• ▽ An over-principled resistance that rather than being selective in resistance and thus less at risk, will maintain normal patterns and incur opposition. A resistance to change that will always bring crisis.

# Gate 37 — The Gate of Friendship

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 37 header page. Channel 37–40 “Community,” Harmonic Gate 40 “Deliverance,” Center Solar Plexus, Circuit Ego (Tribal), with a Pisces degree span. One paragraph explains communal support, loyalty/reciprocity, the “gate of the Mouth,” and the role of touch in emotional awareness.

Long description:  
This gate is presented as the most communal of all gates— the transpersonal expression of sensitivity in the Tribe. It offers support and friendship to others and focuses sensitivity for communal expression. Within the Tribal grouping (Ego and Defense), communities are established and maintained. After Defense satisfies the sexual imperative, community becomes essential; biologically Sensitivity is about food and feeding the Tribe. Gate 37 is the gate of the Mouth. Its friendship always carries an underlying bargain: loyalty seeking loyalty in return. The emotional wave here yields hope/pain cycles around friendship and promises kept/broken. It is the gate of the Sense of Touch— outstretched arms ready to embrace the outsider. The awareness lies in the “touch,” not in the energy to embrace.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Friendship

Channel 37-40 Community  
A design of being a part seeking a whole  
Harmonic Gate 40 Deliverance  
Center Solar Plexus  
Circuit Ego  
Astrologic Position 05° 45′ 00″ ♓ – 11° 22′ 30″ ♓

This is the most communal of all the gates. It is the transpersonal expression of the potential sensitivity of Revolu-  
tion. This gate is also the focusing of sensitivity for communal expression. This gate is the possibility to offer sup-  
port and friendship to others. The Tribal Circuit group (Ego and Defense) are the most intensely social. They es-  
tablish, support and maintain our communities. Once the sexual imperative has been satisfied (Defense), the  
community is an essential development. Sensitivity, biologically is about food. The Tribe must be fed. The 37th  
gate is the gate of the Mouth. The friendship offered by this gate can never be free of an underlying bargain. It is  
always seeking loyalty and looking for a promise of loyalty in return. The wave of emotional awareness leads to  
the hope and pain cycle of friendships, loyalty/ disloyalty, promise/ broken promise. This is the gate of the Sense  
of Touch. It is the outstretched arms ready to embrace the outsider. The possibility of awareness in this gate lies  
in the ‘touch’ not in the energy to embrace.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF PLANNING  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF BARGAINS  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF MIGRATION

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Pisces ♓ U+2653

Notes:  
Tribal/Ego stream emphasis on loyalty and reciprocity; “gate of the Mouth” and the Sense of Touch.

  "notes": "Gate of the Mouth; friendship framed by loyalty/reciprocity; awareness in the 'touch'."  
}

## Gate 37 — THE FAMILY, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 37 “THE FAMILY” lines page for the Tribal/Ego stream (Solar Plexus), showing six themes (Purpose, Love, Leadership by example, Evenhandedness, Responsibility, The mother/father) with Pisces degree spans. Notes emphasize values, harmony, exemplary leadership, order, individual responsibility, and a respected parental position.

Long description:  
Title “THE FAMILY,” with the subtitle: “THE MANIFESTATION MACRO- AND MICRO-COSMICALLY OF THE ORGANIC NATURE OF COMMUNITIES.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with degree ranges. Each line has a blue key phrase and two bullets—exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽)—each prefixed by a small glyph. A large gray “37” appears at top-right; a blue page number “37” sits at the bottom above a gold baseline.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Pisces):  
6 Purpose — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
5 Love — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″  
4 Leadership by example — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
3 Evenhandedness — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″  
2 Responsibility — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
1 The mother/father — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″

The energy to maintain the family is enhanced by recognizing its values.  
• ♀ ▲ The gift of not only recognizing the inner meaning of the family but an appreciation for its values. The possibility of extending friendships through the appreciation of its value.  
• ☿ ▽ A need for diversity that may ignore the achievements of the family in favour of withdrawal. The need for diversity that despite appreciation will prefer casual friendships.

Natural and unaffected devotion to the family.  
• ♀ ▲ Natural harmony and perfected sharing. Natural harmony and sharing possible through friendship.  
• ♂ ▽ Emotional dependency that often turns love into hatred. The possibility of dependency turning love into hate.

Any member of the family may take on a leading role through exemplary behaviour.  
• C ▲ The manifestation of the highest principles in everyday and practical affairs. The possibility of the highest principles in all relationships resulting in a leadership role.  
• 2 ▽ A conservatism that generally only accepts the leadership of the father, who may or may not be a valued role model. The possibility of being insensitive to the leadership of anyone other than those accepted by tradition.

The success of any group is dependent on maintaining order.  
• 2 ▲ The ability to judge what behaviour is appropriate and to react in a balanced manner to transgressions. The possibility to have the sensitivity to know what behaviour is appropriate in a relationship.  
• ♂ ▽ An ironic tendency to either test incessantly the borders of acceptability or to respond to the very same tendency in others with severity. The possible lack of sensitivity to what behaviour is appropriate.

The recognition of the principle of individual responsibility as the foundation for successful cooperation.  
• 2 ▲ The understanding of the principle of individual responsibility as the foundation for successful cooperation. The possibility of friendship through individual responsibility.  
• ☿ ▽ A tendency to point out the responsibility of others. The possibility that friendship will lead to pointing out the responsibilities of others.

A position of inherent respect that ensures a focus for the development of guidelines.  
• ♀ ▲ Harmony is the key to the successful maintenance of relationships. It is only through harmony that the beauty and the values of the family can endure. Friendship that is rooted in sensitivity and ensures harmony. No polarity.  
• ▽ No planet in detriment.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
♀ U+2640 Venus; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ☿ U+263F Mercury; C \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/ U+263E); “2” \= edition-specific node symbol; ▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment.
