# Gate 38 — The Gate of the Fighter

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 38 header page. Channel 38–28 “Struggle,” Harmonic Gate 28 “Preponderance of the Great,” Center Root, Circuit Knowing, with an astrologic span in Capricorn. One paragraph describes Root pressure to fight for the value of life, the connection to Splenic fears, challenge vs. play with Gate 28, stubbornness that overcomes odds, and how struggle is often individual and misunderstood.

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of the Fighter.” A compact spec block lists channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Capricorn degree range. A thin rule separates this header from a single explanatory paragraph about the Root as pressure (not awareness), fuel to fight/struggle as energy, the beginning of the Stream of Intuition here, and the Root→Spleen channels being difficult yet healthy. The text explains that this fuel combats the fear of death, frames 38 as the struggle to know that life is of value, and notes that the struggle is often individual and misunderstood. Through 38 we feel pressure to challenge, met in 28 by willingness to play; with definition the “game” can become serious—even life-threatening (see 28.6 Neptune in detriment). Stubbornness can overcome odds; with the Spleen as our well-being center, this definition makes the fighter’s stubborn struggle ultimately healthy—if it’s your design, struggle is the way to know.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Fighter

Channel 38-28 Struggle  
A design of stubbornness  
Harmonic Gate 28 Preponderance of the Great  
Center Root  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 09° 30′ 00″ ♑ – 15° 07′ 30″ ♑

The Root is a Pressure Center, not an Awareness. The fuel to fight, to struggle is an energy. The Stream of Intui-  
tion begins in this gate. The channels which connect the Root to the Spleen are by their nature difficult while at the  
same time being healthy.

The Spleen is a center of underlying fears. This fuel to fight is the essential energy necessary to combat our fear of  
death. This channel is about the struggle to know that life is of value. Since this is a fuel of the Knowing Circuit, the  
struggle ultimately is individual. Often the fighter is shunned as an outsider and their struggling, deeply misunder-  
stood. Through the 38th Gate, we have the pressure to challenge, met in the 28th Gate, by the willingness to play.  
Through the definition the game playing can become very serious, even life threatening, see 28\. 6 Neptune in  
detriment. Here, it is the stubbornness which can overcome the odds. Remember that the Splenic center is our  
center for well being. To have this definition, to be stubborn, to struggle, is ultimately healthy. If it is your design,  
then struggle is the only way to know.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF TENSION (4)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF OPPOSITION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF INDIVIDUALISM (2)

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):

Capricorn ♑ U+2651

Notes:  
Side index in the gutter shows neighboring gates: “58 ◁ 38 ▷ 54.” Blue page number “38”; gold baseline near footer.

## Gate 38 — OPPOSITION, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 38 “OPPOSITION” lines page for the Root (Knowing circuit). Six themes—Misunderstanding, Alienation, Investigation, Alliance, Politeness, Qualification—each with Capricorn degree spans and paired notes marked by ▲ (exaltation) and ▽ (detriment). The page frames opposition as preserving individual integrity against detrimental forces.

Long description:  
Title: “OPPOSITION.” Gold subtitle: “THE ABILITY TO PRESERVE INDIVIDUAL INTEGRITY THROUGH OPPOSITION TO DETRIMENTAL FORCES.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with degree ranges. Each line presents a blue subheading and two bullet notes (exaltation/detriment) with small glyphs. A large gray “38” is at top right; the blue page number “38” appears near the lower corners; a thin gold baseline runs above the footer.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Capricorn):  
6 Misunderstanding — 14° 11′ 15″ – 15° 07′ 30″  
5 Alienation — 13° 15′ 00″ – 14° 11′ 15″  
4 Investigation — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
3 Alliance — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″  
2 Politeness — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
1 Qualification — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″

Opposition without basis.  
• 2 ▲ Eventual crystallization which clears up misunderstandings. Energy which attracts misunderstanding, and the stubbornness to oppose.  
• ⊕ ▽ The recognition that misunderstanding is at the root of opposition but the insistence, that it is the misunderstanding of the opposing forces. Where misunderstanding is at the root of struggle, the energy of stubbornness to insist and maintain one’s position.

A stage in opposition when one is totally isolated.  
• 2 ▲ The ambition and concentration to endure. The energy to stubbornly fight alone.  
• ω ▽ Alienation experienced as painful confinement that by its very nature blinds one to the possibility that there are those at some point who may come to one’s aid. The energy for stubbornness is so strong that it is difficult to recognize, that there are others that can help in times of struggle.

Analysis which strengthens opposition.  
• ω ▲ The detective who develops or the insider who accepts a joint opposition whose success can only be based on trust. The energy to recognize who can be of value in times of struggle.  
• ♂ ▽ The aggressiveness in opposition that prejudices investigation and limits trustworthiness. The power of adrenalin to provoke aggressiveness.

The ability to sustain through integration with similar forces.  
• ☉ ▲ The ability to sustain through integration with similar forces, thus ensuring the vitality to persevere. The energy to integrate with others in times of struggle.  
• ⊕ ▽ Selfish alliance which saps the energy of one’s partners, in order to ensure personal vitality. The energy to selfishly use others in times of struggle.

Opposition that does not transgress normal codes of behaviour.  
• ω ▲ The value of discretion. The energy for the intuitive awareness for discretion.  
• C ▽ An over-politeness that is both servile and obviously superficial and defeats its very purpose. The energy for over-politeness in times of struggle.

Tempering opposition based on the circumstances.  
• Ψ ▲ A psychic attunement that guarantees proper action. The psychic gift of knowing when and how to fight.  
• ♂ ▽ The tendency to oppose as a general rule. The drive to fight as a general rule.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 (exaltation); ▽ U+25BD (detriment); 2 \= edition’s node symbol; ⊕ U+2295 (Earth); ω U+03C9 (omega); ♂ U+2642 (Mars); ☉ U+2609 (Sun); Ψ U+03A8 (psi); C \= stylized Moon mark used in this edition (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E).

# Gate 39 — The Gate of the Provocateur

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 39 header page. Channel 39–55 “Emoting” (design of moodiness), Harmonic Gate 55 “Abundance,” Center Root, Circuit Knowing, astrologic span in Aquarius. One long paragraph explains provocation as a combative fuel seeking spirit, the evolution of Emotional Awareness, mood-wave dynamics (hope↔pain), and why patience with the Solar Plexus wave is essential.

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of the Provocateur.” A compact specification block lists the channel (39–55 Emoting), harmonic gate (55 Abundance), center (Root), circuit (Knowing), and an Aquarius-to-Aquarius degree range. A thin rule separates the header from a single explanatory paragraph about Obstruction as fuel to provoke/meet provocation in service of finding spirit; the emergence of the Solar Plexus as an awareness center; moodiness as energy (not awareness); and how misreading the mood cycle can distort behavior. Bottom row: three labeled Rave mandalas — Right Angle Cross of Tension (2), Juxtaposition Cross of Provocation, Left Angle Cross of Individualism. Left gutter shows neighbors. Blue page number “39” and a gold baseline at the foot.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of the Provocateur

Channel 39-55 Emoting  
A design of moodiness  
Harmonic Gate 55 Abundance  
Center Root  
Circuit Knowing  
Astrologic Position 09° 30′ 00″ ♒ – 15° 07′ 30″ ♒

Like its mirror, the 38th gate of the Fighter (they are in exact opposition in the Zodiac), Obstruction is a combative  
fuel. Sometime between the birth of Buddha and Jesus, the Solar Plexus Center began to evolve into our third  
Awareness center. The Awareness of the ‘Spirit’. Rarely experienced in our time, Emotional Awareness is our col-  
lective destiny. The energy to provoke is an essential ingredient It is in the design that the individual must fight, is  
provoked and must provoke to find the spirit. The provocateur is seeking out the spirit, trying to provoke it into  
expression. Since this gate is always looking for its spirit, this fuel also gives it the energy to persevere, to deal  
with the moods that this process releases. With this energy, whoever you can provoke is not for you\! The journey  
is towards the spirit. This channel is a Design of Moodiness. The mood swings, from hope to pain, is the energy of  
the Solar Plexus and not its potential awareness. Patience is the key. If the mood cycle is not seen for what it is,  
the wave frequency of the Solar Plexus, then a result may be ‘eating’ disorders.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF TENSION (2)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF PROVOCATION  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF INDIVIDUALISM

## Gate 39 — OBSTRUCTION, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 39 “OBSTRUCTION” lines page (Root • Knowing). Six lines explore how obstruction provokes analysis, assessment, and reevaluation—channeling pressure into problem-solving, bypassing, timing, responsibility, direct confrontation, and strategic disengagement.

Long description:  
Title “OBSTRUCTION” with the subtitle: “THE VALUE OF OBSTRUCTION IN PROVOKING ANALYSIS, ASSESSMENT AND REEVALUATION.” The left margin lists lines 6→1 with Aquarius degree spans. Each line supplies a blue key phrase and two notes—exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽)—with small glyphs indicating planetary/mood signatures. Large gray “39” at the top right; blue page number “39” at the bottom above a gold baseline.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aquarius):  
6 Misunderstanding — 14° 11′ 15″ – 15° 07′ 30″  
5 Single-mindedness — 13° 15′ 00″ – 14° 11′ 15″  
4 Temperance — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
3 Responsibility — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″  
2 Confrontation — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
1 Disengagement — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″

The natural gift for solving problems.  
• C ▲ Practicality and the ability to guide and nurture others. The energy to provoke others by attempting to solve their problems.  
• ♂ ▽ Ego dominance that generally abuses this gift for self-aggrandizement and personal ambition rather than for the benefit of others. The emotional drive to dominate that provokes others.

The rare ability not to overcome but to bypass obstacles.  
• Ψ ▲ The imagination to establish new patterns that make the obstacles irrelevant. The imaginative energy to provoke through bypassing obstacles.  
• ♂ ▽ Simple hardheadedness. Though less exalted, it can often result in success and gather support by its very determination. The energy to provoke through hardheadedness.

Careful evaluation and assessment before taking action.  
• C ▲ The values of feelings and instincts in establishing the proper timing. The energy to provoke at exactly the right time.  
• ☉ ▽ The faulty belief that willpower alone can overcome any obstacle regardless of circumstances. The energy to provoke that ignores circumstances.

The avoidance of confronting obstacles if failure puts others at risk.  
• 2 ▲ The great benefic whose first consideration is for the larger framework. The energy to provoke through self-sacrifice.  
• ⊕ ▽ The often disastrous assumption that others are more at risk if an obstacle is not confronted. The energy to provoke through the failure to make sacrifices.

The instinctual and direct assault of obstacles.  
• C ▲ The energy to provoke through direct assault.  
• 2 ▽ The principle of preservation that tempts one to go around an obstacle that should be tackled head-on. An emotional discomfort when direct assault is essential.

Tempering opposition based on the circumstances. (Disengagement)  
• ♂ ▲ The determination when confronted by obstacles to withdraw but only temporarily. The energy to provoke by refusing to confront obstacles.  
• ☿ ▽ Disengagement, with an accompanying indecision as to when to reengage. The refusal to confront obstacles without the capacity to know when to engage. The indecision is also a provocation.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; C \= stylized Moon mark (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); ♂ U+2642 Mars; Ψ U+03A8 psi; ☉ U+2609 Sun; ⊕ U+2295 Earth; ☿ U+263F Mercury; “2” \= edition’s node-like symbol.

# Gate 40 — The Gate of Aloneness

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 40 header page. Channel 40–37 “Community,” Harmonic Gate 37 “The Family,” Center Heart (Ego), Circuit Ego, astrologic span in Virgo. One paragraph explains willpower as fuel for community, the Ego’s solitary nature and need for loyalty, and how emotional support from one’s community pumps the will to victory.

Long description:  
White page titled “The Gate of Aloneness.” A compact spec block lists channel, harmonic gate, center, circuit, and a Virgo-to-Virgo degree range. Below a thin rule is a single explanatory paragraph: this gate is one of the three gates of social caution; it represents aloneness and the Heart/Ego circuit’s unusual nature (its awareness expresses indirectly). Deliverance (40) is willpower, not awareness; the Ego’s voice says, “My belly’s full, why should I bother feeding you?” It is always alone—even in a crowd—yet essential to community survival. Without sustained will the principles of community collapse. Gate 37 embraces Gate 40 and offers what the Ego is vulnerable to: loyalty. Emotional support fuels the will. In sport and life, performing before one’s own community, cheered on by their loyalty, pumps the will and leads to victories the whole community experiences as its own.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen):  
The Gate of Aloneness

Channel 40-37 Community  
A design of being a part seeking a whole  
Harmonic Gate 37 The Family  
Center Heart  
Circuit Ego  
Astrologic Position 05° 45′ 00″ ♍ – 11° 22′ 30″ ♍

This is one of the three gates of social caution (12,33). It is the gate of aloneness. The Ego Circuit is unusual since  
both its Streams of Awareness do not find their expression directly in the Throat as in the other major Circuits. The  
awareness possibility is the conditioning of the nature of the Ego. The Heart center is a binary of Heart/Ego  
strength and Stomach/Ego Power. Deliverance is the gate of Will power and it is to be carefully noticed, this is not  
an awareness center and not everyone has access to this power. In the Mechanics of the Maia, it is clear, that  
there is no free will. The Ego of this gate says, ‘My belly’s full, why should I bother feeding you?’ It is always alone,  
even in a crowded home, yet it is essential to the survival of the community, the principles of the community will  
collapse unless their is a sustained will to maintain the community. The 37 embraces the 40 and offers the only  
thing that the Ego is vulnerable to, loyalty. Emotional support drives the will. In athletics, it is well known, that to  
play before your own community, cheered on by their loyalty, pumps up the will power and regularly leads to vic-  
tory, which the entire community then experiences as its own.

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF PLANNING (3)  
THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF DENIAL  
THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF MIGRATION (2)

## Gate 40 — **DELIVERANCE**, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
 Alt text:  
 Gate 40 lines page titled “DELIVERANCE.” Subtitle: “THE POINT OF TRANSITION BETWEEN STRUGGLE AND LIBERATION.” Six lines describe how the Heart/Ego energy moves from struggle to release: removing corrupt power, rejecting negative ties, organizing deliverance, staying humble to avoid attracting opposition, resolutely shedding old influences, and recuperating in healthy solitude.

Long description:  
 After the channel of struggle, Gate 40’s **Ego** energy focuses on the *transition* to liberation. Each line frames a different discipline of release: necessary, measured removal of harmful forces; principled rejection of negative bonds; intelligent organization; quiet enjoyment that avoids provocation; resolute clarity about the disruptive effect of certain relationships; and the simple strength to **rest alone** so renewal can take root.

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, **Virgo**):  
 6 **Decapitation** — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
 5 **Rigidity** — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″  
 4 **Organization** — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
 3 **Humility** — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″  
 2 **Resoluteness** — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
 1 **Recuperation** — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″

6. **The necessary destruction of inferior forces in positions of power before liberation can take place.**  
    • ▲ Authority with magnanimity—removing from power only those who truly warrant it. *Power and authority of the ego to eject individuals in legitimate defense of the group.*  
    • ▽ Terror by excess—punishment extended to an entire class. *Distortion of the ego through power and authority.*

7. **The recognition that to achieve liberation all negative forces must be rejected.**  
    • ▲ The revolutionary who demands absolute victory. *Power of the ego maintained in rejecting negative relationships.*  
    • ▽ Allowing “necessary” deviations in hopes of purging them later. *Weakness of the ego in its loneliness—failing to reject negative relationships.*

8. **The power to transform and the intuitive intellect to select and organize for maintaining deliverance.**  
    • ▲ *Power of the ego—organized and active—to maintain separateness.*  
    • ▽ Zeal for quantity over quality of support, ultimately destabilizing deliverance. *Disempowering focus that ignores the quality of organization.*

9. **A calculated mode in deliverance to avoid attracting the attention of negative forces.**  
    • ▲ Subtle enjoyment without flaunting. *Capacity of the ego to avoid negative forces even if it means being alone.*  
    • ▽ Ego arrogance that demands (and gets) attention. *Capacity of the ego to demand attention.*

10. **The power and authority in deliverance to shed forever the qualities that hampered liberation.**  
     • ▲ Through aloneness, recognizing the importance and potential disruptive effect of others.  
     • ▽ Peacefulness that sympathizes with and tries to nurture the very forces that hampered liberation. *Loneliness blinding the ego to others’ disruptive effect.*

11. **The ability to relax and enjoy the fruits of one’s labors.**  
     • ▲ *Ego strength to enjoy being alone.*  
     • ▽ *The Moon cannot stand still*—the ego uncomfortable with being alone too long.

---

# Gate 41 — The Gate of Contraction

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 41 header page. Channel 41–30 “Recognition,” Harmonic Gate 30 “The Clinging Fire,” Center Root, Circuit Sensing, with a degree span in Aquarius. One paragraph explains Gate 41 as the fuel for recognition through feelings, the lone “initiator” of the Sensing stream, and why patience with the emotional wave is essential.

Long description:  
This gate is “Contraction,” likened to a gardener cutting back in autumn. Genetically, Gate 41 is described as the only Initiator (start) Codon, highlighting the importance of the Sensing Circuit—specifically the Stream of Feeling. Gate 41 fuels the potential to recognize (through feelings). Its fuel nature parallels Gate 64; 41 can hold the full range of feelings, yet—like mental judgment—only one feeling will be of value in the moment. There is a stated parallel between the three Root→Solar Plexus channels and the three Root→Spleen channels. The Channel of Judgment (58/18) mirrors this Feeling Stream: the focus is to recognize (to “judge” abstractly) through a single feeling. But judgment is conditioned by the frequency of the now, whereas recognition here is conditioned by the wave; it is not immediate—always a process. Patience is called the great virtue for the Emotionally defined.

Figures / Crosses:

Right Angle Cross of the Unexpected (4)

Juxtaposition Cross of Fantasy

Left Angle Cross of the Alpha (2)

## Gate 41 — DECREASE, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 41 “DECREASE” (Root • Sensing), lines page with Aquarius degree spans. The theme is limiting resources to maximize the development of potential. Each line describes how feeling-fueled restraint, prudence, and selection channel the Root’s pressure into recognition via the emotional wave.

Long description:  
The page title is “DECREASE,” with the subtitle: “THE LIMITATION OF RESOURCES WHICH MAXIMIZES DEVELOPMENT OF POTENTIAL.” Along the left margin, lines 6→1 show degree ranges within Aquarius. Each line presents a blue keynote and two notes—exaltation (▲) and detriment (▽)—that frame how energy/feelings should be directed when resources are constrained. Large gray “41” at the top-right; a thin gold rule near the footer; standard two-column layout.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aquarius):  
6 Contagion — 06° 41′ 15″ – 07° 37′ 30″  
5 Authorization — 05° 45′ 00″ – 06° 41′ 15″  
4 Correction — 04° 48′ 45″ – 05° 45′ 00″  
3 Efficiency — 03° 52′ 30″ – 04° 48′ 45″  
2 Caution — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
1 Reasonableness — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″

The law that the maximization of potential not only ends decrease but inevitably such transcendence benefits others.  
• ▲ The maximization of the potential of form. The father whose success will benefit his children. The fuel for recognition through feelings.  
• ▽ The tendency in transcendence to keep it a secret in fear of being deprived of its special advantages. The fuel for secret or repressed feelings.

The external recognition of one’s potential despite limitations.  
• ▲ The value of energy when properly channelled. The fuel for properly channelled feelings despite limitations.  
• ▽ A persistent dissatisfaction with limitation which hampers development even when supported. Limitations initiate the fuel for negative feelings.

Successful adaptation to limitation.  
• ▲ Survival of the fittest and its eventual flourishing. The energy for adaption and a deep feeling for survival.  
• ▽ The expenditure of energy on maintaining relationships rather than on correction. In times of decrease weak associations are shunned as costly, no matter how attractive. The energy which fuels holding on to feelings rather than adapt.

In times of decrease selfishness is justified.  
• ▲ Material ambition and the discipline to go at it alone. The energy that fuels the feeling for personal ambition.  
• ▽ Instinctive assistance, admirable but in this position misguided, where two will use up resources twice as fast as one. The energy that fuels the feeling to share.

Humanism tempered by pragmatism.  
• ▲ A conservative nature that does not risk its own security in aiding others. Energy for one’s own feelings but not for others.  
• ▽ A desire for recognition that throws caution to the wind, depleting valuable resources for only temporary gain. The energy for the display of feelings for recognition.

The appropriate delegation of responsibility.  
• ▲ The imagination to make the very best out of very little. Coolness, where the energy to release feelings is selective.  
• ▽ A tendency in understanding, to assume capability, leading to strain. The hothead, the urge to release feelings.

# Gate 42 — The Gate of Growth

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 42 header page. Channel 42–53 “Maturation,” Harmonic Gate 53 “Development,” Center Sacral, Circuit Sensing, astrologic span in Aries. The text explains completion as the heart of growth—bringing things to a close so the next step can begin—and why patience with the cyclic experiential process is essential.

Long description:  
This gate maximizes the potential of beginnings through expansion, and just as importantly it brings things to a close, so the next step can be taken. The power to complete a cycle is the heart of the growth process—“one thing at a time” is its mantra. In this Format lies the core of the Abstract/Sensing process. Because this stream has no Splenic activation, cycles can be unhealthy when dominated by fear; without the Spleen’s recognition of the value of the now, that value must still be accepted as essential. We exist in the now anyway—despite perspective. The change of the cycle is not the goal; the grail is the experience. The Abstract Being must be absorbed in the experience of the now, to reflect on it later. The potential of Growth is the power to mature by completing each cycle.

Figures / crosses (bottom of page):

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF MAIA

THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF COMPLETION

THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF LIMITATION

## Gate 42 — INCREASE, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 42 “INCREASE” (Root → Sacral, Sensing stream) lines page. Theme: the expansion of resources to maximize full potential. Each line shows how growth is shared, internalized, mediated, learned through mistakes, identified with trends, and diversified — always via the emotional/cyclical process.

Long description:  
The page presents six keyed lines with degree spans (Aries). For each line, two notes contrast exalted vs. detriment expressions of growth pressure: nurturing vs. refusal to share; self-actualization vs. reclusive withdrawal; mediation vs. distorted harmonizing; using mistakes vs. brooding; recognizing trends vs. withdrawing from change; and healthy diversification vs. decadent over-expansion. Layout: large “42” at top-right, gold subtitle, two-column body.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aries):  
6 Nurturing — 25° 26′ 15″ – 26° 22′ 30″  
5 Self-actualization — 24° 30′ 00″ – 25° 26′ 15″  
4 The middle man — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
3 Trial and error — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″  
2 Identification — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
1 Diversification — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″

The law that the maximization of potential not only ends decrease but inevitably such transcendence benefits others.  
• C ▲ A natural and instinctive nurturing of others. The power to share the process of growth with others.  
• 2 ▽ A restrictive and malefic materialism that is self-alienating and encourages aggression. The refusal to share the benefits of growth with others.

The external recognition of one’s potential despite limitations.  
• ☉ ▲ The fulfillment and actualization of purpose as a natural path whose reward is a healthy sense of self, rather than the power and influence that naturally follow. Growth that is self-fulfilling and naturally leads to influence.  
• ♀ ▽ Self-actualization as a strictly inner experience that may demand or result in a reclusive nature. Inner growth that empowers reclusiveness.

The middle man.  
• C ▲ The quintessential manifestation of the mediator. The maturity to bring growth through mediation.  
• ♀ ▽ Where the gift to establish and maintain relationships is ill-suited in this position to act in mediation where harmony must take a back seat to pragmatism. A lack of maturity where the power to harmonize distorts mediation and limits growth.

In times of increase, mistakes are a natural part of the process.  
• ♂ ▲ The energy and assertion to turn mistakes into advantages. The power to accept mistakes as part of growth.  
• C ▽ A moodiness, that in error may succumb to brooding and unnecessary caution. Mistakes give power to moodiness and caution.

Humanism tempered by pragmatism.  
• ☉ ▲ Recognition and acute capitalization of trends. Power for growth through participating in trends.  
• ♀ ▽ An ascetically motivated withdrawal in times of progressive change. Growth which stops in reaction to trends or change.

The appropriate delegation of responsibility.  
• ☉ ▲ The ability when surplus resources are available to extend one’s activities beyond their normal scope. Growth through expansion particularly when defined to the root.  
• ♀ ▽ A tendency when surplus resources are available to centrifugal application. Decadence. Too much expansion can lead to decadence.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2; ▽ U+25BD; ☉ U+2609 (Sun); ♀ U+2640 (Venus); ♂ U+2642 (Mars); C \= stylized Moon mark (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); “2” \= edition node symbol.

# Gate 43 — The Gate of Insight

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 43 header page. Channel 43–23 “Structuring,” Harmonic Gate 23 “Splitting Apart,” Center Ajna, Circuit Knowing, and an astrologic span in Scorpio. The text presents Gate 43 as the “Inner Ear” of the Knowing circuit—individual mental knowing that must transform an inner truth into a unique insight and then find access to the Throat to manifest.

Long description:  
The Knowing circuit is linked to hearing. Here “breakthrough” is not a third-eye vision but the Inner Ear—the capacity to listen inwardly to one’s own voice and shape a rationalized inner truth into insight. People with Gate 43 can be difficult to teach, not through fault but due to a genetic protection from undue influence; teaching must be simple and start at the beginning. This gate’s knowing is individual and may not be backed by facts—it can even appear delusional until it is correctly conceptualized. It takes fortitude—and courage—to stand behind it. Gate 43 marks the final shaping of an awareness; once the possibility is fully conceptualized, it awaits access to the Throat for manifestation.

Transcribed text (exact header data as seen):  
The Gate of Insight

Channel 43-23 Structuring  
A design of individuality (Genius to Freak)

## Gate 43 — BREAKTHROUGH, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 43 “BREAKTHROUGH” (Ajna • Knowing). Six lines describe how an inner, individual insight establishes a new order only when it is centered, timed, and carried with depth—versus ego, over-harmonizing, one-track stubbornness, oversensitivity to condemnation, incautious action, or impatience.

Long description:  
The subtitle reads: “IN ORDER FOR ACHIEVEMENT TO BE MAINTAINED A NEW ORDER MUST BE FAIRLY ESTABLISHED.” Each line treats a stage of moving an inner knowing into form: centering the new order; adapting relationships for effective sharing; focusing the mind beyond stubborn obsession; withstanding condemnation; maintaining disciplined thought habits; and waiting for resistance to clear before actualization.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Scorpio):  
6 Breakthrough — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
5 Progression — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″  
4 The one-track mind — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
3 Expediency — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″  
2 Dedication — 19° 48′ 45″ – 20° 45′ 00″  
1 Patience — 18° 52′ 30″ – 19° 48′ 45″

Breakthrough  
• ☉ ▲ Actualization and centering, that in breakthrough naturally establish both internally and externally a new order. Unique knowing that is both personally and collectively of value.  
• ♂ ▽ An ego tendency that in breakthrough sees justification of its lesser attributes and carries them over into the new order. Where the value of knowing is more important than other aspects of the life.

Progression  
• C ▲ The step-by-step adaptation to relationships which hinder breakthrough through practical actions which will not jeopardize eventual success. The gift of knowing when an unique insight can be shared effectively with others.  
• ♀ ▽ A tendency to concentrate on harmonizing relationships that tends to strengthen the position of restrictive forces rather than benefiting breakthrough. An over-reliance on the receptivity of others that turns harmony into the conditioner of individual expression.

The one-track mind  
• ☿ ▲ The overreliance and stubborn obsession with one’s mental abilities in the face of recurring obstacles. This design, given Mercurial mental gifts, may though rarely succeed. A stubborn reliance on one’s unique insights that demands focused mental ability to be properly conceptualized.  
• 2 ▽ A belief in inherent right action based on limited knowledge that shuns advice. The vanity to attempt expression without depth.

Expediency  
• ω ▲ The powerful drive for regeneration that will, when breakthrough is threatened, use any means, align with any force and endure any condemnation to achieve the goal. A certainty in knowing which can withstand condemnation.  
• C ▽ An oversensitivity to condemnation, that may preclude justified expediency and result in failure. Individual insight abandoned in the face of condemnation.

Dedication  
• ω ▲ The maintenance of specialized attitudes which promote breakthrough. Unique mental habits and thought processes which promote insight.  
• C ▽ A dedication to action, that when actualization is in sight may become incautious. The urge for expression that will abandon its normal processes when an opportunity arises.

Patience  
• ω ▲ The recognition that new forms cannot be established until resistance has been eliminated. The depth necessary to bring into form an individual insight.  
• ♀ ▽ An impatience to enjoy the sweets of accomplishment that inevitably leaves a sour taste in the mouth. A delight in the insight but a lack of depth to establish it.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
☉ U+2609 Sun; ♂ U+2642 Mars; ♀ U+2640 Venus; ☿ U+263F Mercury; ω U+03C9 omega; C \= stylized Moon mark (cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); “2” \= edition node symbol.  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment.

# Gate 44 — The Gate of Alertness

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 44 header page. Channel 44–26 “Surrender,” Harmonic Gate 26 “The Taming Power of the Great,” Center Spleen, Circuit Ego (Tribal), with a Scorpio degree span. The text describes instinctive, pattern-based memory that transmits the Ego’s value to the Tribe (“transmitter”), trading loyalty and resources for willpower, and names 44 as the fear of the past.

Long description:  
This page frames Gate 44 as the Channel of Entrepreneurial Enterprise, a transmitter in the Tribal (Ego) circuit. Its memory is instinctive—not recalled on command, but cued by patterns encountered in the present—and its “alertness” is a sensitivity to those patterns. Because this is a Spleen gate, it does not speak directly through the Throat; instead it negotiates within the Tribe: Gate 37 offers social loyalty, Gate 44 offers individual loyalty and access to its memory resources in exchange for the Ego’s willpower to steady Tribal fear. “Coming to Meet” is the fear of the past—old patterns returning. As a public channel, it brings Ego to Tribe; as a mirror to Gate 37, it links social support with entrepreneurial recognition.

Transcribed text (header data):  
The Gate of Alertness  
Channel: 44–26 — Surrender (A design of a transmitter)  
Harmonic Gate: 26 — The Taming Power of the Great  
Center: Spleen  
Circuit: Ego  
Astrologic Position: 07° 37′ 30″ ♏ – 13° 15′ 00″ ♏

Figures (bottom captions):  
THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE FOUR WAYS (3) · THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF ALERTNESS · THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF INCARNATION (2)

## Gate 44 — COMING TO MEET, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 44 “COMING TO MEET” lines page (Spleen • Ego/Tribal). Theme: successful interaction depends on the absence of preconditions. Six lines show how instinctive, pattern-based alertness can renew, manipulate, cut through hypocrisy, handle interference, manage structures, and set conditions.

Long description:  
Title “COMING TO MEET,” subtitle “THE SUCCESS OF ANY INTERACTION IS BASED ON THE ABSENCE OF ANY PRECONDITIONS.” Left margin lists the six lines (6→1) with Scorpio degree ranges. For each line, a blue key phrase is followed by two notes: an exaltation (▲) and a detriment (▽), each prefixed by a small glyph. Large gray “44” appears at top right; blue page number “44” at the bottom; thin gold baseline.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Scorpio):  
6 Aloofness — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
5 Manipulation — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″  
4 Honesty — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
3 Interference — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″  
2 Management — 08° 33′ 45″ – 09° 30′ 00″  
1 Conditions — 07° 37′ 30″ – 08° 33′ 45″

Aloofness  
• ω ▲ A perfected renewal in which the establishment of a new form inures one to the condemnation of the rejected. The awareness of patterns which ensures one’s instinctive well-being.  
• ⊕ ▽ Intolerance and arrogance for lesser mortals. The awareness of patterns which can bolster the ego at the expense of the well-being of others.

Manipulation  
• \[glyph unclear\] ▲ The ability to transform interaction with inferior elements into the energizing of progressive process with the additional benefit that, in tapping the inferior elements, they remain weak. Where the instinctive recognition of the patterns leads to the possible manipulation of others.  
• ♂ ▽ The tendency in this form of manipulation to become abusive and degenerate to their level. The possibility that the instinctive recognition of the patterns could lead to the abuse of others.

Honesty  
• ω ▲ Indifference at its most logical and cutting. The indifference possible when guided by the instinctive memory.  
• ☉ ▽ The Sun in detriment, that in extreme situations, in cases of self-sustainment, will accept assistance from forces it has totally rejected. Here the honesty is the genuine need. The sacrifice of indifference for survival.

Interference  
• ♂ ▲ The ability to recognize the threat of interference and to prepare for its effects. The alertness and instinct to handle the ego of others.  
• Ψ ▽ A deluded response to interference that gets stuck in projection and as a result is unrealistic in appraisal and error-prone. The possibility that the instinct cannot handle the ego of others.

Management  
• 2 ▲ The development and management of a proper collective structure which restricts inferior elements by creating cooperative modes that integrates these forces with progressive and superior forces. The possibility that alertness to patterns will result in management capability.  
• ♂ ▽ A tendency in management to concentrate exclusively on goals, ignoring inferior elements which leads inevitably to quantitative success and qualitative failure. The instinctive memory for the patterns that bypasses the development of managerial capability.

Conditions  
• ω ▲ The establishment of frameworks as the result of interaction. A mastery of the collective that can establish restrictive conditions for inferior components and with the ability to enforce them. An alertness to patterns that can lead to mastery of the collective.  
• ♀ ▽ An attractive nature that will interact with inferior forces and will fail to apply restrictions resulting in a risk to continued harmony. The failure of the instinct out of the need for harmony.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; ω U+03C9; ♂ U+2642; ♀ U+2640; ☉ U+2609; ⊕ U+2295. The exaltation glyph on line 5 is unclear at this resolution.

# Gate 45 — The Gate of the Gatherer

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 45 header page. Channel 45-21 “The Money Line,” Harmonic Gate 21 “Biting Through,” Center Throat, Circuit Ego (Tribal), astrologic span in Gemini. Text presents Gate 45 as the voice “I have”—the dominant Gatherer/King/Queen whose role is to hold resources for the Tribe and keep peace by honestly declaring “We have.”

Long description:  
This is the gate of the Master/Mistress, the King/Queen—Dominance. Throat gates have voices; here it says “I have.” “Gathering Together” is the expression of the entire Tribal group and this is a Throat gate of primary action. The potential power of three motors can focus through this expression; in composite (with 21\) the Channel of Materialism can be highly beneficial. Gate 45 is the gate of the pasha: it says to 21 (the hunter/huntress), “I have this land; I’ll let you hunt it—make sure I get the best piece of meat.” When 45 is generous and recognizes 21’s willful independence, all is well. More often, 21 believes it deserves the best, and 45 wants to tell it how to hunt. Gate 21 carries the demands of the community toward its rulers; Gate 45 is key to harmony: when the ruler can honestly say “We have,” the Tribe rests in peace. (Practical aside: for depressed 21s or 45s—go shopping\!)

Figures (bottom captions):

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF RULERSHIP (2)

THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF POSSESSION

THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF CONFRONTATION

## Gate 45 — GATHERING TOGETHER, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 45 “GATHERING TOGETHER” (Throat • Ego/Tribal) lines page. Theme: the natural, generally beneficial attraction of like forces—how a ruler/center gathers, directs, includes, and educates for the tribe’s material harmony.

Long description:  
Subtitle: “THE NATURAL AND GENERALLY BENEFICIAL ATTRACTION OF LIKE FORCES.” The six lines describe practical tribal dynamics—reconsidering outsiders, centered leadership, principled direction, responses to exclusion, building consensus via shared techniques, and canvassing/education—framed as material (on-the-ground) expressions of gathering.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Gemini):  
6 Reconsideration — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
5 Leadership — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″  
4 Direction — 19° 48′ 45″ – 20° 45′ 00″  
3 Exclusion — 18° 52′ 30″ – 19° 48′ 45″  
2 Consensus — 17° 56′ 15″ – 18° 52′ 30″  
1 Canvassing — 17° 00′ 00″ – 17° 56′ 15″

An outsider that can admit that its prior rejection was an error, will generally be accepted into the gathering.  
• ▲ An innate empathy with the outsider mentality and its eccentric and often misunderstood logic. A material direction which serves the outsider.  
• ▽ Where Uranus will innovate to find a place for an outsider, Jupiter will demand that it conforms. A material direction that is focused on conditioning the outsider to conform.

All gathering together must have a center and a focus.  
• ▲ The intuitive intellect and gift for innovation that enhance the group effort and ensure continuity through respect of the center. The gift for expressing leadership on the material plane.  
• ▽ A sense of right action that assumes a respect that may not as yet have been earned. The drive for leadership that may not have yet earned the right.

The ability to focus the opportunity of gathering together for the service of higher principles.  
• ▲ The expression of higher principles on the material plane.  
• ▽ The tendency to try to influence the direction of a group action for personal benefit. The lack of expression of higher principles on the material plane.

The ability when excluded to take whatever measures are necessary to dissolve the antiquated form and to accept even humiliation to achieve inclusion.  
• ▲ The instinct to find a way to be included in a material process.  
• ▽ An aggressive and often violent reaction to exclusion. The expression of frustration when not included in a material process.

Gathering together is strengthened by acknowledged common interest.  
• ▲ The inventiveness to establish techniques by which common ground can be assessed. A material direction through the expression of techniques for the benefit of others.  
• ▽ An innate rebellion against conformity. The refusal to accept the material techniques of others.

The ability to promote and develop gathering together through the education of the uncommitted.  
• ▲ The material direction lies in education.  
• ▽ An overzealousness that turns canvassing into proselytization and tends to alienate rather than gather support. The drive for material that leads to aggressive education.

# Gate 46 — The Gate of the Determination of the Self

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 46 header page. Channel 46–29 “Discovery,” Harmonic Gate 29 “The Abysmal,” Center G, Circuit Sensing. A paragraph explains serendipity—being in the right place at the right time—as a bodily, experiential love that fuels the Abstract process through surrender to cycles and wholehearted participation in experience.

Long description:  
A white page titled “The Gate of the Determination of the Self.” The spec block lists Channel 46–29 Discovery (design of succeeding where others fail), Harmonic Gate 29 The Abysmal, Center G, Circuit Sensing, and an astrologic span from late Virgo into early Libra.  
The body text situates Gate 46 within the Cross of the Vessel—a gate of Love, specifically love of the body and flesh, and the grace of Serendipity: recognizing (and trusting) that one is in the right place at the right time. It frames 46 as both a gate of discovery / good fortune and a Design of “succeeding where others fail” (and inversely, “failing where others succeed”). Through this channel the Abstract/Sensing process finds its promise or chaos: determination to accept “this is the right place” is deeply spiritual. One must surrender to the cycle—there lies the potential for discovery. If the abstract being cannot accept and commit to life’s cyclic nature, bodily stress follows: crisis, disappointment, upheaval.  
Bottom of page shows three mandalas labeled: THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF THE VESSEL OF LOVE (3), THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF SERENDIPITY, and THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF HEALING (2).

Transcribed header (as printed):

Channel: 46–29 — Discovery (A design of succeeding where others fail)

Harmonic Gate: 29 — The Abysmal

Center: G

Circuit: Sensing

## Gate 46 — PUSHING UPWARD, Lines 6→1)

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 46 “PUSHING UPWARD” (G • Sensing/Abstract). Six lines describe how good fortune—often mistaken for serendipity—actually comes from effort, disciplined pacing, right impact, practical projection, demanding dedication, and quiet work in obscurity until recognized.

Long description:  
Title: “PUSHING UPWARD.” Subtitle: “GOOD FORTUNE THAT MAY BE PERCEIVED AS THE RESULT OF SERENDIPITY BUT DERIVES FROM EFFORT AND DEDICATION.” The page lays out six stages for turning bodily love-of-experience into recognizable success: guarding commitments (integrity), keeping the right rhythm (pacing), landing impact at the right time, staying with what works (projection), succeeding despite a prickly temperament (prima donna), and persevering in obscurity until discovery.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Virgo → Libra within Gate 46):  
6 Integrity — 02° 56′ 15″ – 03° 52′ 30″  
5 Pacing — 02° 00′ 00″ – 02° 56′ 15″  
4 Impact — 01° 03′ 45″ – 02° 00′ 00″  
3 Projection — 00° 07′ 30″ – 01° 03′ 45″  
2 The prima donna — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
1 Being discovered — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″

Integrity  
• ▲ The wisdom to secure one’s identity through careful consideration of the restrictive potential of commitments. The determination to say no to restrictive commitments.  
• ▽ Deceiving oneself and others by overextending one’s resources and eventually having to break promises. A drive for success that will not say no, and will end up breaking promises.

Pacing  
• ▲ The maintenance of proper rhythm that, in its instinctive practicality, avoids radical divergence from successful patterns. The determination to stay with the rhythm which brings success.  
• ▽ An irrational rejection of the very patterns that have proven successful. Determined to say no to the very rhythm that brings success.

Impact  
• ▲ The ability, once recognized, to move rapidly from obscurity to influence. The good luck which comes from being determined that, in the right place at the right time, leads to recognition.  
• ▽ A reaction in achievement that tends to bite the hand that fed it. The determination, in success, to ignore those who helped create it.

Projection  
• ▲ A practical approach to good fortune: maintaining the same patterns and attitudes that brought success; not getting sidetracked by new expectations. The determination to stay with what brings success.  
• ▽ Treating a projected future as the present, leading to unjustified egoism and loss of momentum/support. The determination to treat a projected success as a reality.

The prima donna  
• ▲ A difficult, demanding nature that succeeds despite its behavior because of depth of talent. The determination to succeed that may offend others.  
• ▽ Unrealistic demands and egocentric mediocrity. The determination to be treated as a success before it has been realized.

Being discovered  
(Dedication in obscurity that is unexpectedly discovered.)  
• ▲ Art for art’s sake. Any self-fulfilling creative endeavor is eventually recognized. Potential for creative success through dedication.  
• ▽ The ability to spot others’ potential in obscurity, but only to serve one’s own success. The determination to recognize—and benefit from—the success of others.

# Gate 47 — The Gate of Realizing

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 47 header page. Channel 47–64 “Abstraction,” Harmonic Gate 64 “Before Completion,” Center Ajna, Circuit Sensing, astrologic span in Virgo. A paragraph describes realization under pressure: the Sensing circuit’s abstract clips coalesce into meaning over time—patience is required—hence 47’s association with “oppression.”

Long description:  
This is the gate of realization, but the process is often uncomfortable: mental stress and anxiety can mount since this abstract current can’t be substantiated by facts. Gate 64 fuels the potential; imagine expecting a feature film and getting thousands of clips—the potential of 47 is to realize which clip makes sense. The Sensing format is cyclic, and realization is an ongoing process; pressure remains until the moment of “aha.” This is the gate of Oppression (waiting under pressure). The secret of the entire circuit is also the secret here: patience. You cannot rummage through the image pool to force resolution; you must wait for the moment of realization.

Figures (bottom captions):

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF RULERSHIP (3)

THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF OPPRESSION

THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF INFORMING (2)

Harmonic Gate: 64 — Before Completion

Center: Ajna

Circuit: Sensing

Astrologic Position: 17° 00′ 00″ ♍ — 22° 37′ 30″ ♍

## Gate 47 — OPPRESSION, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 47 “OPPRESSION” (Ajna • Sensing) lines page. It describes how realization under pressure can feel restrictive or adverse—arising from inner weakness, external force, or both—and how clarity emerges by taking stock, disciplined ambition, releasing self-oppression, withstanding repression, saintly acceptance, and finally admitting futility when there’s no way forward.

Long description:  
The subtitle states: “A RESTRICTIVE AND ADVERSE STATE AS A RESULT OF INTERNAL WEAKNESS OR EXTERNAL STRENGTH OR BOTH.” Each line frames a stage of coping with mental pressure: (6) futility when no exaltation is possible; (5) a saintly grace that maintains integrity with oppressors while aiding the oppressed; (4) identity that survives external repression—or collapses when overwhelmed; (3) self-oppression that can resolve into the realization “I’m actually okay,” or harden destructively; (2) ambition that chooses healthy action over indecision; and (1) taking stock—eradicating negative causes vs. projecting blame outward.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Virgo):  
6 Futility — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
5 The saint — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″  
4 Repression — 19° 48′ 45″ – 20° 45′ 00″  
3 Self-oppression — 18° 52′ 30″ – 19° 48′ 45″  
2 Ambition — 17° 56′ 15″ – 18° 52′ 30″  
1 Taking stock — 17° 00′ 00″ – 17° 56′ 15″

A difficult position for which there is no exaltation.  
• ▲ No polarity.  
• ♂ ▽ The Sun in detriment, where the strength of will alone may find a way to adapt and survive, but without hope of ever overcoming the oppression. Life as an ordeal stripped of realization.

This position has special significance and there is no planet in detriment.  
• ▲ The gift in times of oppression to maintain without hypocrisy a harmonic relationship with one’s oppressors, while providing aid and succour to the oppressed. Realization at its most exalted. Acceptance of the weight of the abstract process and the grace that naturally follows.  
• ▽ No planet in detriment.

The constraints of external oppression.  
• 2 ▲ The strength of identity that even in times of the most powerful oppression can maintain its resources and to some extent ensure their survival for the benefit of others. A sense of identity that can be maintained despite external conditioning.  
• C ☽ ▽ When robbed of its light, the Moon is lost in darkness; barely able to nourish itself, let alone others. The identity overwhelmed by external conditioning.

Self-oppression.  
• 2 ▲ The conscious and well-integrated being whose natural right action will eventually come to see the unfoundedness of its oppression. The eventual realization that one is really o.k.  
• ♂ ▽ A self-oppression so highly energized that it may prove irreversible and destructive. Extreme difficulty in realizing one’s self-worth.

Ambition.  
• 2 ▲ The ambition and drive to overcome personal oppression in order to achieve security. Realizing that to be busy is mentally healthy.  
• ☿ ▽ An indecisiveness in times of personal oppression, whether to apply intelligence to recovery or accept its weight to take advantage of other prevailing conditions that may only be temporary. The inability to sense when and which activity is healthy.

Taking stock.  
• 2 ▲ The ability in times of hardship to concentrate on eradicating the negative factors that have led to oppression. Realizing that negative thoughts have to be eradicated.  
• Ψ ▽ The delusion of seeing oppression as an exclusively external phenomenon with often disastrous results. The sense that the world is against you.

# Gate 48 — The Gate of Depth

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 48 header page. Channel 48–16 “The Wave Length,” Harmonic Gate 16 “Enthusiasm,” Center Spleen, Circuit Understanding, astrologic span in Libra. The page explains “The Well” as a pool of depth—instinctive memory patterned by judgment (Gate 18)—that supplies the collective with usable information and underpins talent by recognizing which patterns to concentrate on now.

Long description:  
The relationship between Gate 18 and Gate 48 is correction ↔ depth. “The Well” is a resource, a pool: the awareness possibility to store vital information as a pattern. Its quality (depth) depends on what the judgmental process has produced. Gate 48 opens potential expression via its connection toward the Throat; without depth, the common good cannot be established. The Understanding (Logic) circuit shares what it finds; its format is focus and concentration on patterns—the basis of Talent. Unsurprisingly, correction for society is a recurring theme. “The Well” is depth available in the moment, keyed to judging the situation now. In the Collective circuits the keynote is sharing—whether vital or trivial, depth gets shared.

Transcribed header (as printed):

Channel: 48–16 — The Wave Length (A design of talent)

Harmonic Gate: 16 — Enthusiasm

Center: Spleen

Circuit: Understanding

Astrologic Position: 09° 30′ 00″ ♎ — 15° 07′ 30″ ♎

Figures / crosses at bottom:

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF TENSION (3)

THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF DEPTH

THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF ENDEAVOUR (2)

## Gate 48 — THE WELL, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 48 “THE WELL” lines page (Spleen • Understanding/Logic). Six themes—Self-fulfillment, Action, Restructuring, Incommunicado, Degeneracy, Insignificance—explain how depth (“the well”) becomes a practical resource for the common good through taste, focus, and pattern-based awareness.

Long description:  
Subtitle: “THE NECESSARY AND QUALITATIVE FOUNDATION THAT IS A PREREQUISITE TO ESTABLISH THE COMMON GOOD.” Each line shows how depth expresses on the material plane: a valued center that gives and renews itself; the urge (or hesitation) to act; restructuring under restriction; the “no-Moon” phase of unrecognized potential; resisting decay by holding to the best; and the discernment to ignore trivia and attend to what’s practical. Layout: large “48” at top right; Libra degree spans listed for each line.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Libra):  
6 Self-fulfillment — 14° 11′ 15″ – 15° 07′ 30″  
5 Action — 13° 15′ 00″ – 14° 11′ 15″  
4 Restructuring — 12° 18′ 45″ – 13° 15′ 00″  
3 Incommunicado — 11° 22′ 30″ – 12° 18′ 45″  
2 Degeneracy — 10° 26′ 15″ – 11° 22′ 30″  
1 Insignificance — 09° 30′ 00″ – 10° 26′ 15″

An undiminishable resource.  
• ♀ ▲ The valued center, that as it gives, it receives and thus can continue to give. A depth and potential talent that is of value to others.  
• C ▽ A tendency to superficiality which, though generous and nurturing, lacks the inspirational quality to become common currency. Where depth is limited, taste is superficial and affects the quality of possible talent.

Action.  
• ♂ ▲ The natural urge to apply energy to action. A taste for action.  
• C ▽ Overreliance on protection: in social renewal, planning obsesses at the expense of action. Insecurity with one’s depth can fail to take action.

Restructuring.  
• ⊕ ▲ Good sense to use short-term restriction to assess and restructure for long-term goals, easing development when activity resumes. Awareness that depth will face restrictions—hence a taste for short-term projects.  
• ⊕ ▽ Resistance to restructuring on speculation; when activity resumes it must be done anyway. A taste for long-term projects frustrated in restraint.

Incommunicado.  
• C ▲ “No Moon” phase—potential light unrecognized, unavailable, unknowable—yet the transience can strengthen those afflicted. Where developing taste and depth is a long-term process.  
• ☿ ▽ Profound anxiety when profound intelligence is “lost in the wilderness.” A long-term process (developing taste & depth) tending to anxiety.

Degeneracy.  
• ω ▲ To establish a new form, do not neglect the best to accommodate the inferior; otherwise deterioration follows. Confidence in awareness to resist inferior influences.  
• ♀ ▽ Misguided harmony that degenerates through inferior values into decadence. A lack of confidence in awareness inviting interference and decadence.

Insignificance.  
• C ▲ Instinctive recognition of what is practical and deserves attention. A taste for recognizing what is practical and deserves attention.  
• ♂ ▽ Ego energy applied to trivialities. A taste for trivia.

# Gate 49 — The Gate of Principles

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 49 “The Gate of Principles.” Channel 49–19 “Synthesis” (a design of being sensitive) linking the Solar Plexus (awareness of feelings) to tribal matters of food, bonds, and revolt. Harmonic Gate 19 “Approach.” Circuit: Ego (tribal). Astrologic position in Aquarius (≈ 18° 52′ 30″–24° 30′ 00″ ♒). A long paragraph describes Revolution, animistic bonds with animals, the “breeder” theme, the need to manage food resources and social access, and how emotionally driven principles can swing between hope and harsh rejection.

Long description:  
This is the gate of Revolution—pure wave-based energy to reject what violates communal values, and, at the same time, an awareness that can be ritualistic and spiritual. In the Design of Mammals, this is one of the rare cross-species channels linking Humans and animals (the channel of Animism), exalting animals as gods and giving the 49 the sensitivity to interact with them; it is the gate of the “breeder,” concerned with taming and breeding to secure food resources. At the collective level, 49 is the potential awareness of who/what belongs in order to maintain or restructure the community; it relies on its harmonic 19 for the social contract—food (access) in exchange for support. In mundane politics, Sensitivity maps to the “left” and Instinct to the “right”; the risk in 49’s principled stance is the Solar Plexus wave itself: when fueled by emotion, Revolutions ride hope and pain—“power to the people” can flip into the suffering of the “gulag.” With depth and clarity, however, 49 supplies the principled ground from which just corrections and humane reforms can be organized.

Transcribed text (verbatim body):  
This is the gate of Revolution, at once pure energy, and the capacity in the wave to reject and at the same time, potentially aware and ritualistically spiritual. In the Design of Mammals (see Glossary, Natural World) this channel forms one of the three cross-species connections between animals and Humans. This is the channel of Animism and the exaltation of Animals as gods. The 49th gate has the sensitivity to interact with animals and is the gate of the ‘breeder’. Taming and breeding animals to ensure food resources. Revolution is the potential awareness to know what and whom is needed or not to maintain or restructure the community. It seeks the support of the 19, in return for the guarantee of food (social) access. On the mundane plane, Sensitivity is the political ‘left’ and the Stream of Instinct, the political ‘right’. The risk with the principles of this gate, is the nature of the Solar Plexus. When determined by emotional energy, the revolution follows the wave of hope and pain, the hope of ‘power to the people’ becomes the pain of starvation in the ‘gulag’.

Symbols (Unicode):  
• Aquarius ♒ U+2652

Crosses referenced:  
• Right Angle Cross of Explanation · Juxtaposition Cross of Principles · Left Angle Cross of Revolution

When determined by emotional energy, the revolution follows the wave of hope and pain, the hope of ‘power to the people’ becomes the pain of starvation in the ‘gulag’."

## Gate 49 — REVOLUTION, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 49 “REVOLUTION” (Solar Plexus • Ego/Tribal) lines page. Theme: ideally, revolution transforms forms according to the highest principles — not merely for power. Lines show sensitive, principled ways to expand support, organize, set a rights-based platform, handle popular discontent, exhaust peaceful avenues before last resort, and apply the law of necessity.

Long description:  
The subtitle reads: “IDEALLY THE TRANSFORMATION OF FORMS BASED ON THE HIGHEST PRINCIPLES AND NOT SIMPLY FOR POWER.” Each line frames a facet of principled change guided by the Solar Plexus wave: attracting support through impressionability versus fatal rejectionism; organizing for practical needs versus power consolidation; guaranteeing rights versus hollow promises; rejecting failed forms versus savage purges; exhausting peaceful options versus impatient coups; and applying the law of necessity versus manufacturing “necessity” through disorder.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aquarius):  
6 Attraction — 23° 33′ 45″ – 24° 30′ 00″  
5 Organization — 22° 37′ 30″ – 23° 33′ 45″  
4 Platform — 21° 41′ 15″ – 22° 37′ 30″  
3 Popular discontent — 20° 45′ 00″ – 21° 41′ 15″  
2 The last resort — 19° 48′ 45″ – 20° 45′ 00″  
1 The law of necessity — 18° 52′ 30″ – 19° 48′ 45″

The power of revolution in action to expand its support.  
• Ψ ▲ An innate impressionability that transforms the fence-sitter into the committed. The sensitivity and potential to embrace and transform others.  
• 2 ▽ Stubborn, and often fatal, rejectionism. Oversensitivity that leads to rejection of principles and others as a rule.

Organization.  
• C ▲ Practical provisions for the needs of others in revolutionary times which ensure support and continued understanding. A potential sensitivity to the practical needs of others.  
• ♂ ▽ A concentration on the organization of power to clearly define authority often at the expense of higher principles. A rejection of higher principles in seeking to organize others.

Platform.  
• 2 ▲ A political and social agenda, embodied in guarantees to human rights that ensures a just and valued replacement of the old order. A potential sensitivity to the needs of society.  
• ♂ ▽ Promises, promises, promises — only to guarantee support with little possibility of implementation. A potential to insensitively take advantage of the needs of society.

Popular discontent.  
• Ψ ▲ The ability to destroy antiquated forms once the restrictions have been removed. The potential in sensitivity to reject failed principles or relationships.  
• ω ▽ Pluto in detriment, with general support — a savageness in eliminating the old order that may permanently scar the new. An insensitivity in rejection and rejecting.

The last resort.  
• ⊕ ▲ The determination to exhaust every possible peaceful avenue for change and then, when satisfied that no other course is possible, to plan in detail before revolution is attempted. The potential to explore every possibility before rejecting.  
• ω ▽ The overwhelming revolutionary urge that is impatient with accommodation and negotiation; the tendency toward a coup d’état with little general support. Impatience with accommodation.

The law of necessity.  
• 2 ▲ The understanding and application of this law to maximize expansion of support and ensure viability. The awareness that the potential of a principle is based on it being accepted as viable.  
• C ▽ The misuse of influence — insisting that “necessity” can be created by acts of disorder (‘Helter Skelter’ syndrome), committing disorder to “prove” disorder exists. Oversensitivity to rejection that can turn a principle into a crusade.

Symbols (Unicode where applicable):  
▲ U+25B2 exaltation; ▽ U+25BD detriment; Ψ U+03A8; 2 (edition node symbol); C (stylized Moon, cf. ☽/☾ U+263D/U+263E); ♂ U+2642; ω U+03C9; ⊕ U+2295.

# Gate 50 — The Gate of Values

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 50 header page. Channel 50–27 “Preservation,” Harmonic Gate 27 “Nourishment,” Center Spleen, Circuit Defence (Tribal), astrologic span from late Libra into early Scorpio. It frames Gate 50 as Tribal Law — the values that guard and maintain community, establish the rules of caring, and anchor personal immunity and collective defense; its underlying fear is the fear of responsibility.

Long description:  
Gate 50 is the Gate of Values, the guardian that maintains what Gate 6’s intimacy builds. It’s the Spleen’s awareness possibility for all three streams — Taste, Intuition, Instinct — and a key gate in well-being: the heart of personal immunity and the basis of community defense. In the Tribal process (Defence circuit, our sexual circuit), values are the foundation — they tell us who and what must be nurtured (27) and how the rules of caring are set so offspring grow into adulthood guided by 50’s law. Emerging from our most ancient awareness, this “well of values” is a fundamental ingredient of human survival. Each Spleen gate carries an underlying fear; here it is the fear of responsibility. When 50 is healthy, the Tribe is fed, defended, and ethically held together.

Figures / crosses (bottom captions):

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF LAWS (3)

THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF VALUES

THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF WISHES (2)

## Gate 50 — THE CAULDRON, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 50 “THE CAULDRON” lines page (Spleen • Defence/Tribal). Theme: the value of historical continuity whose traditional values serve and enrich the present and the future. Six lines show how leadership holds to values, why consistency resists casual change, how corruption arises when inferior values dominate, how adaptability seeks support to preserve principles, how determination sustains values under pressure, and how humble origins can refine destiny.

Long description:  
The subtitle reads: “THE VALUE OF HISTORICAL CONTINUITY WHOSE TRADITIONAL VALUES SERVE AND ENRICH THE PRESENT AND THE FUTURE.” Each line renders a practical Tribal law stance: holding harmony in power, conservative steadiness after success, the danger of inferior values, aligning with supportive forces to maintain principles, strength of purpose under conditioning, and refining one’s beginnings rather than rejecting them.

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Libra → Scorpio):  
6 Leadership — 01° 03′ 45″ – 02° 00′ 00″  
5 Consistency — 00° 07′ 30″ – 01° 03′ 45″  
4 Corruption — 29° 11′ 15″ – 00° 07′ 30″  
3 Adaptability — 28° 15′ 00″ – 29° 11′ 15″  
2 Determination — 27° 18′ 45″ – 28° 15′ 00″  
1 The immigrant — 26° 22′ 30″ – 27° 18′ 45″

Leadership  
• ♀ ▲ The gift in a position of power to maintain harmony even in severity. The strength to maintain one’s values with vigour and continue to have harmonic relationships with others.  
• C ▽ An inherent moodiness, that in a position of power may at times alienate or offend and effect overall efficiency. The strength to maintain one’s values but at the expense of harmonic relationships.

Consistency — When continuity has brought success, it should not be tampered with.  
• 2 ▲ The disciplined and natural conservatism to avoid unnecessary change. The conservative awareness that basic principles should not be abandoned casually.  
• ♂ ▽ A perverse reaction to rebel against the methodology of one’s very success. When effectively stimulated, the drive to rebel against basic principles.

Corruption — The lack of enriching values.  
• 2 ▲ The malefic gift of turning inferior values into material success. Given this difficult position, Saturn’s exaltation is, that its actions are limited to selfishness and unsavouriness rather than criminality. The capacity to maintain one’s strength despite inferior values.  
• ♂ ▽ With such energy and no traditional values, the worst can be expected. The potential disregard of values that may lead to corruption or the breakdown of the defense system.

Adaptability  
• C ▲ The Moon exalted, that when unable to make it alone will naturally align itself with nurturing and protective forces. The awareness that to maintain one’s principles and values the support of others is necessary.  
• ☿ ▽ A resentment when its natural mental gifts are ignored and is forced to curry favour in order to survive. Discomfort with the awareness that one cannot stand alone by one’s principles.

Determination  
• ☉ ▲ The strength of purpose that can enjoy overcoming adversity to achieve its goals. The strength derived from maintaining one’s values in the face of opposition or conditioning.  
• ▽ A discomfort with adversity that may lead to determined withdrawal. A lack of strength, where values are threatened by opposition or conditioning.

The immigrant — Humbleness of origin that benefits rather than restricts destiny.  
• ♂ ▲ The desire to be effective and successful that builds on the most fundamental strengths while it refines its nature. The awareness that the growth and refinement of values will benefit destiny.  
• ♀ ▽ A dissatisfaction with/ or embarrassment with origins, that makes an obsession of refinement. A dissatisfaction with original values that demands refinement.

# Gate 51 — The Gate of Shock

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 51 header page. Channel 51–25 “Initiation,” Harmonic Gate 25 “Innocence,” Center Heart, Circuit Centering, astrologic span in Aries. A paragraph describes individual initiative—the power to compete, to leap into the void, to shock—linked to the Warrior/Fool archetype and the “Arousing” that empowers a direction of Love.

Long description:  
Gate 51 is the only Heart gate not in the Ego circuit; it sits in Centering. It embodies individual initiative and the competitive shock that jolts movement. “Walk down the street with a 51, they’re a step ahead”—the Ego drive here can lead to courage or folly, but it always shocks. The trigrams are Thunder over Thunder (Arousing). This is one of the three Mystical Channels; its potential is to leap into the void. It’s the gate of wounding; the Heart must be looked after physically and spiritually. The Warrior and the Fool can do great damage, yet without this drive there is no centering in the evolutionary process. Like Gate 14 empowers direction, Gate 51 Arouses a direction of Love—life’s love and its constant challenge to survival.

Figures (bottom captions):

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF PENETRATION

THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF SHOCK

THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF THE CLARION

Channel: 51–25 — Initiation (A design of needing to be first)

Harmonic Gate: 25 — Innocence

Center: Heart

Circuit: Centering

Astrologic Position: 15° 07′ 30″ ♈ — 20° 45′ 00″ ♈

## Gate 51 — THE AROUSING, Lines 6→1

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 51 “THE AROUSING” (Heart • Centering). Six lines show how the shock archetype responds to disorder through recognition and adaptation: separating from panic, finding symmetry in chaos, working within limitation, improvising adaptation, knowing when to withdraw, and using past crises as reference.

Long description:  
This page outlines the warrior–fool energy of Gate 51 as a disciplined capacity to meet shocks. In crisis, the healthy pattern is to center first (separate), then grasp the nature of the shock to re-pattern (symmetry), acknowledge limits without losing initiative (limitation), improvise (adaptation), choose tactical withdrawal when indicated (withdrawal), and finally learn — turning prior shocks into a living reference (experience).

Transcribed text (exact lines as seen; blue subheads kept; italics preserve emphasis)

Left margin (line names \+ degree ranges, Aries):  
6 Separation — 19° 48′ 45″ – 20° 45′ 00″  
5 Symmetry — 18° 52′ 30″ – 19° 48′ 45″  
4 Limitation — 17° 56′ 15″ – 18° 52′ 30″  
3 Adaptation — 17° 00′ 00″ – 17° 56′ 15″  
2 Withdrawal — 16° 03′ 45″ – 17° 00′ 00″  
1 Reference — 15° 07′ 30″ – 16° 03′ 45″

Separation  
• ☉ ▲ In times of crisis when all those around are confused and in disorder, the ability not to succumb to the panic but to have the will and vitality to survive it alone. The power of the ego to meet challenges alone.  
• ω ▽ Curiously, the same gift, but one by its attitude that invites disapproval that in the extreme may even prevent a successful separation. The egoism to meet the challenge alone that may provoke and empower the challengers.

Symmetry  
• ☉ ▲ Perfected illumination that in grasping the nature of the shock, can transform its normal patterns into a symmetrical adaptation that rides the shock and avoids its devastation. The perfection of the warrior ego through instinctive adaptation.  
• ♂ ▽ A tendency in seeking the core, to harmonize with one shock only to be overwhelmed by the next. The egoism to indulge in victory and lose vigilance.

Limitation  
• (unclear glyph) ▲ A pure inventiveness and sometimes genius to find some opportunity even in the midst of the most devastating shocks. The warrior ego that will find some way to answer the challenge.  
• ☿ ▽ A reasoned make-do mentality that is ineffectual in times of severe shock. The superficial ego that lacks the resources and depth to answer challenges.

Adaptation  
• ☉ ▲ The life-sustaining awareness that thinks on its feet and thus creates opportunities. The power of spontaneity in times of challenge.  
• 2 ▽ The destabilization that occurs when one’s field of normal activity is radically and unpredictably disturbed, where the tendency is withdrawal rather than adaptation. The ego that may be destabilized in times of challenge.

Withdrawal  
• ♂ ▲ The recognition of the mechanics of shock that indicates when withdrawal is the only logical action. The instinctive withdrawal when the power of the ego is threatened.  
• ☿ ▽ Being too smart for one’s own good and rejecting withdrawal in the vain belief that one can outsmart natural forces. The egoism to reject withdrawal and face possible defeat.

Reference — The advantage of previous crisis experience.  
• ω ▲ The gift of re-examination that is the foundation of preparedness. The power of the ego conditioned by experience.  
• 2 ▽ A tendency to emotional withdrawal after a shock. The weakness of the ego in times of challenge.

# Gate 52 — The Gate of Inaction

\[HUMAN-READABLE\]  
Alt text:  
Gate 52 header page. Channel 52–9 “Concentration,” Harmonic Gate 9 “The Taming Power of the Small,” Center Root, Circuit Understanding (Logic), astrologic span in Aquarius. The text frames 52 as stillness: the passive fuel from the Root that focuses and channels energy. Without focus, the pressure becomes restlessness, tension, and depressive tone; with focus, stillness supports withdrawal and productive concentration.

Long description:  
The Understanding Circuit is conditioned by this Format energy — it sets the proper frequency for the whole circuit. Gate 52 is “about stillness,” but specifically the focus and channeling of energy (passive fuel, not active output). All Root format gates can be sources of depressive tone when pressure has no focus; in 52, lack of focus → restlessness → depression. The gate’s mechanic enforces stillness; lack of active outlet looks like anti-social withdrawal, yet concentration benefits from withdrawal. When focused, this pressure becomes the backbone of sustained attention for the Logical process.

Figures / Crosses (bottom of page):

THE RIGHT ANGLE CROSS OF SERVICE (2)

THE JUXTAPOSITION CROSS OF STILLNESS

THE LEFT ANGLE CROSS OF DEMANDS
